FILE: /Ai-Hackathon-project/app/api/news/route.ts
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { fetchAllNews } from '@/lib/rss';
import { NewsTopic } from '@/types/news';

export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic';
export const revalidate = 300; // 5 min cache

export async function GET(request: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const { searchParams } = new URL(request.url);
    const topicsParam = searchParams.get('topics');

    let topics: NewsTopic[] | undefined = undefined;
    if (topicsParam) {
      topics = topicsParam.split(',').map((t) => t.trim() as NewsTopic);
    }

    const articles = await fetchAllNews(topics);

    return NextResponse.json({
      success: true,
      articles,
      total: articles.length,
      fetchedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    });
  } catch (error: any) {
    console.error('API /api/news failed:', error);
    return NextResponse.json(
      {
        success: false,
        error: error?.message || 'Failed to fetch live morning feeds',
        articles: [],
      },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}

import type { Metadata } from 'next';
import './globals.css';

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  title: 'Daymark | Your world, clearly delivered.',
  description: 'A personalized digital morning newspaper inspired by modern editorial broadsheets, curated from premier global news sources.',
  keywords: ['Daymark', 'personalized newspaper', 'morning dispatch', 'news broadsheet', 'NaSCon 26'],
  authors: [{ name: 'Daymark Editorial Board' }],
};

export default function RootLayout({
  children,
}: {
  children: React.ReactNode;
}) {
  return (
    <html lang="en">
      <body className="min-h-screen flex flex-col antialiased bg-ivory-100 text-charcoal-900 selection:bg-forest-100 selection:text-forest-900">
        {children}
      </body>
    </html>
  );
}


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /Ai-Hackathon-project/app/page.tsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
'use client';

import React, { useState, useEffect, useMemo, useCallback } from 'react';
import { Article, UserPreferences, UserFeedback, SavedEdition, NewsTopic, DEFAULT_PREFERENCES, AVAILABLE_TOPICS } from '@/types/news';
import { rankArticles, ScoredArticle } from '@/lib/ranking';
import {
  getStoredPreferences,
  saveStoredPreferences,
  getStoredFeedback,
  saveStoredFeedback,
  getStoredEditions,
  saveCurrentEdition,
  deleteStoredEdition,
  INITIAL_FEEDBACK,
} from '@/lib/storage';
import Masthead from '@/components/Masthead';
import ArticleCard from '@/components/ArticleCard';
import SectionBlock from '@/components/SectionBlock';
import OnboardingModal from '@/components/OnboardingModal';
import WhyThisModal from '@/components/WhyThisModal';
import SavedEditionsDrawer from '@/components/SavedEditionsDrawer';
import PrintPreviewModal from '@/components/PrintPreviewBanner';
import { LoadingNewspaper, EmptyState, ErrorState } from '@/components/StatusStates';
import { Sparkles, Bookmark, Search, RotateCcw, Clock, Quote, Printer, ShieldCheck } from 'lucide-react';

export default function MorningChroniclePage() {
  const [preferences, setPreferences] = useState<UserPreferences>(DEFAULT_PREFERENCES);
  const [feedback, setFeedback] = useState<UserFeedback>(INITIAL_FEEDBACK);
  const [savedEditions, setSavedEditions] = useState<SavedEdition[]>([]);
  const [rawArticles, setRawArticles] = useState<Article[]>([]);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [isRefreshing, setIsRefreshing] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Modals & Navigation state
  const [isOnboardingOpen, setIsOnboardingOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isEditionsOpen, setIsEditionsOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isPrintModalOpen, setIsPrintModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [whyThisArticle, setWhyThisArticle] = useState<ScoredArticle | null>(null);
  const [selectedTopicFilter, setSelectedTopicFilter] = useState<NewsTopic | 'all'>('all');
  const [activeEdition, setActiveEdition] = useState<SavedEdition | null>(null);
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [toastMessage, setToastMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [showBookmarksOnly, setShowBookmarksOnly] = useState(false);
  const [isPlayingAudio, setIsPlayingAudio] = useState(false);

  const showToast = (msg: string) => {
    setToastMessage(msg);
    setTimeout(() => {
      setToastMessage((curr) => (curr === msg ? null : curr));
    }, 3200);
  };

  // Load persisted user state on mount
  useEffect(() => {
    const loadedPrefs = getStoredPreferences();
    const loadedFeedback = getStoredFeedback();
    const loadedEditions = getStoredEditions();

    setPreferences(loadedPrefs);
    setFeedback(loadedFeedback);
    setSavedEditions(loadedEditions);

    // If user has never onboarded, show onboarding prompt
    if (!loadedPrefs.onboarded) {
      setIsOnboardingOpen(true);
    }
  }, []);

  // Fetch live articles from API
  const fetchNews = useCallback(async (topicsToFetch?: NewsTopic[]) => {
    try {
      setError(null);
      const url = topicsToFetch && topicsToFetch.length > 0
        ? `/api/news?topics=${topicsToFetch.join(',')}`
        : '/api/news';

      const res = await fetch(url);
      if (!res.ok) {
        throw new Error(`Failed to load wire feed (status ${res.status})`);
      }
      const data = await res.json();
      if (data.success && Array.isArray(data.articles)) {
        setRawArticles(data.articles);
      } else {
        throw new Error(data.error || 'Invalid wire response');
      }
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Fetch news error:', err);
      setError(err?.message || 'Could not connect to live wire');
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
      setIsRefreshing(false);
    }
  }, []);

  // Initial fetch
  useEffect(() => {
    fetchNews(preferences.selectedTopics);
  }, [fetchNews, preferences.selectedTopics]);

  // Handle refresh
  const handleRefresh = async () => {
    setIsRefreshing(true);
    await fetchNews(preferences.selectedTopics);
    showToast('Live wire updated with latest reports');
  };

  // Ranking calculation
  const rankedArticles: ScoredArticle[] = useMemo(() => {
    // If viewing an archived edition snapshot
    const sourceList = activeEdition ? activeEdition.articles : rawArticles;
    const ranked = rankArticles(sourceList, preferences, feedback, false);

    // Filter by topic if active
    let filtered = ranked;
    if (selectedTopicFilter !== 'all') {
      filtered = filtered.filter((a) => a.category === selectedTopicFilter);
    }

    // Filter by search query
    if (searchQuery.trim()) {
      const q = searchQuery.toLowerCase();
      filtered = filtered.filter(
        (a) => a.title.toLowerCase().includes(q) || a.description.toLowerCase().includes(q)
      );
    }

    // Filter by bookmarks only
    if (showBookmarksOnly) {
      filtered = filtered.filter((a) => feedback.savedArticleIds.includes(a.id));
    }

    return filtered;
  }, [activeEdition, rawArticles, preferences, feedback, selectedTopicFilter, searchQuery, showBookmarksOnly]);

  // Lead, Secondary, and Wire splits
  const leadArticle = rankedArticles[0] || null;
  const secondaryArticles = rankedArticles.slice(1, 5);
  const wireBriefs = rankedArticles.slice(5, 12);
  const sectionRemaining = rankedArticles.slice(12);

  // Group remaining into categorical sections
  const techArticles = useMemo(
    () => rankedArticles.filter((a) => a.category === 'technology' && a.id !== leadArticle?.id),
    [rankedArticles, leadArticle]
  );
  const worldArticles = useMemo(
    () => rankedArticles.filter((a) => a.category === 'world' && a.id !== leadArticle?.id),
    [rankedArticles, leadArticle]
  );
  const businessArticles = useMemo(
    () => rankedArticles.filter((a) => a.category === 'business' && a.id !== leadArticle?.id),
    [rankedArticles, leadArticle]
  );
  const scienceArticles = useMemo(
    () => rankedArticles.filter((a) => a.category === 'science' && a.id !== leadArticle?.id),
    [rankedArticles, leadArticle]
  );
  const otherArticles = useMemo(
    () => rankedArticles.filter((a) => !['technology', 'world', 'business', 'science'].includes(a.category) && a.id !== leadArticle?.id),
    [rankedArticles, leadArticle]
  );

  // Feedback Handlers
  const handleLike = (article: ScoredArticle) => {
    const isLiked = feedback.likedArticleIds.includes(article.id);
    const newLiked = isLiked
      ? feedback.likedArticleIds.filter((id) => id !== article.id)
      : [...feedback.likedArticleIds, article.id];

    // Remove from disliked if present
    const newDisliked = feedback.dislikedArticleIds.filter((id) => id !== article.id);

    // Boost topic affinity
    const currentAffinity = feedback.topicAffinities[article.category] || 0;
    const newTopicAffinities = {
      ...feedback.topicAffinities,
      [article.category]: Math.min(currentAffinity + (isLiked ? -1 : 1.5), 5),
    };

    const updatedFeedback: UserFeedback = {
      ...feedback,
      likedArticleIds: newLiked,
      dislikedArticleIds: newDisliked,
      topicAffinities: newTopicAffinities,
    };

    setFeedback(updatedFeedback);
    saveStoredFeedback(updatedFeedback);
    showToast(isLiked ? 'Removed vote' : `Rank boosted for ${article.category} stories`);
  };

  const handleDislike = (article: ScoredArticle) => {
    const isDisliked = feedback.dislikedArticleIds.includes(article.id);
    const newDisliked = isDisliked
      ? feedback.dislikedArticleIds.filter((id) => id !== article.id)
      : [...feedback.dislikedArticleIds, article.id];

    // Remove from liked if present
    const newLiked = feedback.likedArticleIds.filter((id) => id !== article.id);

    // Decrease topic affinity
    const currentAffinity = feedback.topicAffinities[article.category] || 0;
    const newTopicAffinities = {
      ...feedback.topicAffinities,
      [article.category]: Math.max(currentAffinity + (isDisliked ? 1 : -2), -5),
    };

    const updatedFeedback: UserFeedback = {
      ...feedback,
      likedArticleIds: newLiked,
      dislikedArticleIds: newDisliked,
      topicAffinities: newTopicAffinities,
    };

    setFeedback(updatedFeedback);
    saveStoredFeedback(updatedFeedback);
    showToast(isDisliked ? 'Removed downvote' : `Reduced frequency of ${article.category} stories`);
  };

  const handleSkip = (article: ScoredArticle) => {
    const updatedFeedback: UserFeedback = {
      ...feedback,
      skippedArticleIds: [...feedback.skippedArticleIds, article.id],
    };
    setFeedback(updatedFeedback);
    saveStoredFeedback(updatedFeedback);
    showToast(`Story dismissed from today's paper`);
  };

  const handleBookmark = (article: ScoredArticle) => {
    const isSaved = feedback.savedArticleIds.includes(article.id);
    const newSaved = isSaved
      ? feedback.savedArticleIds.filter((id) => id !== article.id)
      : [...feedback.savedArticleIds, article.id];

    const updatedFeedback: UserFeedback = {
      ...feedback,
      savedArticleIds: newSaved,
    };
    setFeedback(updatedFeedback);
    saveStoredFeedback(updatedFeedback);
    showToast(isSaved ? 'Removed from saved clippings' : 'Saved to personal morning clippings');
  };

  // Preference update
  const handleSavePreferences = (newPrefs: UserPreferences) => {
    setPreferences(newPrefs);
    saveStoredPreferences(newPrefs);
    fetchNews(newPrefs.selectedTopics);
    showToast('Preferences updated and broadsheet re-ranked');
  };

  // Save current edition to archive
  const handleSaveTodayEdition = () => {
    if (rankedArticles.length === 0) {
      showToast('No articles available to archive yet');
      return;
    }
    const todayStr = new Date().toLocaleDateString('en-US', {
      weekday: 'short',
      month: 'short',
      day: 'numeric',
      year: 'numeric',
    });

    const newEdition: SavedEdition = {
      id: 'edition_' + Date.now().toString(36),
      dateStr: todayStr,
      savedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      title: preferences.newspaperTitle,
      leadHeadline: leadArticle ? leadArticle.title : 'Morning Dispatch',
      articlesCount: rankedArticles.length,
      articles: rankedArticles,
      preferencesSnapshot: preferences,
    };

    saveCurrentEdition(newEdition);
    setSavedEditions(getStoredEditions());
    showToast(`Saved today's edition (${newEdition.articlesCount} stories) to Archive!`);
  };

  const handleDeleteEdition = (id: string) => {
    deleteStoredEdition(id);
    setSavedEditions(getStoredEditions());
    if (activeEdition?.id === id) {
      setActiveEdition(null);
    }
    showToast('Archived edition deleted');
  };

  const handleSelectEdition = (edition: SavedEdition) => {
    setActiveEdition(edition);
    showToast(`Loaded archived edition from ${edition.dateStr}`);
  };

  const handleResetToLive = () => {
    setActiveEdition(null);
    showToast('Returned to Live Wire edition');
  };

  // Clean up audio speech on unmount
  useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && 'speechSynthesis' in window) {
        window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
      }
    };
  }, []);

  const handleToggleAudio = () => {
    if (typeof window === 'undefined' || !('speechSynthesis' in window)) {
      showToast('Audio dispatch is not supported in this browser.');
      return;
    }

    if (isPlayingAudio) {
      window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
      setIsPlayingAudio(false);
      showToast('Audio dispatch stopped.');
      return;
    }

    if (rankedArticles.length === 0) {
      showToast('No articles available for audio briefing.');
      return;
    }

    window.speechSynthesis.cancel();

    const topStories = rankedArticles.slice(0, 3);
    const script = [
      `Good morning, ${preferences.readerName || 'Reader'}. Welcome to your personalized edition of ${preferences.newspaperTitle || 'The Morning Chronicle'}.`,
      `Here are your top reports for today.`,
      ...topStories.map((art, idx) => `Report ${idx + 1}, from ${art.source.name}. ${art.title}. ${art.description}`),
      `This concludes your morning audio dispatch. Have a productive day.`
    ].join(' ');

    const utterance = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(script);
    utterance.rate = 1.0;
    utterance.onend = () => setIsPlayingAudio(false);
    utterance.onerror = () => setIsPlayingAudio(false);

    setIsPlayingAudio(true);
    showToast('Playing Morning Audio Briefing...');
    window.speechSynthesis.speak(utterance);
  };

  // Print execution
  const executePrint = () => {
    window.print();
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-ivory-100 text-charcoal-900 flex flex-col font-serif selection:bg-forest-100 selection:text-forest-900">
      {/* Toast Notification */}
      {toastMessage && (
        <div className="fixed bottom-6 right-6 z-50 bg-charcoal-900 text-ivory-50 text-xs font-sans px-4 py-2.5 rounded shadow-xl flex items-center space-x-2 animate-in fade-in slide-in-from-bottom-2 no-print border border-charcoal-700">
          <Sparkles className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-forest-300" />
          <span>{toastMessage}</span>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Masthead */}
      <Masthead
        preferences={preferences}
        onOpenPreferences={() => setIsOnboardingOpen(true)}
        onOpenEditions={() => setIsEditionsOpen(true)}
        onPrint={() => setIsPrintModalOpen(true)}
        onRefresh={handleRefresh}
        isRefreshing={isRefreshing}
        selectedTopicFilter={selectedTopicFilter}
        onSelectTopicFilter={setSelectedTopicFilter}
        onSaveCurrentEdition={handleSaveTodayEdition}
        savedEditionsCount={savedEditions.length}
        isPlayingAudio={isPlayingAudio}
        onToggleAudio={handleToggleAudio}
      />

      {/* Secondary Search & Filter Bar (Screen only) */}
      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 w-full py-2.5 no-print">
        <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-3 bg-ivory-200/50 p-2.5 rounded border border-ivory-300">
          {/* Search Box */}
          <div className="relative w-full sm:w-80">
            <Search className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-charcoal-400 absolute left-3 top-2.5" />
            <input
              id="search-articles-input"
              type="text"
              value={searchQuery}
              onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Filter stories by subject or keyword..."
              className="w-full pl-8 pr-3 py-1.5 bg-ivory-50 border border-ivory-300 rounded text-xs font-sans text-charcoal-900 placeholder-charcoal-400 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-forest-600"
            />
            {searchQuery && (
              <button
                onClick={() => setSearchQuery('')}
                className="absolute right-2.5 top-2 text-charcoal-400 hover:text-charcoal-900 text-xs"
              >
                ×
              </button>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Quick status / Filter toggles */}
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-3 text-xs font-sans">
            <button
              onClick={() => setShowBookmarksOnly(!showBookmarksOnly)}
              className={`flex items-center space-x-1.5 px-3 py-1 rounded border transition-colors ${
                showBookmarksOnly
                  ? 'bg-forest-50 border-forest-300 text-forest-800 font-semibold'
                  : 'bg-ivory-50 border-ivory-300 text-charcoal-700 hover:bg-ivory-200'
              }`}
            >
              <Bookmark className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-forest-600" />
              <span>Saved Clippings ({feedback.savedArticleIds.length})</span>
            </button>

            {activeEdition && (
              <button
                onClick={handleResetToLive}
                className="flex items-center space-x-1 px-2.5 py-1 bg-forest-50 text-forest-700 border border-forest-200 rounded hover:bg-forest-100 font-medium"
              >
                <RotateCcw className="w-3 h-3" />
                <span>Exit Archive</span>
              </button>
            )}

            <span className="text-charcoal-500 hidden md:inline">
              Showing <strong>{rankedArticles.length}</strong> curated reports
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Main Content Broadsheet */}
      <main className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 w-full flex-1 py-4 print-broadsheet">
        {isLoading ? (
          <LoadingNewspaper />
        ) : error ? (
          <ErrorState error={error} onRetry={() => fetchNews(preferences.selectedTopics)} />
        ) : rankedArticles.length === 0 ? (
          <EmptyState
            onResetFilters={() => {
              setSelectedTopicFilter('all');
              setSearchQuery('');
              setShowBookmarksOnly(false);
            }}
            onOpenPreferences={() => setIsOnboardingOpen(true)}
          />
        ) : (
          <div>
            {/* FRONT PAGE BROADSHEET LAYOUT */}
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-8 pb-8 border-b border-ivory-300">
              
              {/* PRIMARY BROADSHEET COLUMN (Cols 1-8) */}
              <div className="lg:col-span-8 lg:border-r lg:border-ivory-300 lg:pr-8">
                {/* 1. Lead Story */}
                {leadArticle && (
                  <ArticleCard
                    article={leadArticle}
                    variant="hero"
                    onLike={handleLike}
                    onDislike={handleDislike}
                    onSkip={handleSkip}
                    onBookmark={handleBookmark}
                    onWhyThis={(a) => setWhyThisArticle(a)}
                    isLiked={feedback.likedArticleIds.includes(leadArticle.id)}
                    isDisliked={feedback.dislikedArticleIds.includes(leadArticle.id)}
                    isBookmarked={feedback.savedArticleIds.includes(leadArticle.id)}
                  />
                )}

                {/* 2. Secondary Stories (2-column layout above the fold) */}
                {secondaryArticles.length > 0 && (
                  <div>
                    <div className="flex items-center space-x-2 mb-4">
                      <span className="font-sans font-bold text-[10px] uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-forest-700">
                        Front Page Reports
                      </span>
                      <div className="flex-1 h-px bg-ivory-300"></div>
                    </div>
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-5">
                      {secondaryArticles.map((article) => (
                        <ArticleCard
                          key={article.id}
                          article={article}
                          variant="standard"
                          onLike={handleLike}
                          onDislike={handleDislike}
                          onSkip={handleSkip}
                          onBookmark={handleBookmark}
                          onWhyThis={(a) => setWhyThisArticle(a)}
                          isLiked={feedback.likedArticleIds.includes(article.id)}
                          isDisliked={feedback.dislikedArticleIds.includes(article.id)}
                          isBookmarked={feedback.savedArticleIds.includes(article.id)}
                        />
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>

              {/* EDITORIAL SIDEBAR: THE WIRE & FAST DIGEST (Cols 9-12) */}
              <div className="lg:col-span-4 flex flex-col justify-between space-y-6">
                <div>
                  {/* The Wire Header */}
                  <div className="p-3.5 bg-ivory-200/60 border border-ivory-300 rounded mb-4">
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                      <h3 className="font-headline font-bold text-sm tracking-wider uppercase text-charcoal-900">
                        The Morning Wire
                      </h3>
                      <span className="text-[10px] font-sans font-semibold bg-forest-600 text-white px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded">
                        FAST DISPATCH
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    <p className="text-[11px] font-serif italic text-charcoal-600 mt-1">
                      High-priority 2-minute developments filtered for your reading pace.
                    </p>
                  </div>

                  {/* Wire Items */}
                  <div className="space-y-1">
                    {wireBriefs.map((article) => (
                      <ArticleCard
                        key={article.id}
                        article={article}
                        variant="wire"
                        onLike={handleLike}
                        onDislike={handleDislike}
                        onSkip={handleSkip}
                        onBookmark={handleBookmark}
                        onWhyThis={(a) => setWhyThisArticle(a)}
                        isLiked={feedback.likedArticleIds.includes(article.id)}
                        isDisliked={feedback.dislikedArticleIds.includes(article.id)}
                        isBookmarked={feedback.savedArticleIds.includes(article.id)}
                      />
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* Editorial Reflection Box (Inspired by Reference Image 2: REFLECTA) */}
                <div className="p-5 bg-ivory-50 border border-ivory-300 rounded-lg font-serif text-charcoal-700 border-l-4 border-l-forest-600 shadow-2xs">
                  <div className="flex items-center space-x-1.5 font-sans font-semibold uppercase text-[10px] tracking-wider text-forest-700 mb-2">
                    <Quote className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                    <span>Daily Reflection</span>
                  </div>
                  <p className="text-sm sm:text-base italic leading-relaxed text-charcoal-800">
                    &ldquo;Knowledge becomes wisdom when connected with curiosity.&rdquo;
                  </p>
                  <div className="mt-3 pt-2.5 border-t border-ivory-200 flex items-center justify-between text-[11px] font-sans text-charcoal-500">
                    <span>Daymark Editorial Desk</span>
                    <span className="font-mono text-[10px]">Issue No. 42</span>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

            </div>

            {/* CATEGORICAL SECTIONS BELOW THE FOLD */}
            {selectedTopicFilter === 'all' && (
              <div className="mt-8 space-y-10">
                {/* Tech & AI Section */}
                <SectionBlock
                  title="Technology, Computation & Artificial Intelligence"
                  sectionCode="SECTION B"
                  articles={techArticles.slice(0, 3)}
                  onLike={handleLike}
                  onDislike={handleDislike}
                  onSkip={handleSkip}
                  onBookmark={handleBookmark}
                  onWhyThis={(a) => setWhyThisArticle(a)}
                  likedIds={feedback.likedArticleIds}
                  dislikedIds={feedback.dislikedArticleIds}
                  bookmarkedIds={feedback.savedArticleIds}
                />

                {/* World Affairs Section */}
                <SectionBlock
                  title="Global Geopolitics & World Affairs"
                  sectionCode="SECTION C"
                  articles={worldArticles.slice(0, 3)}
                  onLike={handleLike}
                  onDislike={handleDislike}
                  onSkip={handleSkip}
                  onBookmark={handleBookmark}
                  onWhyThis={(a) => setWhyThisArticle(a)}
                  likedIds={feedback.likedArticleIds}
                  dislikedIds={feedback.dislikedArticleIds}
                  bookmarkedIds={feedback.savedArticleIds}
                />

                {/* Business & Economy Section */}
                <SectionBlock
                  title="Markets, Capital & Global Commerce"
                  sectionCode="SECTION D"
                  articles={businessArticles.slice(0, 3)}
                  onLike={handleLike}
                  onDislike={handleDislike}
                  onSkip={handleSkip}
                  onBookmark={handleBookmark}
                  onWhyThis={(a) => setWhyThisArticle(a)}
                  likedIds={feedback.likedArticleIds}
                  dislikedIds={feedback.dislikedArticleIds}
                  bookmarkedIds={feedback.savedArticleIds}
                />

                {/* Science & Nature Section */}
                <SectionBlock
                  title="Science, Cosmos & Earth Systems"
                  sectionCode="SECTION E"
                  articles={scienceArticles.slice(0, 3)}
                  onLike={handleLike}
                  onDislike={handleDislike}
                  onSkip={handleSkip}
                  onBookmark={handleBookmark}
                  onWhyThis={(a) => setWhyThisArticle(a)}
                  likedIds={feedback.likedArticleIds}
                  dislikedIds={feedback.dislikedArticleIds}
                  bookmarkedIds={feedback.savedArticleIds}
                />

                {/* Culture & Opinion Section */}
                {otherArticles.length > 0 && (
                  <SectionBlock
                    title="Culture, Medicine & Editorial Commentary"
                    sectionCode="SECTION F"
                    articles={otherArticles.slice(0, 3)}
                    onLike={handleLike}
                    onDislike={handleDislike}
                    onSkip={handleSkip}
                    onBookmark={handleBookmark}
                    onWhyThis={(a) => setWhyThisArticle(a)}
                    likedIds={feedback.likedArticleIds}
                    dislikedIds={feedback.dislikedArticleIds}
                    bookmarkedIds={feedback.savedArticleIds}
                  />
                )}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        )}
      </main>

      {/* DAYMARK COLOPHON & FOOTER */}
      <footer className="w-full bg-ivory-200/50 border-t border-ivory-300 py-10 mt-16 print-header">
        <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 text-center">
          <h4 className="font-headline text-2xl font-black text-charcoal-900 tracking-tight">
            {preferences.newspaperTitle || 'DAYMARK'}
          </h4>
          <p className="text-xs font-serif italic text-charcoal-600 mt-1 max-w-lg mx-auto leading-relaxed">
            Your world, clearly delivered. Personalized daily for {preferences.readerName || 'our readers'} • Sourced from BBC News, The New York Times, Ars Technica, and NPR.
          </p>

          <div className="mt-4 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-center gap-4 text-[11px] font-sans text-charcoal-500 uppercase tracking-wider">
            <span className="flex items-center text-forest-700 font-medium">
              <ShieldCheck className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-forest-600 mr-1" />
              NaSCon &apos;26 Official Entry
            </span>
            <span>•</span>
            <span>Zero Hallucination Policy</span>
            <span>•</span>
            <span>Deterministic Scoring Algorithm</span>
            <span>•</span>
            <span>100% Client-Side Storage</span>
          </div>

          <div className="mt-4 pt-4 border-t border-ivory-300 text-[10px] text-charcoal-400 font-sans no-print">
            Click &ldquo;Print / PDF&rdquo; to download an archival broadsheet or save as PDF.
          </div>
        </div>
      </footer>

      {/* Onboarding & Preferences Modal */}
      <OnboardingModal
        isOpen={isOnboardingOpen}
        onClose={() => setIsOnboardingOpen(false)}
        preferences={preferences}
        onSave={handleSavePreferences}
        isFirstVisit={!preferences.onboarded}
      />

      {/* Why This Story Modal (Explainability) */}
      <WhyThisModal
        article={whyThisArticle}
        onClose={() => setWhyThisArticle(null)}
        preferences={preferences}
      />

      {/* Saved Editions Archive Drawer */}
      <SavedEditionsDrawer
        isOpen={isEditionsOpen}
        onClose={() => setIsEditionsOpen(false)}
        editions={savedEditions}
        onSelectEdition={handleSelectEdition}
        onDeleteEdition={handleDeleteEdition}
        onSaveCurrent={handleSaveTodayEdition}
        activeEditionId={activeEdition?.id}
        onResetToLive={handleResetToLive}
      />

      {/* Print / Save PDF Modal */}
      <PrintPreviewModal
        isOpen={isPrintModalOpen}
        onClose={() => setIsPrintModalOpen(false)}
        onConfirmPrint={executePrint}
        newspaperTitle={preferences.newspaperTitle}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

'use client';

import React from 'react';
import { ThumbsUp, ThumbsDown, X, Bookmark, ExternalLink, Sparkles, Clock, ArrowRight } from 'lucide-react';
import { ScoredArticle } from '@/lib/ranking';

interface ArticleCardProps {
  article: ScoredArticle;
  variant?: 'hero' | 'standard' | 'wire';
  onLike: (article: ScoredArticle) => void;
  onDislike: (article: ScoredArticle) => void;
  onSkip: (article: ScoredArticle) => void;
  onBookmark: (article: ScoredArticle) => void;
  onWhyThis: (article: ScoredArticle) => void;
  isLiked?: boolean;
  isDisliked?: boolean;
  isBookmarked?: boolean;
}

export default function ArticleCard({
  article,
  variant = 'standard',
  onLike,
  onDislike,
  onSkip,
  onBookmark,
  onWhyThis,
  isLiked = false,
  isDisliked = false,
  isBookmarked = false,
}: ArticleCardProps) {
  const pubDate = new Date(article.publishedAt);
  const formattedDate = pubDate.toLocaleDateString('en-US', {
    month: 'short',
    day: 'numeric',
    year: 'numeric',
  });
  const formattedTime = pubDate.toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' });

  // 1. HERO LEAD STORY VARIANT (Inspired by TIDE cover story)
  if (variant === 'hero') {
    return (
      <article className="pb-8 mb-8 border-b border-ivory-300 print-break-inside-avoid">
        {/* Cover Story Header Tagline */}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-sans mb-3 flex-wrap gap-2">
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
            <span className="text-[11px] font-sans font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-forest-600">
              Cover Story
            </span>
            <span className="text-ivory-400">•</span>
            <span className="text-[11px] font-sans uppercase tracking-wider text-charcoal-600">
              {article.category}
            </span>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
            <button
              onClick={() => onWhyThis(article)}
              className="flex items-center space-x-1 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-forest-50 hover:bg-forest-100 text-forest-700 text-[11px] font-sans font-medium border border-forest-200 transition-colors no-print cursor-pointer"
              title="Click to view why this was ranked for you"
            >
              <Sparkles className="w-3 h-3 text-forest-600" />
              <span>{article.relevanceScore}% Match</span>
            </button>
            <span className="text-ivory-400 no-print">•</span>
            <span className="flex items-center text-charcoal-500 text-[11px] font-sans">
              <Clock className="w-3 h-3 mr-1" />
              {article.readTimeMinutes} min read
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Responsive Grid: Photo + Content */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-6 lg:gap-8 items-start">
          {/* Hero Image */}
          {article.imageUrl ? (
            <div className="lg:col-span-6 w-full overflow-hidden rounded bg-ivory-200 border border-ivory-300 shadow-2xs">
              <img
                src={article.imageUrl}
                alt={article.title}
                className="w-full h-[280px] sm:h-[360px] lg:h-[420px] object-cover hover:scale-[1.01] transition-transform duration-500"
                loading="eager"
                onError={(e) => {
                  (e.target as HTMLElement).style.display = 'none';
                }}
              />
              <div className="py-1.5 px-3 text-[10px] text-charcoal-500 font-sans italic bg-ivory-50 border-t border-ivory-200 flex justify-between">
                <span>Via {article.source.name}</span>
                <span>{formattedDate}</span>
              </div>
            </div>
          ) : null}

          {/* Headline & Abstract Column */}
          <div className={article.imageUrl ? 'lg:col-span-6' : 'lg:col-span-12'}>
            <h2 className="font-headline text-3xl sm:text-5xl lg:text-5xl xl:text-6xl font-normal sm:font-medium italic text-charcoal-900 leading-[1.08] tracking-tight mb-4 hover:text-forest-700 transition-colors">
              <a href={article.url} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">
                {article.title}
              </a>
            </h2>

            {/* Byline & Source info */}
            <div className="text-xs font-sans text-charcoal-500 mb-4 flex items-center flex-wrap gap-2">
              <span className="font-medium text-charcoal-800">Reporting by {article.source.name}</span>
              {article.author && (
                <>
                  <span className="text-ivory-400">•</span>
                  <span>By {article.author}</span>
                </>
              )}
              <span className="text-ivory-400">•</span>
              <span>{formattedDate}</span>
            </div>

            {/* Narrative text with dropcap */}
            <p className="text-base sm:text-lg font-serif text-charcoal-700 leading-relaxed editorial-dropcap mb-6">
              {article.description}
            </p>

            {/* Bottom Actions Row */}
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between pt-4 border-t border-ivory-200 text-xs font-sans flex-wrap gap-3">
              <a
                href={article.url}
                target="_blank"
                rel="noopener noreferrer"
                className="inline-flex items-center space-x-1.5 text-forest-700 hover:text-forest-900 font-semibold font-sans group transition-colors"
              >
                <span>Read the full feature</span>
                <ArrowRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5 group-hover:translate-x-1 transition-transform" />
              </a>

              {/* Feedback controls */}
              <div className="flex items-center space-x-1 no-print">
                <button
                  onClick={() => onLike(article)}
                  className={`p-1.5 rounded transition-colors ${
                    isLiked
                      ? 'bg-forest-100 text-forest-800 font-bold'
                      : 'hover:bg-ivory-200 text-charcoal-500'
                  }`}
                  title="More stories like this"
                >
                  <ThumbsUp className="w-4 h-4" />
                </button>
                <button
                  onClick={() => onDislike(article)}
                  className={`p-1.5 rounded transition-colors ${
                    isDisliked
                      ? 'bg-terracotta-100 text-terracotta-600 font-bold'
                      : 'hover:bg-ivory-200 text-charcoal-500'
                  }`}
                  title="Less stories like this"
                >
                  <ThumbsDown className="w-4 h-4" />
                </button>
                <button
                  onClick={() => onBookmark(article)}
                  className={`p-1.5 rounded transition-colors ${
                    isBookmarked
                      ? 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800'
                      : 'hover:bg-ivory-200 text-charcoal-500'
                  }`}
                  title={isBookmarked ? 'Saved in clippings' : 'Save story'}
                >
                  <Bookmark className="w-4 h-4" />
                </button>
                <button
                  onClick={() => onSkip(article)}
                  className="p-1.5 rounded hover:bg-ivory-200 text-charcoal-400 hover:text-charcoal-800 transition-colors"
                  title="Dismiss story from today's paper"
                >
                  <X className="w-4 h-4" />
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </article>
    );
  }

  // 2. WIRE / SIDEBAR COMPACT DISPATCH VARIANT
  if (variant === 'wire') {
    return (
      <div className="py-3 border-b border-ivory-200/80 last:border-b-0 print-break-inside-avoid group">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-[11px] font-sans text-charcoal-500 mb-1">
          <span className="font-semibold text-forest-700 uppercase tracking-wider text-[10px]">
            {article.source.name}
          </span>
          <span className="font-mono text-charcoal-400 text-[10px]">{formattedTime}</span>
        </div>
        <h4 className="text-sm font-serif font-bold text-charcoal-900 leading-snug group-hover:text-forest-700 transition-colors">
          <a href={article.url} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">
            {article.title}
          </a>
        </h4>
        <p className="text-xs font-serif text-charcoal-600 line-clamp-2 mt-1 leading-normal">
          {article.description}
        </p>
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mt-2 pt-1 text-[11px] font-sans text-charcoal-500">
          <button
            onClick={() => onWhyThis(article)}
            className="flex items-center space-x-1 text-forest-600 hover:underline no-print text-[10px]"
          >
            <Sparkles className="w-2.5 h-2.5" />
            <span>{article.relevanceScore}% match</span>
          </button>
          <a
            href={article.url}
            target="_blank"
            rel="noopener noreferrer"
            className="inline-flex items-center space-x-0.5 text-charcoal-700 hover:text-charcoal-900 font-medium text-[11px]"
          >
            <span>Read</span>
            <ExternalLink className="w-2.5 h-2.5 ml-0.5" />
          </a>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  // 3. STANDARD EDITORIAL GRID STORY
  return (
    <article className="border border-ivory-300 bg-ivory-50/60 hover:bg-white rounded p-4 sm:p-5 flex flex-col justify-between transition-all duration-200 hover:shadow-xs hover:border-ivory-400 print-break-inside-avoid group">
      <div>
        {/* Source & Category Header */}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-sans text-charcoal-500 mb-2.5">
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-1.5">
            <span className="font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-[10px] text-forest-600">
              {article.category}
            </span>
            <span>•</span>
            <span className="font-medium text-charcoal-700">{article.source.name}</span>
          </div>
          <button
            onClick={() => onWhyThis(article)}
            className="flex items-center space-x-1 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-ivory-200/80 hover:bg-ivory-300 text-charcoal-800 text-[10px] font-sans font-medium transition-colors no-print"
            title="Click to view why this was ranked for you"
          >
            <Sparkles className="w-2.5 h-2.5 text-forest-600" />
            <span>{article.relevanceScore}%</span>
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Thumbnail if present */}
        {article.imageUrl && (
          <div className="mb-3 overflow-hidden rounded bg-ivory-200 border border-ivory-300">
            <img
              src={article.imageUrl}
              alt={article.title}
              className="w-full h-40 object-cover group-hover:scale-[1.02] transition-transform duration-300"
              loading="lazy"
              onError={(e) => {
                (e.target as HTMLElement).style.display = 'none';
              }}
            />
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Headline */}
        <h3 className="text-lg sm:text-xl font-headline font-semibold text-charcoal-900 leading-snug mb-2 group-hover:text-forest-700 transition-colors">
          <a href={article.url} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">
            {article.title}
          </a>
        </h3>

        {/* Abstract */}
        <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-serif text-charcoal-600 line-clamp-3 leading-relaxed mb-4">
          {article.description}
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* Footer & Feedback Actions */}
      <div className="pt-3 border-t border-ivory-200 flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-sans">
        <div className="flex items-center space-x-2 text-[11px] text-charcoal-500">
          <span>{formattedDate}</span>
          <span>•</span>
          <span>{article.readTimeMinutes}m read</span>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center space-x-1 no-print">
          <button
            onClick={() => onLike(article)}
            className={`p-1 rounded hover:bg-ivory-200 transition-colors ${
              isLiked ? 'text-forest-700 font-bold' : 'text-charcoal-400 hover:text-charcoal-700'
            }`}
            title="More stories like this"
          >
            <ThumbsUp className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => onDislike(article)}
            className={`p-1 rounded hover:bg-ivory-200 transition-colors ${
              isDisliked ? 'text-terracotta-600 font-bold' : 'text-charcoal-400 hover:text-charcoal-700'
            }`}
            title="Less stories like this"
          >
            <ThumbsDown className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => onBookmark(article)}
            className={`p-1 rounded hover:bg-ivory-200 transition-colors ${
              isBookmarked ? 'text-amber-700' : 'text-charcoal-400 hover:text-charcoal-700'
            }`}
            title="Save article"
          >
            <Bookmark className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => onSkip(article)}

'use client';

import React from 'react';
import { SlidersHorizontal, Printer, Archive, RotateCw, Volume2, VolumeX, Sparkles, Tag, ArrowUpRight } from 'lucide-react';
import { UserPreferences, NewsTopic, AVAILABLE_TOPICS } from '@/types/news';

interface MastheadProps {
  preferences: UserPreferences;
  onOpenPreferences: () => void;
  onOpenEditions: () => void;
  onPrint: () => void;
  onRefresh: () => void;
  isRefreshing: boolean;
  selectedTopicFilter: NewsTopic | 'all';
  onSelectTopicFilter: (topic: NewsTopic | 'all') => void;
  onSaveCurrentEdition: () => void;
  savedEditionsCount: number;
  isPlayingAudio?: boolean;
  onToggleAudio?: () => void;
}

export default function Masthead({
  preferences,
  onOpenPreferences,
  onOpenEditions,
  onPrint,
  onRefresh,
  isRefreshing,
  selectedTopicFilter,
  onSelectTopicFilter,
  onSaveCurrentEdition,
  savedEditionsCount,
  isPlayingAudio = false,
  onToggleAudio,
}: MastheadProps) {
  const today = new Date();
  const dateFormatted = today.toLocaleDateString('en-US', {
    weekday: 'long',
    month: 'long',
    day: 'numeric',
    year: 'numeric',
  });
  const monthYearFormatted = today.toLocaleDateString('en-US', {
    month: 'short',
    year: 'numeric',
  }).toUpperCase();

  const titleText = preferences.newspaperTitle || 'DAYMARK';

  return (
    <header className="w-full bg-ivory-100 border-b border-ivory-300">
      {/* 1. TOP UTILITY STRIP */}
      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 py-2.5 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between text-[11px] font-sans tracking-wider text-charcoal-600 border-b border-ivory-200">
        {/* Left: Volume & Issue Info */}
        <div className="flex items-center space-x-3">
          <span className="font-semibold text-charcoal-900 tracking-widest">
            VOL. XII / NO. 42
          </span>
          <span className="text-ivory-400">•</span>
          <span className="uppercase text-charcoal-600">
            {monthYearFormatted}
          </span>
          <span className="hidden sm:inline text-ivory-400">•</span>
          <span className="hidden sm:inline text-charcoal-500 font-serif italic lowercase">
            {dateFormatted}
          </span>
        </div>

        {/* Right: Personalized Greeting & Secondary Controls */}
        <div className="flex items-center space-x-4 mt-1 sm:mt-0">
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-1.5 text-charcoal-700">
            <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-forest-500 animate-pulse" />
            <span className="text-charcoal-500 font-sans">Curated for</span>
            <button
              onClick={onOpenPreferences}
              className="font-serif italic font-semibold text-charcoal-900 hover:text-forest-600 transition-colors underline decoration-ivory-300 underline-offset-2"
              title="Click to personalize name and topics"
            >
              {preferences.readerName || 'Reader'}
            </button>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center space-x-2 no-print">
            {onToggleAudio && (
              <button
                id="btn-toggle-audio"
                onClick={onToggleAudio}
                className={`flex items-center space-x-1 px-2.5 py-1 rounded text-[11px] font-sans transition-all ${
                  isPlayingAudio
                    ? 'bg-forest-600 text-white font-medium shadow-xs'
                    : 'bg-ivory-200/80 hover:bg-ivory-200 text-charcoal-800'
                }`}
                title={isPlayingAudio ? 'Pause Audio Brief' : 'Play Audio Brief (Web Speech)'}
              >
                {isPlayingAudio ? (
                  <VolumeX className="w-3 h-3 text-white" />
                ) : (
                  <Volume2 className="w-3 h-3 text-forest-600" />
                )}
                <span>{isPlayingAudio ? 'Listening...' : 'Audio Brief'}</span>
              </button>
            )}

            <button
              id="btn-print-newspaper"
              onClick={onPrint}
              className="flex items-center space-x-1 px-3 py-1 bg-charcoal-900 hover:bg-charcoal-800 text-ivory-50 rounded text-[11px] font-medium transition-all shadow-xs"
              title="Save as PDF or Print"
            >
              <Printer className="w-3 h-3 text-ivory-300" />
              <span>Print / PDF</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* 2. GRAND MASTHEAD */}
      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 py-6 md:py-8 text-center relative">
        <div className="inline-block cursor-pointer group" onClick={onOpenPreferences} title="Click to customize title">
          <h1
            id="newspaper-main-title"
            className="font-headline text-4xl sm:text-6xl md:text-7xl lg:text-8xl font-black text-charcoal-900 tracking-[-0.03em] leading-none transition-colors group-hover:text-forest-700"
          >
            {titleText}
          </h1>
          <p className="font-serif italic text-sm sm:text-base md:text-lg text-charcoal-600 mt-2 tracking-normal">
            Your world, clearly delivered.
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* 3. SECTION NAVIGATION BAR (Editorial Hairlines) */}
      <div className="border-t border-b border-charcoal-900/10 bg-ivory-50/50 no-print">
        <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6">
          <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row items-center justify-between py-2 gap-2">
            {/* Section Links */}
            <nav className="flex items-center flex-wrap justify-center gap-x-5 gap-y-1.5 text-xs font-sans tracking-wider uppercase font-medium">
              <button
                id="filter-all-topics"
                onClick={() => onSelectTopicFilter('all')}
                className={`py-1 transition-all relative ${
                  selectedTopicFilter === 'all'
                    ? 'text-charcoal-900 font-bold border-b-2 border-forest-600 pb-0.5'
                    : 'text-charcoal-500 hover:text-charcoal-900'
                }`}
              >
                Cover & Front Page
              </button>
              {AVAILABLE_TOPICS.map((t) => {
                const isSelected = selectedTopicFilter === t.id;
                const isUserTopic = preferences.selectedTopics.includes(t.id);
                return (
                  <button
                    key={t.id}
                    id={`filter-${t.id}`}
                    onClick={() => onSelectTopicFilter(t.id)}
                    className={`py-1 transition-all relative flex items-center space-x-1 ${
                      isSelected
                        ? 'text-charcoal-900 font-bold border-b-2 border-forest-600 pb-0.5'
                        : isUserTopic
                        ? 'text-charcoal-800 hover:text-charcoal-900 font-medium'
                        : 'text-charcoal-400 hover:text-charcoal-700'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <span>{t.label.split(' ')[0]}</span>
                    {isUserTopic && (
                      <span className="w-1 h-1 rounded-full bg-forest-600" />
                    )}
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </nav>

            {/* Quick Action Tools */}
            <div className="flex items-center space-x-3 text-xs font-sans text-charcoal-600">
              <button
                id="btn-refresh-wire"
                onClick={onRefresh}
                disabled={isRefreshing}
                className="flex items-center space-x-1 hover:text-charcoal-900 transition-colors disabled:opacity-50"
                title="Sync live wire"
              >
                <RotateCw className={`w-3 h-3 ${isRefreshing ? 'animate-spin text-forest-600' : ''}`} />
                <span>{isRefreshing ? 'Syncing...' : 'Sync Wire'}</span>
              </button>

              <span className="text-ivory-400">|</span>

              <button
                id="btn-save-today-edition"
                onClick={onSaveCurrentEdition}
                className="hover:text-charcoal-900 transition-colors"
                title="Archive today's edition snapshot"
              >
                Save Issue
              </button>

              <span className="text-ivory-400">|</span>

              <button
                id="btn-open-editions-archive"
                onClick={onOpenEditions}
                className="hover:text-charcoal-900 transition-colors"
                title="View past editions"
              >
                Archive ({savedEditionsCount})
              </button>

              <span className="text-ivory-400">|</span>

              <button
                id="btn-open-preferences"
                onClick={onOpenPreferences}
                className="flex items-center space-x-1 hover:text-charcoal-900 transition-colors text-forest-700 font-medium"
                title="Edit reading preferences"
              >
                <SlidersHorizontal className="w-3 h-3" />
                <span>Curate</span>
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* 4. ACTIVE PERSONALIZED LENSES STRIP */}
      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 py-2 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between text-xs font-sans text-charcoal-600 border-b border-ivory-200/80 no-print">
        <div className="flex items-center flex-wrap gap-2">
          <span className="text-[11px] font-medium text-charcoal-500 uppercase tracking-wider flex items-center">
            <Sparkles className="w-3 h-3 text-forest-600 mr-1" />
            Curated Lenses:
          </span>
          {preferences.selectedTopics.map((topicId) => {
            const meta = AVAILABLE_TOPICS.find((t) => t.id === topicId);
            return (
              <span
                key={topicId}
                className="inline-flex items-center px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-forest-50 text-forest-700 text-[11px] font-medium border border-forest-100"
              >
                {meta ? meta.label : topicId}
              </span>
            );
          })}
          {preferences.customInterests.slice(0, 3).map((interest) => (
            <span
              key={interest}
              className="inline-flex items-center px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-ivory-200 text-charcoal-800 text-[11px] font-serif italic border border-ivory-300"
            >
              #{interest}
            </span>
          ))}
          {preferences.customInterests.length > 3 && (
            <span className="text-[10px] text-charcoal-400 font-sans">
              +{preferences.customInterests.length - 3} more
            </span>
          )}
        </div>

        <button
          onClick={onOpenPreferences}
          className="text-[11px] text-charcoal-500 hover:text-forest-700 font-sans font-medium transition-colors hidden sm:inline-flex items-center space-x-0.5"
        >
          <span>Adjust Lenses</span>
          <ArrowUpRight className="w-3 h-3" />
        </button>
      </div>
    </header>
  );
}

FILE: /Ai-Hackathon-project/components/OnboardingModal.tsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
'use client';

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { X, Check, Plus, Sparkles, Tag, Compass } from 'lucide-react';
import { UserPreferences, NewsTopic, AVAILABLE_TOPICS } from '@/types/news';

interface OnboardingModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  preferences: UserPreferences;
  onSave: (newPrefs: UserPreferences) => void;
  isFirstVisit?: boolean;
}

const INTEREST_SUGGESTIONS = [
  'Artificial Intelligence',
  'Space Exploration',
  'Clean Energy',
  'Quantum Computing',
  'Global Geopolitics',
  'Semiconductors & Chips',
  'Biotechnology',
  'Electric Vehicles',
  'Venture Capital',
  'Cybersecurity',
  'Macroeconomics',
  'Neuroscience',
];

export default function OnboardingModal({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  preferences,
  onSave,
  isFirstVisit = false,
}: OnboardingModalProps) {
  const [readerName, setReaderName] = useState(preferences.readerName || '');
  const [newspaperTitle, setNewspaperTitle] = useState(preferences.newspaperTitle || 'DAYMARK');
  const [selectedTopics, setSelectedTopics] = useState<NewsTopic[]>(preferences.selectedTopics || ['technology', 'world']);
  const [customInterests, setCustomInterests] = useState<string[]>(preferences.customInterests || []);
  const [interestInput, setInterestInput] = useState('');
  const [readingPace, setReadingPace] = useState<'quick' | 'standard' | 'deep'>(preferences.readingPace || 'standard');

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const toggleTopic = (topic: NewsTopic) => {
    if (selectedTopics.includes(topic)) {
      if (selectedTopics.length > 1) {
        setSelectedTopics(selectedTopics.filter((t) => t !== topic));
      }
    } else {
      setSelectedTopics([...selectedTopics, topic]);
    }
  };

  const addCustomInterest = (interest: string) => {
    const trimmed = interest.trim();
    if (trimmed && !customInterests.some((i) => i.toLowerCase() === trimmed.toLowerCase())) {
      setCustomInterests([...customInterests, trimmed]);
      setInterestInput('');
    }
  };

  const removeCustomInterest = (interestToRemove: string) => {
    setCustomInterests(customInterests.filter((i) => i !== interestToRemove));
  };

  const handleKeyDownInterest = (e: React.KeyboardEvent) => {
    if (e.key === 'Enter') {
      e.preventDefault();
      addCustomInterest(interestInput);
    }
  };

  const handleSave = () => {
    onSave({
      ...preferences,
      readerName: readerName.trim() || 'Reader',
      newspaperTitle: newspaperTitle.trim() || 'DAYMARK',
      selectedTopics,
      customInterests,
      readingPace,
      onboarded: true,
    });
    onClose();
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-charcoal-900/60 backdrop-blur-xs animate-in fade-in duration-200">
      <div 
        className="bg-ivory-100 border border-ivory-300 rounded-lg shadow-2xl max-w-2xl w-full max-h-[92vh] overflow-y-auto p-6 sm:p-8"
        role="dialog"
        aria-modal="true"
        aria-labelledby="onboarding-title"
      >
        {/* Header */}
        <div className="flex items-start justify-between pb-4 border-b border-ivory-300">
          <div>
            <div className="flex items-center space-x-1.5 text-forest-700 font-sans font-semibold text-[11px] uppercase tracking-widest">
              <Compass className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>{isFirstVisit ? 'Welcome to Daymark' : 'Curate Your Morning Broadsheet'}</span>
            </div>
            <h2 id="onboarding-title" className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-headline font-bold text-charcoal-900 mt-1">
              Personalize Your Edition
            </h2>
            <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-serif italic text-charcoal-600 mt-1">
              Your world, clearly delivered. Select your focus areas and topics for a tailored daily dispatch.
            </p>
          </div>
          {!isFirstVisit && (
            <button
              onClick={onClose}
              className="p-1 text-charcoal-400 hover:text-charcoal-800 transition-colors"
              aria-label="Close preferences"
            >
              <X className="w-5 h-5" />
            </button>
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-6 py-4">
          {/* Section 1: Masthead Identity */}
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-[11px] font-sans font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-charcoal-700 mb-1">
                Your Name
              </label>
              <input
                type="text"
                value={readerName}
                onChange={(e) => {
                  setReaderName(e.target.value);
                  if (newspaperTitle === 'DAYMARK' || newspaperTitle.includes('DAYMARK')) {
                    setNewspaperTitle(e.target.value.trim() ? `${e.target.value.trim().toUpperCase()} DAYMARK` : 'DAYMARK');
                  }
                }}
                placeholder="e.g. Eleanor Vance"
                className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-ivory-50 border border-ivory-300 rounded text-sm text-charcoal-900 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-forest-600 font-serif"
              />
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className="block text-[11px] font-sans font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-charcoal-700 mb-1">
                Publication Masthead Name
              </label>
              <input
                type="text"
                value={newspaperTitle}
                onChange={(e) => setNewspaperTitle(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="e.g. DAYMARK"
                className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-ivory-50 border border-ivory-300 rounded text-sm text-charcoal-900 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-forest-600 font-headline uppercase"
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Section 2: Core News Topics */}
          <div>
            <label className="block text-[11px] font-sans font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-charcoal-700 mb-2">
              Select Editorial Topics (At least 1)
            </label>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-4 gap-2.5">
              {AVAILABLE_TOPICS.map((topic) => {
                const isSelected = selectedTopics.includes(topic.id);
                return (
                  <button
                    key={topic.id}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => toggleTopic(topic.id)}
                    className={`p-3 rounded border text-left flex flex-col justify-between transition-all ${
                      isSelected
                        ? 'bg-forest-50 border-forest-600 text-charcoal-900 shadow-2xs'
                        : 'bg-ivory-50/80 border-ivory-200 text-charcoal-600 hover:border-ivory-300'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between w-full mb-1">
                      <span className="text-xs font-sans font-bold">{topic.label}</span>
                      {isSelected ? (
                        <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-forest-700" />
                      ) : (
                        <div className="w-3.5 h-3.5 rounded-full border border-ivory-400" />
                      )}
                    </div>
                    <span className="text-[10px] text-charcoal-500 line-clamp-1">{topic.description}</span>
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Section 3: Custom Interests & Keyword Boosters */}
          <div>
            <label className="block text-[11px] font-sans font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-charcoal-700 mb-1">
              Custom Topic Lenses & Priority Keywords
            </label>
            <p className="text-xs text-charcoal-500 mb-2">
              Add niche interests. Our ranking engine elevates stories matching these exact phrases and subjects.
            </p>

            {/* Input row */}
            <div className="flex gap-2 mb-2.5">
              <input
                type="text"
                value={interestInput}
                onChange={(e) => setInterestInput(e.target.value)}
                onKeyDown={handleKeyDownInterest}
                placeholder="e.g. Clean Energy, SpaceX, Venture Capital..."
                className="flex-1 px-3 py-2 bg-ivory-50 border border-ivory-300 rounded text-sm text-charcoal-900 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-forest-600 font-serif"
              />
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => addCustomInterest(interestInput)}
                className="px-4 py-2 bg-charcoal-900 text-ivory-50 rounded text-xs font-sans font-medium hover:bg-black transition-colors flex items-center space-x-1"
              >
                <Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                <span>Add</span>
              </button>
            </div>

            {/* Active custom tags */}
            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5 min-h-[34px] p-2 bg-ivory-200/50 rounded border border-ivory-300 mb-2">
              {customInterests.length === 0 ? (
                <span className="text-xs text-charcoal-400 font-serif italic">No custom lenses added yet. Click suggestions below or type your own.</span>
              ) : (
                customInterests.map((interest) => (
                  <span
                    key={interest}
                    className="inline-flex items-center space-x-1 px-2.5 py-1 bg-ivory-50 border border-forest-300 rounded-full text-xs font-sans font-medium text-forest-800 shadow-2xs"
                  >
                    <span>{interest}</span>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => removeCustomInterest(interest)}
                      className="text-charcoal-400 hover:text-terracotta-600 ml-1"
                    >
                      <X className="w-3 h-3" />
                    </button>
                  </span>
                ))
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Quick Suggestions Chips */}
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5">
              <span className="text-[11px] font-sans text-charcoal-500 mr-1 flex items-center">
                <Tag className="w-3 h-3 mr-1" /> Suggestions:
              </span>
              {INTEREST_SUGGESTIONS.filter((s) => !customInterests.includes(s)).slice(0, 6).map((s) => (
                <button
                  key={s}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => addCustomInterest(s)}
                  className="text-[11px] font-sans px-2.5 py-0.5 bg-ivory-200 hover:bg-ivory-300 text-charcoal-700 rounded transition-colors"
                >
                  + {s}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Section 4: Reading Pace */}
          <div>
            <label className="block text-[11px] font-sans font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-charcoal-700 mb-2">
              Morning Reading Pace
            </label>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-3">
              {[
                {
                  id: 'quick',
                  label: 'Quick Scan',
                  time: '~5 Minutes',
                  desc: 'Focuses on rapid wire briefs & top bullet points',
                },
                {
                  id: 'standard',
                  label: 'Standard Edition',
                  time: '~15 Minutes',
                  desc: 'A balanced broadsheet with lead stories and analysis',
                },
                {
                  id: 'deep',
                  label: 'Deep Dive',
                  time: '~30 Minutes',
                  desc: 'In-depth reporting, long reads, and commentary',
                },
              ].map((pace) => {
                const isSelected = readingPace === pace.id;
                return (
                  <button
                    key={pace.id}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setReadingPace(pace.id as any)}
                    className={`p-3 rounded border text-left transition-all ${
                      isSelected
                        ? 'bg-forest-50 border-forest-600 text-charcoal-900 shadow-2xs'
                        : 'bg-ivory-50/80 border-ivory-200 text-charcoal-600 hover:border-ivory-300'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
                      <span className="text-xs font-sans font-bold">{pace.label}</span>
                      <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-forest-700 font-semibold">{pace.time}</span>
                    </div>
                    <p className="text-[11px] text-charcoal-500 font-serif leading-tight">{pace.desc}</p>
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Footer actions */}
        <div className="pt-4 border-t border-ivory-300 flex items-center justify-between">
          <span className="text-xs font-serif text-charcoal-500 italic">
            Deterministic rule-based ranking • Saved locally
          </span>
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-3">
            {!isFirstVisit && (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={onClose}
                className="px-4 py-2 text-xs font-sans font-medium text-charcoal-600 hover:text-charcoal-900"
              >
                Cancel
              </button>
            )}
            <button
              id="btn-save-preferences-confirm"
              type="button"
              onClick={handleSave}
              className="px-6 py-2.5 bg-forest-600 hover:bg-forest-700 text-white rounded text-sm font-sans font-semibold tracking-wide shadow-xs transition-colors flex items-center space-x-2"
            >
              <Sparkles className="w-4 h-4 text-ivory-200" />
              <span>{isFirstVisit ? 'Generate My Morning Edition' : 'Update & Re-Rank Daymark'}</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

'use client';

import React from 'react';
import { Printer, CheckCircle2 } from 'lucide-react';

interface PrintPreviewModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onConfirmPrint: () => void;
  newspaperTitle: string;
}

export default function PrintPreviewModal({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  onConfirmPrint,
  newspaperTitle,
}: PrintPreviewModalProps) {
  if (!isOpen) return null;

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-charcoal-900/60 backdrop-blur-xs animate-in fade-in duration-150 no-print">
      <div 
        className="bg-ivory-100 border border-ivory-300 rounded-lg shadow-2xl max-w-md w-full p-6 text-center"
        role="dialog"
        aria-modal="true"
        aria-labelledby="print-dialog-title"
      >
        <div className="w-12 h-12 bg-forest-50 border border-forest-200 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-3">
          <Printer className="w-6 h-6 text-forest-700" />
        </div>

        <h3 id="print-dialog-title" className="text-xl font-headline font-bold text-charcoal-900 mb-1">
          Export as Broadsheet PDF
        </h3>

        <p className="text-xs font-serif italic text-charcoal-600 mb-4 leading-relaxed">
          Your personalized edition of <strong className="text-charcoal-900 font-sans not-italic font-bold">{newspaperTitle}</strong> is formatted for clean broadsheet print and digital archiving.
        </p>

        {/* Tip Box */}
        <div className="p-3.5 bg-ivory-200/50 border border-ivory-300 rounded-md text-left text-xs font-sans space-y-2 mb-5">
          <div className="flex items-start space-x-2">
            <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-forest-600 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
            <p className="text-charcoal-800">
              In the browser prompt, select Destination: <strong>&ldquo;Save as PDF&rdquo;</strong>.
            </p>
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-start space-x-2">
            <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-forest-600 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
            <p className="text-charcoal-800">
              In More Settings, enable <strong>&ldquo;Background graphics&rdquo;</strong> for authentic paper tone.
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center justify-center space-x-3">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            className="px-4 py-2 border border-ivory-300 rounded text-xs font-sans text-charcoal-600 hover:bg-ivory-200"
          >
            Cancel
          </button>
          <button
            id="confirm-print-action"
            type="button"
            onClick={() => {
              onConfirmPrint();
              onClose();
            }}
            className="px-5 py-2 bg-forest-600 hover:bg-forest-700 text-white rounded text-xs font-sans font-medium shadow-xs flex items-center space-x-1.5 transition-colors"
          >
            <Printer className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            <span>Open Print Dialog</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: /Ai-Hackathon-project/components/SavedEditionsDrawer.tsx

'use client';

import React from 'react';
import { X, Calendar, Newspaper, Trash2, ArrowRight, BookOpen } from 'lucide-react';
import { SavedEdition } from '@/types/news';

interface SavedEditionsDrawerProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  editions: SavedEdition[];
  onSelectEdition: (edition: SavedEdition) => void;
  onDeleteEdition: (id: string) => void;
  onSaveCurrent: () => void;
  activeEditionId?: string | null;
  onResetToLive: () => void;
}

export default function SavedEditionsDrawer({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  editions,
  onSelectEdition,
  onDeleteEdition,
  onSaveCurrent,
  activeEditionId,
  onResetToLive,
}: SavedEditionsDrawerProps) {
  if (!isOpen) return null;

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 overflow-hidden bg-charcoal-900/50 backdrop-blur-xs flex justify-end animate-in fade-in duration-150">
      <div 
        className="w-full max-w-md bg-ivory-100 h-full shadow-2xl border-l border-ivory-300 flex flex-col"
        role="dialog"
        aria-modal="true"
        aria-labelledby="archive-drawer-title"
      >
        {/* Drawer Header */}
        <div className="p-5 border-b border-ivory-300 flex items-center justify-between bg-ivory-50">
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
            <Newspaper className="w-5 h-5 text-forest-700" />
            <h3 id="archive-drawer-title" className="font-headline text-lg font-bold text-charcoal-900">
              Daymark Editions Archive
            </h3>
          </div>
          <button
            onClick={onClose}
            className="p-1 text-charcoal-400 hover:text-charcoal-800 transition-colors"
            aria-label="Close archive"
          >
            <X className="w-5 h-5" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Current status bar */}
        <div className="px-5 py-3 bg-ivory-200/60 border-b border-ivory-300 flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-sans">
          {activeEditionId ? (
            <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
              <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-forest-600 animate-pulse" />
              <span className="font-semibold text-charcoal-900">Archived Edition Loaded</span>
              <button
                onClick={onResetToLive}
                className="text-forest-700 underline ml-1 hover:text-forest-900 font-medium"
              >
                Return to Live Wire
              </button>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
              <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-forest-600" />
              <span className="font-medium text-charcoal-700">Currently viewing Live Edition</span>
            </div>
          )}
          <button
            onClick={onSaveCurrent}
            className="px-2.5 py-1 bg-charcoal-900 hover:bg-black text-ivory-50 rounded text-[11px] font-medium transition-colors"
          >
            + Save Issue
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Editions List */}
        <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-5 space-y-3">
          {editions.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="text-center py-12 text-charcoal-500 font-serif">
              <BookOpen className="w-10 h-10 mx-auto text-ivory-400 mb-2" />
              <p className="font-bold text-charcoal-800">No saved editions yet</p>
              <p className="text-xs text-charcoal-500 mt-1 max-w-xs mx-auto">
                Snapshot today&apos;s morning paper to revisit headlines, articles, and your reading list later.
              </p>
              <button
                onClick={onSaveCurrent}
                className="mt-4 px-4 py-2 bg-charcoal-900 text-ivory-50 rounded text-xs font-sans font-medium"
              >
                Save Today&apos;s Edition
              </button>
            </div>
          ) : (
            editions.map((ed) => {
              const isSelected = activeEditionId === ed.id;
              return (
                <div
                  key={ed.id}
                  className={`p-3.5 rounded border transition-all ${
                    isSelected
                      ? 'bg-forest-50 border-forest-600 shadow-2xs'
                      : 'bg-ivory-50/80 border-ivory-300 hover:border-ivory-400'
                  }`}
                >
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-[11px] font-sans text-charcoal-500 mb-1">
                    <span className="flex items-center font-medium text-forest-700">
                      <Calendar className="w-3 h-3 mr-1" />
                      {ed.dateStr}
                    </span>
                    <span>{ed.articlesCount} stories</span>
                  </div>

                  <h4 className="font-serif font-bold text-sm text-charcoal-900 line-clamp-2 leading-snug mb-1">
                    {ed.leadHeadline || 'Morning Edition Headlines'}
                  </h4>

                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between pt-2 border-t border-ivory-200 text-xs font-sans mt-2">
                    <button
                      onClick={() => {
                        onSelectEdition(ed);
                        onClose();
                      }}
                      className="inline-flex items-center space-x-1 text-forest-700 font-semibold hover:underline"
                    >
                      <span>{isSelected ? 'Currently Viewing' : 'Read Edition'}</span>
                      <ArrowRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                    </button>

                    <button
                      onClick={() => onDeleteEdition(ed.id)}
                      className="text-charcoal-400 hover:text-terracotta-600 p-1 transition-colors"
                      title="Delete saved edition"
                    >
                      <Trash2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              );
            })
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Footer */}
        <div className="p-4 border-t border-ivory-300 bg-ivory-50 text-center text-xs font-serif text-charcoal-500">
          Saved offline to browser storage
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

interface SectionBlockProps {

  dislikedIds: string[];
  bookmarkedIds: string[];

export default function SectionBlock({
  title,
  sectionCode = 'SECTION',
  articles,
  onLike,
  onDislike,
  onSkip,
  onBookmark,
  onWhyThis,
  likedIds,
  dislikedIds,
  bookmarkedIds,

      </div>

            key={article.id}
            article={article}
            variant="standard"
            onLike={onLike}
            onDislike={onDislike}
            onSkip={onSkip}
            onBookmark={onBookmark}
            onWhyThis={onWhyThis}
            isLiked={likedIds.includes(article.id)}
            isDisliked={dislikedIds.includes(article.id)}
            isBookmarked={bookmarkedIds.includes(article.id)}
          />
        ))}
      </div>

  );
}
