'use client';

import React from 'react';
import { X, Sparkles, CheckCircle2, Clock, ThumbsUp, Compass, Target } from 'lucide-react';
import { ScoredArticle } from '@/lib/ranking';
import { UserPreferences } from '@/types/news';

interface WhyThisModalProps {
  article: ScoredArticle | null;
  onClose: () => void;
  preferences: UserPreferences;
}

export default function WhyThisModal({ article, onClose, preferences }: WhyThisModalProps) {
  if (!article) return null;

  const breakdown = article.scoreBreakdown || {
    topicScore: 20,
    keywordScore: 16,
    recencyScore: 16,
    paceScore: 10,
    feedbackScore: 0,
    total: article.relevanceScore || 85,
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-charcoal-900/60 backdrop-blur-xs animate-in fade-in duration-150">
      <div 
        className="bg-ivory-100 border border-ivory-300 rounded-lg shadow-2xl max-w-lg w-full p-6 sm:p-7"

              <span>Transparent Curation Logic</span>

              Why this story was ranked for you
            </h3>
          </div>

            onClick={onClose}
            className="p-1 text-charcoal-400 hover:text-charcoal-800 transition-colors"
            aria-label="Close dialog"

            <X className="w-5 h-5" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Article Summary */}
        <div className="py-3 border-b border-ivory-200">
          <span className="text-[10px] font-sans font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-forest-700">
            {article.source.name} • {article.category.toUpperCase()}
          </span>
          <h4 className="text-sm font-serif font-bold text-charcoal-900 leading-snug mt-0.5">
            {article.title}
          </h4>
        </div>

        {/* Big Score Header */}
        <div className="my-4 p-4 bg-ivory-200/60 rounded-md border border-ivory-300 flex items-center justify-between">
          <div>
            <span className="text-xs font-sans text-charcoal-500 block">Personal Relevance Score</span>
            <span className="text-sm font-serif italic text-charcoal-800">{article.rankingReason}</span>
          </div>
          <div className="text-right">
            <span className="text-3xl font-headline font-black text-forest-700">
              {breakdown.total}%
            </span>
            <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-charcoal-400 block uppercase">Match Quality</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Breakdown List */}
        <div className="space-y-3 py-2 text-xs font-sans">
          {/* Topic Match */}
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
              <Compass className="w-4 h-4 text-forest-700" />
              <div>
                <span className="font-semibold text-charcoal-900">Topic Alignment ({article.category})</span>
                <span className="text-[11px] text-charcoal-500 block">
                  {preferences.selectedTopics.includes(article.category)
                    ? 'Active subscription in your preferences'
                    : 'Discovered from general editorial wire'}
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>
            <span className="font-mono font-bold text-charcoal-900">+{breakdown.topicScore} pts</span>
          </div>

          {/* Keywords Match */}
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
              <Target className="w-4 h-4 text-forest-700" />
              <div>
                <span className="font-semibold text-charcoal-900">Custom Interests Match</span>
                <span className="text-[11px] text-charcoal-500 block">
                  {article.matchedKeywords && article.matchedKeywords.length > 0
                    ? `Matched: "${article.matchedKeywords.join('", "')}"`
                    : 'No specific keyword match; broad topic relevancy'}
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>
            <span className="font-mono font-bold text-charcoal-900">+{breakdown.keywordScore} pts</span>
          </div>

          {/* Recency */}
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
              <Clock className="w-4 h-4 text-forest-700" />
              <div>
                <span className="font-semibold text-charcoal-900">Morning Freshness (Recency)</span>
                <span className="text-[11px] text-charcoal-500 block">
                  Published {new Date(article.publishedAt).toLocaleDateString()} at{' '}
                  {new Date(article.publishedAt).toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' })}
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>
            <span className="font-mono font-bold text-charcoal-900">+{breakdown.recencyScore} pts</span>
          </div>

          {/* Pace Alignment */}
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
              <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-forest-600" />
              <div>
                <span className="font-semibold text-charcoal-900">Reading Pace Alignment</span>
                <span className="text-[11px] text-charcoal-500 block">
                  {article.readTimeMinutes} min read fits your &quot;{preferences.readingPace}&quot; pacing
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>
            <span className="font-mono font-bold text-charcoal-900">+{breakdown.paceScore} pts</span>
          </div>

          {/* Feedback adjustment */}
          {breakdown.feedbackScore !== 0 && (
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
                <ThumbsUp className="w-4 h-4 text-charcoal-700" />
                <div>
                  <span className="font-semibold text-charcoal-900">User Interaction Affinity</span>
                  <span className="text-[11px] text-charcoal-500 block">Feedback from your previous votes</span>
                </div>
              </div>
              <span className={`font-mono font-bold ${breakdown.feedbackScore > 0 ? 'text-forest-700' : 'text-terracotta-600'}`}>
                {breakdown.feedbackScore > 0 ? `+${breakdown.feedbackScore}` : breakdown.feedbackScore} pts
              </span>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Footer */}
        <div className="pt-4 border-t border-ivory-300 mt-2 flex justify-end">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            className="px-4 py-2 bg-charcoal-900 text-ivory-50 rounded text-xs font-sans font-medium hover:bg-black transition-colors"
          >
            Understood
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

# System Architecture: Daymark

> **"Your world, clearly delivered."**  
> Editorial Digital Broadsheet Architecture for NaSCon '26

```mermaid
graph TD
    subgraph External Sources [Public RSS News Wires - PKR 0]
        BBC[BBC News RSS<br/>World, Tech, Business, Science, Health]
        NYT[New York Times RSS<br/>World, Tech, Science, Business, Climate]
        ARS[Ars Technica & NPR RSS<br/>Technology, Culture, Wire]
    end

    subgraph Server Layer [Next.js App Router Server]
        API["/api/news<br/>Route Handler"]
        FETCHER["lib/rss.ts<br/>Parallel Multi-Feed Fetcher<br/>Timeout & Graceful Fallbacks"]
        PARSER["fast-xml-parser<br/>Normalizer, Thumbnail Extractor,<br/>HTML Sanitizer"]
    end

    subgraph Client Layer [Next.js Client Application]
        STORE["lib/storage.ts<br/>LocalStorage Persistence<br/>(Preferences, Feedback, Editions)"]
        RANKER["lib/ranking.ts<br/>Explainable Scoring Engine<br/>Topic Match + Custom Keywords + Recency + Feedback"]
        SPEECH["Web Speech API<br/>SpeechSynthesis Native Audio Brief"]
        PAGE["app/page.tsx<br/>Daymark Broadsheet Controller"]
        
        subgraph Daymark Editorial UI
            MAST["components/Masthead.tsx<br/>Daymark Masthead, Issue Meta, Navigation, Curated Lenses"]
            COVER["Cover Story (Hero Variant)<br/>Two-Column Tide-Inspired Layout"]
            WIRE["The Morning Wire<br/>Compact 2-min briefs sidebar"]
            REFLECT["Daily Reflection<br/>Reflecta-Inspired Editorial Card"]
            SECTIONS["Categorical Section Blocks<br/>Tech, World, Business, Science"]
            MODAL_ONB["components/OnboardingModal.tsx<br/>Topic & Interest Lens Curator"]
            MODAL_WHY["components/WhyThisModal.tsx<br/>Transparent Explainability Breakdown"]
            DRAWER_ARCH["components/SavedEditionsDrawer.tsx<br/>Archive & Restore Past Editions"]
            MODAL_PRINT["components/PrintPreviewBanner.tsx<br/>Print Dialog & Save as PDF Engine"]
        end
    end

    External Sources --> FETCHER
    FETCHER --> PARSER
    PARSER --> API
    API -->|Aggregated Articles| PAGE
    STORE -->|Preferences & Feedback| RANKER
    PAGE --> RANKER
    PAGE --> SPEECH
    RANKER -->|Ranked & Scored Articles| COVER
    RANKER --> WIRE
    RANKER --> SECTIONS
    PAGE --> MAST
    PAGE --> REFLECT
    PAGE --> MODAL_ONB
    PAGE --> MODAL_WHY
    PAGE --> DRAWER_ARCH
    PAGE --> MODAL_PRINT
```

---

## Visual Design Palette
- **Canvas / Paper**: Warm Ivory `#F7F5EF` (`bg-ivory-100`)
- **Typography**: Near-black Charcoal `#20211F` (`text-charcoal-900`)
- **Primary Accent**: Restrained Forest Green `#496653` (`text-forest-700`, `bg-forest-50`, `border-forest-600`)
- **Secondary Accent**: Terracotta `#B85D38`
- **Dividers & Rules**: Thin hairlines `#E5DFD1` (`border-ivory-300`)


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /Ai-Hackathon-project/lib/ranking.ts
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import { Article, UserPreferences, UserFeedback } from '@/types/news';

export interface ScoredArticle extends Article {
  relevanceScore: number;
  rankingReason: string;
  matchedKeywords: string[];
  scoreBreakdown: {
    topicScore: number;
    keywordScore: number;
    recencyScore: number;
    paceScore: number;
    feedbackScore: number;
    total: number;
  };
}

export function rankArticles(
  articles: Article[],
  preferences: UserPreferences,
  feedback: UserFeedback,
  includeSkipped: boolean = false
): ScoredArticle[] {
  const scored = articles
    .filter((art) => {
      if (!includeSkipped && feedback.skippedArticleIds.includes(art.id)) {
        return false;
      }
      return true;
    })
    .map((article) => {
      let topicScore = 0;
      let keywordScore = 0;
      let recencyScore = 0;
      let paceScore = 0;
      let feedbackScore = 0;
      const matchedKeywords: string[] = [];
      const reasons: string[] = [];

      const textToSearch = `${article.title} ${article.description}`.toLowerCase();

      // 1. Topic Score (Base 0 - 30)
      const isSelectedTopic = preferences.selectedTopics.includes(article.category);
      if (isSelectedTopic) {
        topicScore += 20;
        reasons.push(`Selected topic (${article.category})`);
      } else {
        topicScore += 5; // general discovery
      }

      // Add dynamic topic affinity from feedback
      const affinity = feedback.topicAffinities[article.category] || 0;
      const topicAffinityScore = affinity * 2.5; // -12.5 to +12.5
      topicScore += topicAffinityScore;
      if (affinity > 0) {
        reasons.push(`High engagement with ${article.category}`);
      }

      // 2. Custom Interests Match (0 - 35)
      for (const interest of preferences.customInterests) {
        const cleanInterest = interest.trim().toLowerCase();
        if (!cleanInterest) continue;

        // Exact phrase match
        if (textToSearch.includes(cleanInterest)) {
          keywordScore += 16;
          matchedKeywords.push(interest);
        } else {
          // Token matches
          const tokens = cleanInterest.split(/\s+/).filter((t) => t.length > 3);
          const hasTokenMatch = tokens.some((token) => textToSearch.includes(token));
          if (hasTokenMatch) {
            keywordScore += 8;
            matchedKeywords.push(interest);
          }
        }
      }

      // Keyword affinities from feedback
      for (const [kw, aff] of Object.entries(feedback.keywordAffinities)) {
        if (textToSearch.includes(kw.toLowerCase())) {
          keywordScore += aff * 3;
        }
      }
      keywordScore = Math.min(Math.max(keywordScore, 0), 35);
      if (matchedKeywords.length > 0) {
        reasons.push(`Matches interest: "${matchedKeywords.slice(0, 2).join('", "')}"`);
      }

      // 3. Recency Score (0 - 20)
      const pubDate = new Date(article.publishedAt);
      const hoursAgo = Math.max(0, (Date.now() - pubDate.getTime()) / (1000 * 60 * 60));
      if (hoursAgo <= 3) {
        recencyScore = 20;
        reasons.push('Just in (under 3h ago)');
      } else if (hoursAgo <= 6) {
        recencyScore = 16;
        reasons.push('Fresh (under 6h ago)');
      } else if (hoursAgo <= 12) {
        recencyScore = 12;
      } else if (hoursAgo <= 24) {
        recencyScore = 8;
      } else {
        recencyScore = 4;
      }

      // 4. Reading Pace Alignment (0 - 10)
      if (preferences.readingPace === 'quick') {
        paceScore = article.readTimeMinutes <= 3 ? 10 : article.readTimeMinutes <= 5 ? 6 : 2;
      } else if (preferences.readingPace === 'deep') {
        paceScore = article.readTimeMinutes >= 5 ? 10 : 5;
      } else {
        // standard
        paceScore = article.readTimeMinutes >= 3 && article.readTimeMinutes <= 6 ? 10 : 6;
      }

      // 5. Direct Feedback (Likes / Dislikes)
      if (feedback.likedArticleIds.includes(article.id)) {
        feedbackScore += 20;
        reasons.push('You liked this');
      }
      if (feedback.dislikedArticleIds.includes(article.id)) {
        feedbackScore -= 45;
        reasons.push('Previously downvoted');
      }

      const totalRaw = topicScore + keywordScore + recencyScore + paceScore + feedbackScore;
      // Clamp 5 to 99 for a polished percentage display
      const total = Math.min(Math.max(Math.round(totalRaw), 5), 99);

      let rankingReason = '';
      if (reasons.length > 0) {
        rankingReason = reasons.slice(0, 2).join(' • ');
      } else {
        rankingReason = 'Curated general interest from morning wire';
      }

      return {
        ...article,
        relevanceScore: total,
        rankingReason,
        matchedKeywords: Array.from(new Set(matchedKeywords)),
        scoreBreakdown: {
          topicScore: Math.round(topicScore),
          keywordScore: Math.round(keywordScore),
          recencyScore: Math.round(recencyScore),
          paceScore: Math.round(paceScore),
          feedbackScore: Math.round(feedbackScore),
          total,
        },
      };
    });

  // Sort by relevance score descending
  return scored.sort((a, b) => (b.relevanceScore || 0) - (a.relevanceScore || 0));
}

import { XMLParser } from 'fast-xml-parser';
import { Article, NewsTopic } from '@/types/news';

interface FeedSource {
  name: string;
  url: string;
  category: NewsTopic;
  domain: string;
}

const FEED_SOURCES: FeedSource[] = [
  // Technology
  { name: 'BBC News', url: 'http://feeds.bbci.co.uk/news/technology/rss.xml', category: 'technology', domain: 'bbc.com' },
  { name: 'Ars Technica', url: 'https://feeds.arstechnica.com/arstechnica/index', category: 'technology', domain: 'arstechnica.com' },
  { name: 'NYT Technology', url: 'https://rss.nytimes.com/services/xml/rss/nyt/Technology.xml', category: 'technology', domain: 'nytimes.com' },

  { name: 'NYT World', url: 'https://rss.nytimes.com/services/xml/rss/nyt/World.xml', category: 'world', domain: 'nytimes.com' },

  { name: 'NYT Science', url: 'https://rss.nytimes.com/services/xml/rss/nyt/Science.xml', category: 'science', domain: 'nytimes.com' },

  // Arts & Culture
  { name: 'NYT Arts', url: 'https://rss.nytimes.com/services/xml/rss/nyt/Arts.xml', category: 'culture', domain: 'nytimes.com' },

  // Health
  { name: 'NYT Health', url: 'https://rss.nytimes.com/services/xml/rss/nyt/Health.xml', category: 'health', domain: 'nytimes.com' },
  { name: 'BBC Health', url: 'http://feeds.bbci.co.uk/news/health/rss.xml', category: 'health', domain: 'bbc.com' },

  // Climate / Environment
  { name: 'NYT Climate', url: 'https://rss.nytimes.com/services/xml/rss/nyt/Climate.xml', category: 'environment', domain: 'nytimes.com' },

  // Opinion / Ideas
  { name: 'NYT Opinion', url: 'https://rss.nytimes.com/services/xml/rss/nyt/Opinion.xml', category: 'opinion', domain: 'nytimes.com' },
];

const parser = new XMLParser({
  ignoreAttributes: false,
  attributeNamePrefix: '@_',
  textNodeName: '#text',
  trimValues: true,
});

function stripHtml(html: string): string {
  if (!html) return '';
  return html
    .replace(/<!\[CDATA\[([\s\S]*?)\]\]>/gi, '$1')
    .replace(/<[^>]*>/g, '')
    .replace(/&amp;/g, '&')
    .replace(/&lt;/g, '<')
    .replace(/&gt;/g, '>')
    .replace(/&quot;/g, '"')
    .replace(/&#39;/g, "'")
    .replace(/&nbsp;/g, ' ')
    .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
    .trim();
}

function extractImage(item: any): string | undefined {
  // 1. Check media:content (could be array or single object)
  const mediaContent = item['media:content'];
  if (Array.isArray(mediaContent)) {
    const img = mediaContent.find((m) => m['@_medium'] === 'image' || m['@_url']);
    if (img && img['@_url']) return img['@_url'];
  } else if (mediaContent && mediaContent['@_url']) {
    return mediaContent['@_url'];
  }

  // 2. Check media:thumbnail
  const mediaThumb = item['media:thumbnail'];
  if (Array.isArray(mediaThumb)) {
    if (mediaThumb[0] && mediaThumb[0]['@_url']) return mediaThumb[0]['@_url'];
  } else if (mediaThumb && mediaThumb['@_url']) {
    return mediaThumb['@_url'];
  }

  // 3. Check enclosure
  const enclosure = item.enclosure;
  if (enclosure && enclosure['@_url'] && enclosure['@_type']?.startsWith('image/')) {
    return enclosure['@_url'];
  }

  // 4. Check HTML in description or content:encoded
  const htmlContent = item['content:encoded'] || item.description || '';
  if (typeof htmlContent === 'string') {
    const match = htmlContent.match(/<img[^>]+src=["']([^"']+)["']/i);
    if (match && match[1]) {
      return match[1];
    }
  }

  return undefined;
}

function generateSimpleId(str: string): string {
  let hash = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < str.length; i++) {
    hash = (hash << 5) - hash + str.charCodeAt(i);
    hash |= 0;
  }
  return 'art_' + Math.abs(hash).toString(36);
}

function estimateReadTime(text: string): number {
  const words = text.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean).length;
  // Estimate assuming full article is about 4x the summary
  const estimatedFullWords = Math.max(words * 4, 300);
  const minutes = Math.ceil(estimatedFullWords / 200); // 200 words per minute
  return Math.min(Math.max(minutes, 2), 10);
}

export async function fetchFeedArticles(source: FeedSource): Promise<Article[]> {
  const controller = new AbortController();
  const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 6000);

  try {
    const res = await fetch(source.url, {
      signal: controller.signal,
      headers: {
        'User-Agent': 'Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) MorningPaper/1.0',
        'Accept': 'application/rss+xml, application/xml, text/xml, */*',
      },
      next: { revalidate: 300 }, // Cache server-side for 5 minutes
    });

    clearTimeout(timeoutId);

    if (!res.ok) {
      console.warn(`[RSS] Failed to fetch ${source.name}: ${res.status}`);
      return [];
    }

    const xmlText = await res.text();
    const parsed = parser.parse(xmlText);

    let rawItems: any[] = [];
    if (parsed.rss && parsed.rss.channel && parsed.rss.channel.item) {
      rawItems = Array.isArray(parsed.rss.channel.item) ? parsed.rss.channel.item : [parsed.rss.channel.item];
    } else if (parsed.feed && parsed.feed.entry) {
      rawItems = Array.isArray(parsed.feed.entry) ? parsed.feed.entry : [parsed.feed.entry];
    }

    const articles: Article[] = [];

    for (const item of rawItems) {
      const title = stripHtml(typeof item.title === 'string' ? item.title : item.title?.['#text'] || '');
      const rawDesc = item.description || item.summary || item['content:encoded'] || '';
      const description = stripHtml(typeof rawDesc === 'string' ? rawDesc : rawDesc?.['#text'] || '');

      let link = '';
      if (typeof item.link === 'string') {
        link = item.link;
      } else if (item.link && item.link['@_href']) {
        link = item.link['@_href'];
      } else if (Array.isArray(item.link)) {
        const alt = item.link.find((l: any) => l['@_rel'] === 'alternate' || !l['@_rel']);
        link = alt ? alt['@_href'] : item.link[0]?.['@_href'] || '';
      }

      if (!title || !link) continue;

      const publishedAt = item.pubDate || item.published || item.updated || new Date().toISOString();
      const imageUrl = extractImage(item);
      const author = item['dc:creator'] || item.author?.name || undefined;
      const readTime = estimateReadTime(description);

      articles.push({
        id: generateSimpleId(link + title),
        title,
        description: description || 'No summary provided. Tap to read the full report.',
        url: link,
        source: {
          name: source.name,
          domain: source.domain,
        },
        category: source.category,
        publishedAt: new Date(publishedAt).toISOString(),
        imageUrl,
        author: author ? stripHtml(author) : undefined,
        readTimeMinutes: readTime,
      });
    }

    return articles.slice(0, 15); // Take top 15 from each feed
  } catch (error: any) {
    clearTimeout(timeoutId);
    console.warn(`[RSS Error] ${source.name}: ${error?.message || error}`);
    return [];
  }
}

export async function fetchAllNews(selectedTopics?: NewsTopic[]): Promise<Article[]> {
  const sourcesToFetch = selectedTopics && selectedTopics.length > 0
    ? FEED_SOURCES.filter((s) => selectedTopics.includes(s.category))
    : FEED_SOURCES;

  // Run in parallel with Promise.allSettled
  const results = await Promise.allSettled(sourcesToFetch.map((src) => fetchFeedArticles(src)));

  const allArticles: Article[] = [];
  const seenUrls = new Set<string>();

  for (const res of results) {
    if (res.status === 'fulfilled') {
      for (const article of res.value) {
        // Deduplicate
        const cleanUrl = article.url.split('?')[0].toLowerCase();
        if (!seenUrls.has(cleanUrl) && !seenUrls.has(article.title.toLowerCase())) {
          seenUrls.add(cleanUrl);
          seenUrls.add(article.title.toLowerCase());
          allArticles.push(article);
        }
      }
    }
  }

  // Sort initially by publication date descending
  return allArticles.sort((a, b) => new Date(b.publishedAt).getTime() - new Date(a.publishedAt).getTime());
}

import { UserPreferences, UserFeedback, SavedEdition, DEFAULT_PREFERENCES, Article } from '@/types/news';

const PREFS_KEY = 'morning_chronicle_preferences';
const FEEDBACK_KEY = 'morning_chronicle_feedback';
const EDITIONS_KEY = 'morning_chronicle_saved_editions';

export const INITIAL_FEEDBACK: UserFeedback = {
  likedArticleIds: [],
  dislikedArticleIds: [],
  skippedArticleIds: [],
  savedArticleIds: [],
  topicAffinities: {},
  keywordAffinities: {},
};

export function getStoredPreferences(): UserPreferences {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return DEFAULT_PREFERENCES;
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(PREFS_KEY);
    if (!raw) return DEFAULT_PREFERENCES;
    return { ...DEFAULT_PREFERENCES, ...JSON.parse(raw) };
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('Failed to load preferences from storage:', e);
    return DEFAULT_PREFERENCES;
  }
}

export function saveStoredPreferences(prefs: UserPreferences): void {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return;
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(PREFS_KEY, JSON.stringify(prefs));
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('Failed to save preferences to storage:', e);
  }
}

export function getStoredFeedback(): UserFeedback {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return INITIAL_FEEDBACK;
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(FEEDBACK_KEY);
    if (!raw) return INITIAL_FEEDBACK;
    return { ...INITIAL_FEEDBACK, ...JSON.parse(raw) };
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('Failed to load feedback from storage:', e);
    return INITIAL_FEEDBACK;
  }
}

export function saveStoredFeedback(feedback: UserFeedback): void {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return;
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(FEEDBACK_KEY, JSON.stringify(feedback));
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('Failed to save feedback to storage:', e);
  }
}

export function getStoredEditions(): SavedEdition[] {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return [];
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(EDITIONS_KEY);
    if (!raw) return [];
    return JSON.parse(raw);
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('Failed to load saved editions:', e);
    return [];
  }
}

export function saveCurrentEdition(edition: SavedEdition): void {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return;
  try {
    const existing = getStoredEditions();
    // Keep max 20 saved editions
    const filtered = existing.filter((e) => e.id !== edition.id);
    const updated = [edition, ...filtered].slice(0, 20);
    localStorage.setItem(EDITIONS_KEY, JSON.stringify(updated));
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('Failed to save edition to storage:', e);
  }
}

export function deleteStoredEdition(editionId: string): void {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return;
  try {
    const existing = getStoredEditions();
    const updated = existing.filter((e) => e.id !== editionId);
    localStorage.setItem(EDITIONS_KEY, JSON.stringify(updated));
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('Failed to delete edition from storage:', e);
  }
}

export type NewsTopic = 
  | 'world'
  | 'technology'
  | 'business'
  | 'science'
  | 'culture'
  | 'health'
  | 'environment'
  | 'opinion';

export interface Article {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  description: string;
  url: string;
  source: {
    name: string;
    domain?: string;
  };
  category: NewsTopic;
  publishedAt: string;
  imageUrl?: string;
  author?: string;
  readTimeMinutes: number;
  relevanceScore?: number;
  rankingReason?: string;
  matchedKeywords?: string[];
}

export interface UserPreferences {
  readerName: string;
  newspaperTitle: string;
  selectedTopics: NewsTopic[];
  customInterests: string[];
  readingPace: 'quick' | 'standard' | 'deep'; // quick: 5min, standard: 15min, deep: 30min
  onboarded: boolean;
  theme?: 'vintage' | 'modern' | 'sepia' | 'dark';
}

export interface UserFeedback {
  likedArticleIds: string[];
  dislikedArticleIds: string[];
  skippedArticleIds: string[];
  savedArticleIds: string[];
  topicAffinities: Record<string, number>; // -5 to +5
  keywordAffinities: Record<string, number>; // -5 to +5
}

export interface SavedEdition {
  id: string;
  dateStr: string;
  savedAt: string;
  title: string;
  leadHeadline: string;
  articlesCount: number;
  articles: Article[];
  preferencesSnapshot: UserPreferences;
}

export interface TopicMeta {
  id: NewsTopic;
  label: string;
  description: string;
  iconName: string;
}

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { fetchAllNews } from '@/lib/rss';
import { NewsTopic } from '@/types/news';

export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic';
export const revalidate = 300; // 5 min cache

export async function GET(request: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const { searchParams } = new URL(request.url);
    const topicsParam = searchParams.get('topics');

    let topics: NewsTopic[] | undefined = undefined;
    if (topicsParam) {
      topics = topicsParam.split(',').map((t) => t.trim() as NewsTopic);
    }

    const articles = await fetchAllNews(topics);

    return NextResponse.json({
      success: true,
      articles,
      total: articles.length,
      fetchedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    });
  } catch (error: any) {
    console.error('API /api/news failed:', error);
    return NextResponse.json(
      {
        success: false,
        error: error?.message || 'Failed to fetch live morning feeds',
        articles: [],
      },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}

    column-count: 2 !important;
    column-gap: 20px !important;
    column-rule: 1px solid #D1D5DB !important;
  }
}


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /app/layout.tsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import type { Metadata } from 'next';
import './globals.css';

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  title: 'The Morning Chronicle | Your Personalized Digital Broadsheet',
  description: 'An AI-ranked, editorial morning newspaper tailored to your personal interests, curated from leading global publications.',
  keywords: ['personalized newspaper', 'morning edition', 'news aggregator', 'editorial broadsheet', 'NaSCon 26'],
  authors: [{ name: 'Morning Chronicle Editorial Board' }],
};

export default function RootLayout({
  children,
}: {
  children: React.ReactNode;
}) {
  return (
    <html lang="en">
      <body className="min-h-screen flex flex-col antialiased selection:bg-amber-200 selection:text-ink-900">
        {children}
      </body>
    </html>
  );
}


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /app/page.tsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
'use client';

import React, { useState, useEffect, useMemo, useCallback } from 'react';
import { Article, UserPreferences, UserFeedback, SavedEdition, NewsTopic, DEFAULT_PREFERENCES, AVAILABLE_TOPICS } from '@/types/news';
import { rankArticles, ScoredArticle } from '@/lib/ranking';
import {
  getStoredPreferences,
  saveStoredPreferences,
  getStoredFeedback,
  saveStoredFeedback,
  getStoredEditions,
  saveCurrentEdition,
  deleteStoredEdition,
  INITIAL_FEEDBACK,
} from '@/lib/storage';
import Masthead from '@/components/Masthead';
import ArticleCard from '@/components/ArticleCard';
import SectionBlock from '@/components/SectionBlock';
import OnboardingModal from '@/components/OnboardingModal';
import WhyThisModal from '@/components/WhyThisModal';
import SavedEditionsDrawer from '@/components/SavedEditionsDrawer';
import PrintPreviewModal from '@/components/PrintPreviewBanner';
import { LoadingNewspaper, EmptyState, ErrorState } from '@/components/StatusStates';
import { Sparkles, Bookmark, Search, RotateCcw, Clock, Quote, Printer, ShieldCheck } from 'lucide-react';

export default function MorningChroniclePage() {
  const [preferences, setPreferences] = useState<UserPreferences>(DEFAULT_PREFERENCES);
  const [feedback, setFeedback] = useState<UserFeedback>(INITIAL_FEEDBACK);
  const [savedEditions, setSavedEditions] = useState<SavedEdition[]>([]);
  const [rawArticles, setRawArticles] = useState<Article[]>([]);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [isRefreshing, setIsRefreshing] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Modals & Navigation state
  const [isOnboardingOpen, setIsOnboardingOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isEditionsOpen, setIsEditionsOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isPrintModalOpen, setIsPrintModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [whyThisArticle, setWhyThisArticle] = useState<ScoredArticle | null>(null);
  const [selectedTopicFilter, setSelectedTopicFilter] = useState<NewsTopic | 'all'>('all');
  const [activeEdition, setActiveEdition] = useState<SavedEdition | null>(null);
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [toastMessage, setToastMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [showBookmarksOnly, setShowBookmarksOnly] = useState(false);
  const [isPlayingAudio, setIsPlayingAudio] = useState(false);

  const showToast = (msg: string) => {
    setToastMessage(msg);
    setTimeout(() => {
      setToastMessage((curr) => (curr === msg ? null : curr));
    }, 3200);
  };

  // Load persisted user state on mount
  useEffect(() => {
    const loadedPrefs = getStoredPreferences();
    const loadedFeedback = getStoredFeedback();
    const loadedEditions = getStoredEditions();

    setPreferences(loadedPrefs);
    setFeedback(loadedFeedback);
    setSavedEditions(loadedEditions);

    // If user has never onboarded, show onboarding prompt
    if (!loadedPrefs.onboarded) {
      setIsOnboardingOpen(true);
    }
  }, []);

  // Fetch live articles from API
  const fetchNews = useCallback(async (topicsToFetch?: NewsTopic[]) => {
    try {
      setError(null);
      const url = topicsToFetch && topicsToFetch.length > 0
        ? `/api/news?topics=${topicsToFetch.join(',')}`
        : '/api/news';

      const res = await fetch(url);
      if (!res.ok) {
        throw new Error(`Failed to load wire feed (status ${res.status})`);
      }
      const data = await res.json();
      if (data.success && Array.isArray(data.articles)) {
        setRawArticles(data.articles);
      } else {
        throw new Error(data.error || 'Invalid wire response');
      }
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Fetch news error:', err);
      setError(err?.message || 'Could not connect to live wire');
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
      setIsRefreshing(false);
    }
  }, []);

  // Initial fetch
  useEffect(() => {
    fetchNews(preferences.selectedTopics);
  }, [fetchNews, preferences.selectedTopics]);

  // Handle refresh
  const handleRefresh = async () => {
    setIsRefreshing(true);
    await fetchNews(preferences.selectedTopics);
    showToast('Live wire updated with latest reports');
  };

  // Ranking calculation
  const rankedArticles: ScoredArticle[] = useMemo(() => {
    // If viewing an archived edition snapshot
    const sourceList = activeEdition ? activeEdition.articles : rawArticles;
    const ranked = rankArticles(sourceList, preferences, feedback, false);

    // Filter by topic if active
    let filtered = ranked;
    if (selectedTopicFilter !== 'all') {
      filtered = filtered.filter((a) => a.category === selectedTopicFilter);
    }

    // Filter by search query
    if (searchQuery.trim()) {
      const q = searchQuery.toLowerCase();
      filtered = filtered.filter(
        (a) => a.title.toLowerCase().includes(q) || a.description.toLowerCase().includes(q)
      );
    }

    // Filter by bookmarks only
    if (showBookmarksOnly) {
      filtered = filtered.filter((a) => feedback.savedArticleIds.includes(a.id));
    }

    return filtered;
  }, [activeEdition, rawArticles, preferences, feedback, selectedTopicFilter, searchQuery, showBookmarksOnly]);

  // Lead, Secondary, and Wire splits
  const leadArticle = rankedArticles[0] || null;
  const secondaryArticles = rankedArticles.slice(1, 5);
  const wireBriefs = rankedArticles.slice(5, 12);
  const sectionRemaining = rankedArticles.slice(12);

  // Group remaining into categorical sections
  const techArticles = useMemo(
    () => rankedArticles.filter((a) => a.category === 'technology' && a.id !== leadArticle?.id),
    [rankedArticles, leadArticle]
  );
  const worldArticles = useMemo(
    () => rankedArticles.filter((a) => a.category === 'world' && a.id !== leadArticle?.id),
    [rankedArticles, leadArticle]
  );
  const businessArticles = useMemo(
    () => rankedArticles.filter((a) => a.category === 'business' && a.id !== leadArticle?.id),
    [rankedArticles, leadArticle]
  );
  const scienceArticles = useMemo(
    () => rankedArticles.filter((a) => a.category === 'science' && a.id !== leadArticle?.id),
    [rankedArticles, leadArticle]
  );
  const otherArticles = useMemo(
    () => rankedArticles.filter((a) => !['technology', 'world', 'business', 'science'].includes(a.category) && a.id !== leadArticle?.id),
    [rankedArticles, leadArticle]
  );

  // Feedback Handlers
  const handleLike = (article: ScoredArticle) => {
    const isLiked = feedback.likedArticleIds.includes(article.id);
    const newLiked = isLiked
      ? feedback.likedArticleIds.filter((id) => id !== article.id)
      : [...feedback.likedArticleIds, article.id];

    // Remove from disliked if present
    const newDisliked = feedback.dislikedArticleIds.filter((id) => id !== article.id);

    // Boost topic affinity
    const currentAffinity = feedback.topicAffinities[article.category] || 0;
    const newTopicAffinities = {
      ...feedback.topicAffinities,
      [article.category]: Math.min(currentAffinity + (isLiked ? -1 : 1.5), 5),
    };

    const updatedFeedback: UserFeedback = {
      ...feedback,
      likedArticleIds: newLiked,
      dislikedArticleIds: newDisliked,
      topicAffinities: newTopicAffinities,
    };

    setFeedback(updatedFeedback);
    saveStoredFeedback(updatedFeedback);
    showToast(isLiked ? 'Removed vote' : `Rank boosted for ${article.category} stories`);
  };

  const handleDislike = (article: ScoredArticle) => {
    const isDisliked = feedback.dislikedArticleIds.includes(article.id);
    const newDisliked = isDisliked
      ? feedback.dislikedArticleIds.filter((id) => id !== article.id)
      : [...feedback.dislikedArticleIds, article.id];

    // Remove from liked if present
    const newLiked = feedback.likedArticleIds.filter((id) => id !== article.id);

    // Decrease topic affinity
    const currentAffinity = feedback.topicAffinities[article.category] || 0;
    const newTopicAffinities = {
      ...feedback.topicAffinities,
      [article.category]: Math.max(currentAffinity + (isDisliked ? 1 : -2), -5),
    };

    const updatedFeedback: UserFeedback = {
      ...feedback,
      likedArticleIds: newLiked,
      dislikedArticleIds: newDisliked,
      topicAffinities: newTopicAffinities,
    };

    setFeedback(updatedFeedback);
    saveStoredFeedback(updatedFeedback);
    showToast(isDisliked ? 'Removed downvote' : `Reduced frequency of ${article.category} stories`);
  };

  const handleSkip = (article: ScoredArticle) => {
    const updatedFeedback: UserFeedback = {
      ...feedback,
      skippedArticleIds: [...feedback.skippedArticleIds, article.id],
    };
    setFeedback(updatedFeedback);
    saveStoredFeedback(updatedFeedback);
    showToast(`Story dismissed from today's paper`);
  };

  const handleBookmark = (article: ScoredArticle) => {
    const isSaved = feedback.savedArticleIds.includes(article.id);
    const newSaved = isSaved
      ? feedback.savedArticleIds.filter((id) => id !== article.id)
      : [...feedback.savedArticleIds, article.id];

    const updatedFeedback: UserFeedback = {
      ...feedback,
      savedArticleIds: newSaved,
    };
    setFeedback(updatedFeedback);
    saveStoredFeedback(updatedFeedback);
    showToast(isSaved ? 'Removed from saved clippings' : 'Saved to personal morning clippings');
  };

  // Preference update
  const handleSavePreferences = (newPrefs: UserPreferences) => {
    setPreferences(newPrefs);
    saveStoredPreferences(newPrefs);
    fetchNews(newPrefs.selectedTopics);
    showToast('Preferences updated and broadsheet re-ranked');
  };

  // Save current edition to archive
  const handleSaveTodayEdition = () => {
    if (rankedArticles.length === 0) {
      showToast('No articles available to archive yet');
      return;
    }
    const todayStr = new Date().toLocaleDateString('en-US', {
      weekday: 'short',
      month: 'short',
      day: 'numeric',
      year: 'numeric',
    });

    const newEdition: SavedEdition = {
      id: 'edition_' + Date.now().toString(36),
      dateStr: todayStr,
      savedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      title: preferences.newspaperTitle,
      leadHeadline: leadArticle ? leadArticle.title : 'Morning Dispatch',
      articlesCount: rankedArticles.length,
      articles: rankedArticles,
      preferencesSnapshot: preferences,
    };

    saveCurrentEdition(newEdition);
    setSavedEditions(getStoredEditions());
    showToast(`Saved today's edition (${newEdition.articlesCount} stories) to Archive!`);
  };

  const handleDeleteEdition = (id: string) => {
    deleteStoredEdition(id);
    setSavedEditions(getStoredEditions());
    if (activeEdition?.id === id) {
      setActiveEdition(null);
    }
    showToast('Archived edition deleted');
  };

  const handleSelectEdition = (edition: SavedEdition) => {
    setActiveEdition(edition);
    showToast(`Loaded archived edition from ${edition.dateStr}`);
  };

  const handleResetToLive = () => {
    setActiveEdition(null);
    showToast('Returned to Live Wire edition');
  };

  // Clean up audio speech on unmount
  useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && 'speechSynthesis' in window) {
        window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
      }
    };
  }, []);

  const handleToggleAudio = () => {
    if (typeof window === 'undefined' || !('speechSynthesis' in window)) {
      showToast('Audio dispatch is not supported in this browser.');
      return;
    }

    if (isPlayingAudio) {
      window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
      setIsPlayingAudio(false);
      showToast('Audio dispatch stopped.');
      return;
    }

    if (rankedArticles.length === 0) {
      showToast('No articles available for audio briefing.');
      return;
    }

    window.speechSynthesis.cancel();

    const topStories = rankedArticles.slice(0, 3);
    const script = [
      `Good morning, ${preferences.readerName || 'Reader'}. Welcome to your personalized edition of ${preferences.newspaperTitle || 'The Morning Chronicle'}.`,
      `Here are your top reports for today.`,
      ...topStories.map((art, idx) => `Report ${idx + 1}, from ${art.source.name}. ${art.title}. ${art.description}`),
      `This concludes your morning audio dispatch. Have a productive day.`
    ].join(' ');

    const utterance = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(script);
    utterance.rate = 1.0;
    utterance.onend = () => setIsPlayingAudio(false);
    utterance.onerror = () => setIsPlayingAudio(false);

    setIsPlayingAudio(true);
    showToast('Playing Morning Audio Briefing...');
    window.speechSynthesis.speak(utterance);
  };

  // Print execution
  const executePrint = () => {
    window.print();
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-paper-50 text-ink-900 flex flex-col font-serif selection:bg-amber-200">
      {/* Toast Notification */}
      {toastMessage && (
        <div className="fixed bottom-6 right-6 z-50 bg-ink-900 text-white text-xs font-sans px-4 py-2.5 rounded shadow-xl flex items-center space-x-2 animate-in fade-in slide-in-from-bottom-2 no-print">
          <Sparkles className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-300" />
          <span>{toastMessage}</span>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Masthead */}
      <Masthead
        preferences={preferences}
        onOpenPreferences={() => setIsOnboardingOpen(true)}
        onOpenEditions={() => setIsEditionsOpen(true)}
        onPrint={() => setIsPrintModalOpen(true)}
        onRefresh={handleRefresh}
        isRefreshing={isRefreshing}
        selectedTopicFilter={selectedTopicFilter}
        onSelectTopicFilter={setSelectedTopicFilter}
        onSaveCurrentEdition={handleSaveTodayEdition}
        savedEditionsCount={savedEditions.length}
        isPlayingAudio={isPlayingAudio}
        onToggleAudio={handleToggleAudio}
      />

      {/* Secondary Search & Filter Bar (Screen only) */}
      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 w-full py-2.5 no-print">
        <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-3 bg-paper-100 p-2.5 rounded border border-paper-200">
          {/* Search Box */}
          <div className="relative w-full sm:w-80">
            <Search className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-ink-400 absolute left-3 top-2.5" />
            <input
              id="search-articles-input"
              type="text"
              value={searchQuery}
              onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Filter stories by topic or keyword..."
              className="w-full pl-8 pr-3 py-1.5 bg-white border border-paper-300 rounded text-xs font-sans text-ink-900 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-ink-900"
            />
            {searchQuery && (
              <button
                onClick={() => setSearchQuery('')}
                className="absolute right-2.5 top-2 text-ink-400 hover:text-ink-900 text-xs"
              >
                ×
              </button>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Quick status / Filter toggles */}
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-3 text-xs font-sans">
            <button
              onClick={() => setShowBookmarksOnly(!showBookmarksOnly)}
              className={`flex items-center space-x-1.5 px-3 py-1 rounded border transition-colors ${
                showBookmarksOnly
                  ? 'bg-amber-100 border-amber-300 text-amber-900 font-semibold'
                  : 'bg-white border-paper-300 text-ink-700 hover:bg-paper-200'
              }`}
            >
              <Bookmark className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-editorial-amber" />
              <span>Saved Clippings ({feedback.savedArticleIds.length})</span>
            </button>

            {activeEdition && (
              <button
                onClick={handleResetToLive}
                className="flex items-center space-x-1 px-2.5 py-1 bg-paper-200 text-editorial-crimson rounded hover:bg-paper-300 font-medium"
              >
                <RotateCcw className="w-3 h-3" />
                <span>Exit Archive</span>
              </button>
            )}

            <span className="text-ink-500 hidden md:inline">
              Showing <strong>{rankedArticles.length}</strong> curated reports
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Main Content Broadsheet */}
      <main className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 w-full flex-1 py-4 print-broadsheet">
        {isLoading ? (
          <LoadingNewspaper />
        ) : error ? (
          <ErrorState error={error} onRetry={() => fetchNews(preferences.selectedTopics)} />
        ) : rankedArticles.length === 0 ? (
          <EmptyState
            onResetFilters={() => {
              setSelectedTopicFilter('all');
              setSearchQuery('');
              setShowBookmarksOnly(false);
            }}
            onOpenPreferences={() => setIsOnboardingOpen(true)}
          />
        ) : (
          <div>
            {/* FRONT PAGE BROADSHEET LAYOUT */}
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-8 pb-8 border-b-2 border-ink-900">
              
              {/* PRIMARY BROADSHEET COLUMN (Cols 1-8) */}
              <div className="lg:col-span-8 lg:border-r lg:border-paper-300 lg:pr-8">
                {/* 1. Lead Story */}
                {leadArticle && (
                  <ArticleCard
                    article={leadArticle}
                    variant="hero"
                    onLike={handleLike}
                    onDislike={handleDislike}
                    onSkip={handleSkip}
                    onBookmark={handleBookmark}
                    onWhyThis={(a) => setWhyThisArticle(a)}
                    isLiked={feedback.likedArticleIds.includes(leadArticle.id)}
                    isDisliked={feedback.dislikedArticleIds.includes(leadArticle.id)}
                    isBookmarked={feedback.savedArticleIds.includes(leadArticle.id)}
                  />
                )}

                {/* 2. Secondary Stories (2-column layout above the fold) */}
                {secondaryArticles.length > 0 && (
                  <div>
                    <div className="flex items-center space-x-2 mb-3">
                      <span className="font-headline font-bold text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wider text-ink-700">
                        Front Page Reports
                      </span>
                      <div className="flex-1 h-px bg-paper-300"></div>
                    </div>
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-5">
                      {secondaryArticles.map((article) => (
                        <ArticleCard
                          key={article.id}
                          article={article}
                          variant="standard"
                          onLike={handleLike}
                          onDislike={handleDislike}
                          onSkip={handleSkip}
                          onBookmark={handleBookmark}
                          onWhyThis={(a) => setWhyThisArticle(a)}
                          isLiked={feedback.likedArticleIds.includes(article.id)}
                          isDisliked={feedback.dislikedArticleIds.includes(article.id)}
                          isBookmarked={feedback.savedArticleIds.includes(article.id)}
                        />
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>

              {/* EDITORIAL SIDEBAR: THE WIRE & FAST DIGEST (Cols 9-12) */}
              <div className="lg:col-span-4 flex flex-col justify-between space-y-6">
                <div>
                  {/* The Wire Header */}
                  <div className="p-3 bg-paper-200 border-2 border-ink-900 rounded mb-4">
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                      <h3 className="font-headline font-black text-sm uppercase tracking-widest text-ink-900">
                        The Morning Wire
                      </h3>
                      <span className="text-[10px] font-mono bg-editorial-crimson text-white px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded font-bold">
                        FAST DISPATCH
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    <p className="text-[11px] font-serif text-ink-600 mt-1 italic">
                      High-priority 2-minute developments filtered for your reading pace.
                    </p>
                  </div>

                  {/* Wire Items */}
                  <div className="space-y-1">
                    {wireBriefs.map((article) => (
                      <ArticleCard
                        key={article.id}
                        article={article}
                        variant="wire"
                        onLike={handleLike}
                        onDislike={handleDislike}
                        onSkip={handleSkip}
                        onBookmark={handleBookmark}
                        onWhyThis={(a) => setWhyThisArticle(a)}
                        isLiked={feedback.likedArticleIds.includes(article.id)}
                        isDisliked={feedback.dislikedArticleIds.includes(article.id)}
                        isBookmarked={feedback.savedArticleIds.includes(article.id)}
                      />
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* Editorial Quote of the Day & Reader Note */}
                <div className="p-4 bg-paper-100 border border-paper-300 rounded font-serif text-xs text-ink-700 italic border-l-4 border-l-editorial-navy">
                  <div className="flex items-center space-x-1.5 font-sans font-bold uppercase text-[10px] text-editorial-navy not-italic mb-1">
                    <Quote className="w-3 h-3" />
                    <span>Editorial Thought</span>
                  </div>
                  &ldquo;An authentic newspaper is not merely a recorder of events, but a lens through which an informed citizen discovers what truly matters.&rdquo;
                </div>
              </div>

            </div>

            {/* CATEGORICAL SECTIONS BELOW THE FOLD */}
            {selectedTopicFilter === 'all' && (
              <div className="mt-8 space-y-10">
                {/* Tech & AI Section */}
                <SectionBlock
                  title="Technology, Computation & Artificial Intelligence"
                  sectionCode="SECTION B"
                  articles={techArticles.slice(0, 3)}
                  onLike={handleLike}
                  onDislike={handleDislike}
                  onSkip={handleSkip}
                  onBookmark={handleBookmark}
                  onWhyThis={(a) => setWhyThisArticle(a)}
                  likedIds={feedback.likedArticleIds}
                  dislikedIds={feedback.dislikedArticleIds}
                  bookmarkedIds={feedback.savedArticleIds}
                />

                {/* World Affairs Section */}
                <SectionBlock
                  title="Global Geopolitics & World Affairs"
                  sectionCode="SECTION C"
                  articles={worldArticles.slice(0, 3)}
                  onLike={handleLike}
                  onDislike={handleDislike}
                  onSkip={handleSkip}
                  onBookmark={handleBookmark}
                  onWhyThis={(a) => setWhyThisArticle(a)}
                  likedIds={feedback.likedArticleIds}
                  dislikedIds={feedback.dislikedArticleIds}
                  bookmarkedIds={feedback.savedArticleIds}
                />

                {/* Business & Economy Section */}
                <SectionBlock
                  title="Markets, Capital & Global Commerce"
                  sectionCode="SECTION D"
                  articles={businessArticles.slice(0, 3)}
                  onLike={handleLike}
                  onDislike={handleDislike}
                  onSkip={handleSkip}
                  onBookmark={handleBookmark}
                  onWhyThis={(a) => setWhyThisArticle(a)}
                  likedIds={feedback.likedArticleIds}
                  dislikedIds={feedback.dislikedArticleIds}
                  bookmarkedIds={feedback.savedArticleIds}
                />

                {/* Science & Nature Section */}
                <SectionBlock
                  title="Science, Cosmos & Earth Systems"
                  sectionCode="SECTION E"
                  articles={scienceArticles.slice(0, 3)}
                  onLike={handleLike}
                  onDislike={handleDislike}
                  onSkip={handleSkip}
                  onBookmark={handleBookmark}
                  onWhyThis={(a) => setWhyThisArticle(a)}
                  likedIds={feedback.likedArticleIds}
                  dislikedIds={feedback.dislikedArticleIds}
                  bookmarkedIds={feedback.savedArticleIds}
                />

                {/* Culture & Opinion Section */}
                {otherArticles.length > 0 && (
                  <SectionBlock
                    title="Culture, Medicine & Editorial Commentary"
                    sectionCode="SECTION F"
                    articles={otherArticles.slice(0, 3)}
                    onLike={handleLike}
                    onDislike={handleDislike}
                    onSkip={handleSkip}
                    onBookmark={handleBookmark}
                    onWhyThis={(a) => setWhyThisArticle(a)}
                    likedIds={feedback.likedArticleIds}
                    dislikedIds={feedback.dislikedArticleIds}
                    bookmarkedIds={feedback.savedArticleIds}
                  />
                )}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        )}
      </main>

      {/* NEWSPAPER COLOPHON & FOOTER */}
      <footer className="w-full bg-paper-100 border-t-2 border-ink-900 py-8 mt-12 print-header">
        <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 text-center">
          <div className="newspaper-rule-double-thin my-2"></div>

          <h4 className="font-headline text-lg font-black text-ink-900 tracking-wider">
            {preferences.newspaperTitle || 'THE MORNING CHRONICLE'}
          </h4>
          <p className="text-xs font-serif text-ink-600 mt-1 max-w-xl mx-auto leading-relaxed">
            Published daily for {preferences.readerName || 'our readers'} • Aggregated and verified via authentic RSS news wires (BBC News, The New York Times, Ars Technica, and NPR).
          </p>

          <div className="mt-4 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-center gap-4 text-[11px] font-sans text-ink-500 uppercase tracking-wider">
            <span className="flex items-center">
              <ShieldCheck className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-editorial-olive mr-1" />
              NaSCon &apos;26 AI Challenge Entry
            </span>
            <span>•</span>
            <span>Zero Hallucination Policy</span>
            <span>•</span>
            <span>Deterministic Scoring Algorithm</span>
            <span>•</span>
            <span>Client-side Storage Enabled</span>
          </div>

          <div className="mt-4 pt-4 border-t border-paper-200 text-[10px] text-ink-400 font-mono no-print">
            Press &ldquo;Print / Save PDF&rdquo; anytime to produce an archival-grade physical or digital broadsheet edition.
          </div>
        </div>
      </footer>

      {/* Onboarding & Preferences Modal */}
      <OnboardingModal
        isOpen={isOnboardingOpen}
        onClose={() => setIsOnboardingOpen(false)}
        preferences={preferences}
        onSave={handleSavePreferences}
        isFirstVisit={!preferences.onboarded}
      />

      {/* Why This Story Modal (Explainability) */}
      <WhyThisModal
        article={whyThisArticle}
        onClose={() => setWhyThisArticle(null)}
        preferences={preferences}
      />

      {/* Saved Editions Archive Drawer */}
      <SavedEditionsDrawer
        isOpen={isEditionsOpen}
        onClose={() => setIsEditionsOpen(false)}
        editions={savedEditions}
        onSelectEdition={handleSelectEdition}
        onDeleteEdition={handleDeleteEdition}
        onSaveCurrent={handleSaveTodayEdition}
        activeEditionId={activeEdition?.id}
        onResetToLive={handleResetToLive}
      />

      {/* Print / Save PDF Modal */}
      <PrintPreviewModal
        isOpen={isPrintModalOpen}
        onClose={() => setIsPrintModalOpen(false)}
        onConfirmPrint={executePrint}
        newspaperTitle={preferences.newspaperTitle}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

'use client';

import React from 'react';
import { ThumbsUp, ThumbsDown, X, Bookmark, ExternalLink, Sparkles, Clock, Calendar } from 'lucide-react';
import { ScoredArticle } from '@/lib/ranking';

interface ArticleCardProps {
  article: ScoredArticle;
  variant?: 'hero' | 'standard' | 'wire';
  onLike: (article: ScoredArticle) => void;
  onDislike: (article: ScoredArticle) => void;
  onSkip: (article: ScoredArticle) => void;
  onBookmark: (article: ScoredArticle) => void;
  onWhyThis: (article: ScoredArticle) => void;
  isLiked?: boolean;
  isDisliked?: boolean;
  isBookmarked?: boolean;
}

export default function ArticleCard({
  article,
  variant = 'standard',
  onLike,
  onDislike,
  onSkip,
  onBookmark,
  onWhyThis,
  isLiked = false,
  isDisliked = false,
  isBookmarked = false,
}: ArticleCardProps) {
  const pubDate = new Date(article.publishedAt);
  const formattedDate = pubDate.toLocaleDateString('en-US', {
    month: 'short',
    day: 'numeric',
  });
  const formattedTime = pubDate.toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' });

  // 1. HERO LEAD STORY VARIANT
  if (variant === 'hero') {
    return (
      <article className="border-b-2 border-ink-900 pb-6 mb-6 print-break-inside-avoid">
        {/* Category & Relevance Badge Bar */}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-sans mb-2 flex-wrap gap-2">
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
            <span className="font-headline font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-editorial-crimson text-[11px] bg-paper-200 px-2 py-0.5 rounded">
              Lead Story • {article.category}
            </span>
            <span className="text-ink-400">•</span>
            <span className="font-semibold text-ink-800">{article.source.name}</span>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
            <button
              onClick={() => onWhyThis(article)}
              className="flex items-center space-x-1 px-2 py-0.5 bg-paper-200 hover:bg-paper-300 rounded text-ink-800 text-[11px] font-sans font-medium transition-colors no-print cursor-pointer"
              title="Click to view why this was ranked for you"
            >
              <Sparkles className="w-3 h-3 text-editorial-amber" />
              <span>{article.relevanceScore}% Match</span>
            </button>
            <span className="text-ink-400 no-print">•</span>
            <span className="flex items-center text-ink-600 text-[11px]">
              <Clock className="w-3 h-3 mr-1" />
              {article.readTimeMinutes} min read
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Lead Headline */}
        <h2 className="text-2xl sm:text-4xl md:text-5xl font-serif font-black text-ink-900 leading-[1.12] mb-3 tracking-tight hover:text-editorial-navy transition-colors">
          <a href={article.url} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">
            {article.title}
          </a>
        </h2>

        {/* Hero Image if available */}
        {article.imageUrl && (
          <div className="my-4 relative overflow-hidden rounded bg-paper-200 border border-paper-300">
            <img
              src={article.imageUrl}
              alt={article.title}
              className="w-full max-h-[380px] object-cover filter contrast-[1.02]"
              loading="eager"
              onError={(e) => {
                // hide on error
                (e.target as HTMLElement).style.display = 'none';
              }}
            />
            <div className="py-1 px-2 text-[10px] text-ink-500 font-sans italic bg-paper-100 border-t border-paper-200 flex justify-between">
              <span>Photo / Illustration via {article.source.name}</span>
              <span>{formattedDate}</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Story Text / Abstract with Drop Cap */}
        <p className="text-base sm:text-lg font-serif text-ink-800 leading-relaxed editorial-dropcap mb-4">
          {article.description}
        </p>

        {/* Action & Feedback Footer */}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between pt-3 border-t border-paper-200 text-xs font-sans flex-wrap gap-2">
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-3 text-ink-500">
            <span className="flex items-center">
              <Calendar className="w-3.5 h-3.5 mr-1" />
              {formattedDate} at {formattedTime}
            </span>
            {article.author && (
              <span className="italic">By {article.author}</span>
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center space-x-2 no-print">
            <button
              onClick={() => onLike(article)}
              className={`p-1.5 rounded transition-colors ${
                isLiked ? 'bg-green-100 text-green-800 font-bold' : 'hover:bg-paper-200 text-ink-600'
              }`}
              title="More stories like this"
            >
              <ThumbsUp className="w-4 h-4" />
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => onDislike(article)}
              className={`p-1.5 rounded transition-colors ${
                isDisliked ? 'bg-red-100 text-red-800 font-bold' : 'hover:bg-paper-200 text-ink-600'
              }`}
              title="Less stories like this"
            >
              <ThumbsDown className="w-4 h-4" />
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => onBookmark(article)}
              className={`p-1.5 rounded transition-colors ${
                isBookmarked ? 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800' : 'hover:bg-paper-200 text-ink-600'
              }`}
              title={isBookmarked ? 'Saved in clippings' : 'Save to clippings'}
            >
              <Bookmark className="w-4 h-4" />
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => onSkip(article)}
              className="p-1.5 rounded hover:bg-paper-200 text-ink-400 hover:text-ink-800 transition-colors"
              title="Skip story (dismiss from today's paper)"
            >
              <X className="w-4 h-4" />
            </button>
            <a
              href={article.url}
              target="_blank"
              rel="noopener noreferrer"
              className="inline-flex items-center space-x-1 px-3 py-1 bg-ink-900 text-white rounded hover:bg-black transition-colors font-medium ml-1"
            >
              <span>Full Report</span>
              <ExternalLink className="w-3 h-3" />
            </a>
          </div>
        </div>
      </article>
    );
  }

  // 2. WIRE / SIDEBAR COMPACT VARIANT
  if (variant === 'wire') {
    return (
      <div className="py-2.5 border-b border-paper-200 last:border-b-0 print-break-inside-avoid">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-[11px] font-sans text-ink-500 mb-1">
          <span className="font-semibold text-editorial-navy">{article.source.name}</span>
          <span className="font-mono text-ink-400">{formattedTime}</span>
        </div>
        <h4 className="text-sm font-serif font-bold text-ink-900 leading-snug hover:text-editorial-crimson transition-colors">
          <a href={article.url} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">
            {article.title}
          </a>
        </h4>
        <p className="text-xs font-serif text-ink-600 line-clamp-2 mt-1 leading-normal">
          {article.description}
        </p>
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mt-2 pt-1 text-[11px] font-sans text-ink-500">
          <button
            onClick={() => onWhyThis(article)}
            className="flex items-center space-x-1 text-editorial-amber hover:underline no-print"
          >
            <Sparkles className="w-2.5 h-2.5" />
            <span>{article.relevanceScore}% match</span>
          </button>
          <a
            href={article.url}
            target="_blank"
            rel="noopener noreferrer"
            className="flex items-center space-x-0.5 text-ink-700 hover:text-ink-900 font-medium"
          >
            <span>Read</span>
            <ExternalLink className="w-2.5 h-2.5" />
          </a>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  // 3. STANDARD ARTICLE CARD (DEFAULT)
  return (
    <article className="border border-paper-300 bg-white/70 hover:bg-white rounded p-4 flex flex-col justify-between transition-shadow hover:shadow-md print-break-inside-avoid">
      <div>
        {/* Source & Meta Bar */}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-sans text-ink-500 mb-2">
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-1.5">
            <span className="font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-[10px] text-editorial-crimson">
              {article.category}
            </span>
            <span>•</span>
            <span className="font-medium text-ink-700">{article.source.name}</span>
          </div>
          <button
            onClick={() => onWhyThis(article)}
            className="flex items-center space-x-1 px-1.5 py-0.5 bg-paper-100 hover:bg-paper-200 text-ink-800 rounded text-[10px] font-sans font-medium transition-colors no-print"
            title="Click to view why this was ranked for you"
          >
            <Sparkles className="w-2.5 h-2.5 text-editorial-amber" />
            <span>{article.relevanceScore}%</span>
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Thumbnail if present */}
        {article.imageUrl && (
          <div className="mb-2.5 overflow-hidden rounded bg-paper-100 border border-paper-200">
            <img
              src={article.imageUrl}
              alt={article.title}
              className="w-full h-36 object-cover hover:scale-105 transition-transform duration-300"
              loading="lazy"
              onError={(e) => {
                (e.target as HTMLElement).style.display = 'none';
              }}
            />
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Title */}
        <h3 className="text-base sm:text-lg font-serif font-bold text-ink-900 leading-snug mb-2 hover:text-editorial-navy transition-colors">
          <a href={article.url} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">
            {article.title}
          </a>
        </h3>

        {/* Summary Snippet */}
        <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-serif text-ink-700 line-clamp-3 leading-relaxed mb-3">
          {article.description}
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* Footer & Actions */}
      <div className="pt-2.5 border-t border-paper-200 flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-sans">
        <div className="flex items-center space-x-2 text-[11px] text-ink-500">
          <span>{formattedDate}</span>
          <span>•</span>
          <span>{article.readTimeMinutes}m read</span>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center space-x-1 no-print">
          <button
            onClick={() => onLike(article)}
            className={`p-1 rounded hover:bg-paper-100 transition-colors ${
              isLiked ? 'text-green-700 font-bold' : 'text-ink-400 hover:text-ink-700'
            }`}
            title="More like this"
          >
            <ThumbsUp className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => onDislike(article)}
            className={`p-1 rounded hover:bg-paper-100 transition-colors ${
              isDisliked ? 'text-red-700 font-bold' : 'text-ink-400 hover:text-ink-700'
            }`}
            title="Less like this"
          >
            <ThumbsDown className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => onBookmark(article)}
            className={`p-1 rounded hover:bg-paper-100 transition-colors ${
              isBookmarked ? 'text-amber-700' : 'text-ink-400 hover:text-ink-700'
            }`}
            title="Save article"
          >
            <Bookmark className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => onSkip(article)}
            className="p-1 rounded hover:bg-paper-100 text-ink-400 hover:text-editorial-crimson transition-colors"
            title="Dismiss article"
          >
            <X className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
          </button>
          <a
            href={article.url}
            target="_blank"

'use client';

import React from 'react';
import { SlidersHorizontal, Printer, Archive, RotateCw, Sparkles, Sun, Newspaper, Volume2, VolumeX } from 'lucide-react';
import { UserPreferences, NewsTopic, AVAILABLE_TOPICS } from '@/types/news';

interface MastheadProps {
  preferences: UserPreferences;
  onOpenPreferences: () => void;
  onOpenEditions: () => void;
  onPrint: () => void;
  onRefresh: () => void;
  isRefreshing: boolean;
  selectedTopicFilter: NewsTopic | 'all';
  onSelectTopicFilter: (topic: NewsTopic | 'all') => void;
  onSaveCurrentEdition: () => void;
  savedEditionsCount: number;
  isPlayingAudio?: boolean;
  onToggleAudio?: () => void;
}

export default function Masthead({
  preferences,
  onOpenPreferences,
  onOpenEditions,
  onPrint,
  onRefresh,
  isRefreshing,
  selectedTopicFilter,
  onSelectTopicFilter,
  onSaveCurrentEdition,
  savedEditionsCount,
  isPlayingAudio = false,
  onToggleAudio,
}: MastheadProps) {
  // Format current date in classic broadsheet style
  const today = new Date();
  const dateFormatted = today.toLocaleDateString('en-US', {
    weekday: 'long',
    month: 'long',
    day: 'numeric',
    year: 'numeric',
  });

  return (
    <header className="w-full pt-4 pb-2 border-b border-paper-300">
      {/* Top Meta Line: Weather & Issue Tagline */}
      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between text-xs text-ink-600 font-sans uppercase tracking-wider pb-2 border-b border-paper-200">
        <div className="flex items-center space-x-3">
          <span className="flex items-center text-amber-800 font-medium">
            <Sun className="w-3.5 h-3.5 mr-1" />
            68°F • Crisp Morning
          </span>
          <span className="text-paper-300">|</span>
          <span>Vol. CXXVI — No. 42</span>
          <span className="text-paper-300">|</span>
          <span className="text-editorial-navy font-semibold">Late City Edition</span>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center space-x-2 mt-1 sm:mt-0">
          <span className="text-ink-500">Edition Prepared For:</span>
          <span className="font-semibold text-ink-900 border-b border-dotted border-ink-600">
            {preferences.readerName || 'Distinguished Reader'}
          </span>
          <span className="bg-editorial-crimson text-white px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-bold tracking-normal">
            LIVE WIRE
          </span>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Main Newspaper Masthead Title */}
      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 py-4 text-center">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-center space-x-4 mb-1">
          <div className="hidden md:block h-px bg-ink-900 flex-1 max-w-[120px]"></div>
          <span className="text-[11px] font-serif uppercase tracking-[0.25em] text-ink-700 italic">
            Veritas et Sapientia • Curated Global Intelligence
          </span>
          <div className="hidden md:block h-px bg-ink-900 flex-1 max-w-[120px]"></div>
        </div>

        <h1 
          id="newspaper-main-title" 
          className="font-headline text-3xl sm:text-5xl md:text-6xl font-black text-ink-900 tracking-tight cursor-pointer hover:opacity-95 transition-opacity"
          onClick={onOpenPreferences}
          title="Click to customize newspaper name and preferences"
        >
          {preferences.newspaperTitle || 'THE MORNING CHRONICLE'}
        </h1>

        <p className="mt-1 text-xs md:text-sm font-serif italic text-ink-600">
          Personalized Digital Morning Broadsheet • Issue of {dateFormatted}
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* Classic Double Rules */}
      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4">
        <div className="newspaper-rule-double my-1"></div>
      </div>

      {/* Controls & Topic Bar (Hidden on print) */}
      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 py-2 no-print">
        <div className="flex flex-col lg:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-3">
          
          {/* Section Navigation Tabs */}
          <nav className="flex items-center flex-wrap gap-1.5 sm:gap-2 text-xs font-sans font-medium" aria-label="Section Navigation">
            <button
              id="filter-all-topics"
              onClick={() => onSelectTopicFilter('all')}
              className={`px-3 py-1 rounded transition-colors ${
                selectedTopicFilter === 'all'
                  ? 'bg-ink-900 text-white font-semibold shadow-sm'
                  : 'bg-paper-100 text-ink-700 hover:bg-paper-200'
              }`}
            >
              Front Page (All)
            </button>
            {AVAILABLE_TOPICS.map((t) => {
              const isSelected = selectedTopicFilter === t.id;
              const isUserInterested = preferences.selectedTopics.includes(t.id);
              return (
                <button
                  key={t.id}
                  id={`filter-${t.id}`}
                  onClick={() => onSelectTopicFilter(t.id)}
                  className={`px-2.5 py-1 rounded transition-colors flex items-center space-x-1 ${
                    isSelected
                      ? 'bg-ink-900 text-white font-semibold shadow-sm'
                      : isUserInterested
                      ? 'bg-paper-200 text-ink-900 hover:bg-paper-300 font-medium'
                      : 'bg-paper-100 text-ink-500 hover:bg-paper-200'
                  }`}
                >
                  <span>{t.label}</span>
                  {isUserInterested && (
                    <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-editorial-crimson" title="In your preferences" />
                  )}
                </button>
              );
            })}
          </nav>

          {/* Action Buttons */}
          <div className="flex items-center flex-wrap gap-2 text-xs font-sans">
            {onToggleAudio && (
              <button
                id="btn-toggle-audio"
                onClick={onToggleAudio}
                className={`flex items-center space-x-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded border transition-colors ${
                  isPlayingAudio
                    ? 'bg-editorial-crimson text-white border-editorial-crimson animate-pulse font-semibold'
                    : 'bg-paper-100 hover:bg-paper-200 text-ink-800 border-paper-300'
                }`}
                title={isPlayingAudio ? 'Stop Audio Dispatch' : 'Listen to Morning Audio Dispatch (Web Speech)'}
              >
                {isPlayingAudio ? (
                  <VolumeX className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-white" />
                ) : (
                  <Volume2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-editorial-crimson" />
                )}
                <span>{isPlayingAudio ? 'Stop Dispatch' : 'Audio Brief'}</span>
              </button>
            )}

            <button
              id="btn-open-preferences"
              onClick={onOpenPreferences}
              className="flex items-center space-x-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 bg-paper-100 hover:bg-paper-200 text-ink-800 rounded border border-paper-300 transition-colors font-medium"
              title="Change news topics and custom interests"
            >
              <SlidersHorizontal className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-editorial-navy" />
              <span>Preferences</span>
            </button>

            <button
              id="btn-refresh-wire"
              onClick={onRefresh}
              disabled={isRefreshing}
              className="flex items-center space-x-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 bg-paper-100 hover:bg-paper-200 text-ink-800 rounded border border-paper-300 transition-colors disabled:opacity-50"
              title="Fetch latest updates from RSS feeds"
            >
              <RotateCw className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 text-ink-600 ${isRefreshing ? 'animate-spin' : ''}`} />
              <span>{isRefreshing ? 'Updating...' : 'Sync Wire'}</span>
            </button>

            <button
              id="btn-save-today-edition"
              onClick={onSaveCurrentEdition}
              className="flex items-center space-x-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 bg-paper-100 hover:bg-paper-200 text-ink-800 rounded border border-paper-300 transition-colors"
              title="Save today's edition to personal archive"
            >
              <Newspaper className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-editorial-amber" />
              <span>Save Today</span>
            </button>

            <button
              id="btn-open-editions-archive"
              onClick={onOpenEditions}
              className="flex items-center space-x-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 bg-paper-100 hover:bg-paper-200 text-ink-800 rounded border border-paper-300 transition-colors"
              title="Browse past saved editions"
            >
              <Archive className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-ink-600" />
              <span>Archive ({savedEditionsCount})</span>
            </button>

            <button
              id="btn-print-newspaper"
              onClick={onPrint}
              className="flex items-center space-x-1.5 px-3.5 py-1.5 bg-ink-900 hover:bg-black text-white rounded shadow-sm transition-all font-medium"
              title="Download clean newspaper PDF via browser print"
            >
              <Printer className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-300" />
              <span>Print / Save PDF</span>
            </button>
          </div>

        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4">
        <div className="newspaper-rule-single my-1"></div>
      </div>
    </header>
  );
}
