FILE: /components/OnboardingModal.tsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
'use client';

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { X, Check, Plus, Sparkles, BookOpen, Clock, Tag } from 'lucide-react';
import { UserPreferences, NewsTopic, AVAILABLE_TOPICS } from '@/types/news';

interface OnboardingModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  preferences: UserPreferences;
  onSave: (newPrefs: UserPreferences) => void;
  isFirstVisit?: boolean;
}

const INTEREST_SUGGESTIONS = [
  'Artificial Intelligence',
  'Space Exploration',
  'Renewable Energy',
  'Quantum Computing',
  'Global Geopolitics',
  'Microchips & Hardware',
  'Biotechnology',
  'Electric Vehicles',
  'Venture Capital',
  'Cybersecurity',
  'Climate Action',
  'Macroeconomics',
];

export default function OnboardingModal({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  preferences,
  onSave,
  isFirstVisit = false,
}: OnboardingModalProps) {
  const [readerName, setReaderName] = useState(preferences.readerName || '');
  const [newspaperTitle, setNewspaperTitle] = useState(preferences.newspaperTitle || 'THE MORNING CHRONICLE');
  const [selectedTopics, setSelectedTopics] = useState<NewsTopic[]>(preferences.selectedTopics || ['technology', 'world']);
  const [customInterests, setCustomInterests] = useState<string[]>(preferences.customInterests || []);
  const [interestInput, setInterestInput] = useState('');
  const [readingPace, setReadingPace] = useState<'quick' | 'standard' | 'deep'>(preferences.readingPace || 'standard');

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const toggleTopic = (topic: NewsTopic) => {
    if (selectedTopics.includes(topic)) {
      if (selectedTopics.length > 1) {
        setSelectedTopics(selectedTopics.filter((t) => t !== topic));
      }
    } else {
      setSelectedTopics([...selectedTopics, topic]);
    }
  };

  const addCustomInterest = (interest: string) => {
    const trimmed = interest.trim();
    if (trimmed && !customInterests.some((i) => i.toLowerCase() === trimmed.toLowerCase())) {
      setCustomInterests([...customInterests, trimmed]);
      setInterestInput('');
    }
  };

  const removeCustomInterest = (interestToRemove: string) => {
    setCustomInterests(customInterests.filter((i) => i !== interestToRemove));
  };

  const handleKeyDownInterest = (e: React.KeyboardEvent) => {
    if (e.key === 'Enter') {
      e.preventDefault();
      addCustomInterest(interestInput);
    }
  };

  const handleSave = () => {
    onSave({
      ...preferences,
      readerName: readerName.trim() || 'Morning Reader',
      newspaperTitle: newspaperTitle.trim() || 'THE MORNING CHRONICLE',
      selectedTopics,
      customInterests,
      readingPace,
      onboarded: true,
    });
    onClose();
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-sm animate-in fade-in duration-200">
      <div 
        className="bg-paper-50 border-2 border-ink-900 rounded-lg shadow-2xl max-w-2xl w-full max-h-[92vh] overflow-y-auto p-6 md:p-8"
        role="dialog"
        aria-modal="true"
        aria-labelledby="onboarding-title"
      >
        {/* Header */}
        <div className="flex items-start justify-between pb-4 border-b border-paper-300">
          <div>
            <div className="flex items-center space-x-2 text-editorial-crimson font-serif font-bold text-xs uppercase tracking-widest">
              <Sparkles className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>{isFirstVisit ? 'Welcome to NaSCon’26 Morning Paper' : 'Personalize Your Broadsheet'}</span>
            </div>
            <h2 id="onboarding-title" className="text-2xl md:text-3xl font-headline font-bold text-ink-900 mt-1">
              Curate Your Morning Edition
            </h2>
            <p className="text-sm font-serif text-ink-600 mt-1">
              Choose your editorial interests and custom topics to shape your personalized daily front page.
            </p>
          </div>
          {!isFirstVisit && (
            <button
              onClick={onClose}
              className="p-1 text-ink-500 hover:text-ink-900 transition-colors"
              aria-label="Close preferences"
            >
              <X className="w-5 h-5" />
            </button>
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-6 py-4">
          {/* Section 1: Masthead Identity */}
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-sans font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-ink-700 mb-1">
                Your Name
              </label>
              <input
                type="text"
                value={readerName}
                onChange={(e) => {
                  setReaderName(e.target.value);
                  if (newspaperTitle === 'THE MORNING CHRONICLE' || newspaperTitle.includes('TIMES')) {
                    setNewspaperTitle(`THE ${e.target.value.toUpperCase() || 'MORNING'} CHRONICLE`);
                  }
                }}
                placeholder="e.g. Eleanor Vance"
                className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-white border border-paper-300 rounded text-sm text-ink-900 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-ink-900 font-serif"
              />
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-sans font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-ink-700 mb-1">
                Newspaper Masthead Name
              </label>
              <input
                type="text"
                value={newspaperTitle}
                onChange={(e) => setNewspaperTitle(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="e.g. THE DAILY DISPATCH"
                className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-white border border-paper-300 rounded text-sm text-ink-900 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-ink-900 font-headline uppercase"
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Section 2: Core News Topics */}
          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-sans font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-ink-700 mb-2">
              Select Editorial Topics (At least 1)
            </label>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-4 gap-2.5">
              {AVAILABLE_TOPICS.map((topic) => {
                const isSelected = selectedTopics.includes(topic.id);
                return (
                  <button
                    key={topic.id}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => toggleTopic(topic.id)}
                    className={`p-2.5 rounded border text-left flex flex-col justify-between transition-all ${
                      isSelected
                        ? 'bg-paper-200 border-ink-900 text-ink-900 shadow-sm'
                        : 'bg-white border-paper-200 text-ink-600 hover:border-paper-300'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between w-full mb-1">
                      <span className="text-xs font-sans font-bold">{topic.label}</span>
                      {isSelected ? (
                        <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-editorial-crimson" />
                      ) : (
                        <div className="w-3.5 h-3.5 rounded-full border border-paper-300" />
                      )}
                    </div>
                    <span className="text-[10px] text-ink-500 line-clamp-1">{topic.description}</span>
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Section 3: Custom Interests & Keyword Boosters */}
          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-sans font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-ink-700 mb-1">
              Custom Topics & High-Priority Keywords
            </label>
            <p className="text-xs text-ink-500 mb-2">
              Add niche interests. Our ranking engine will search titles and abstracts to boost relevant stories.
            </p>

            {/* Input row */}
            <div className="flex gap-2 mb-2.5">
              <input
                type="text"
                value={interestInput}
                onChange={(e) => setInterestInput(e.target.value)}
                onKeyDown={handleKeyDownInterest}
                placeholder="e.g. Fusion Energy, Quantum Computing, Startups..."
                className="flex-1 px-3 py-2 bg-white border border-paper-300 rounded text-sm text-ink-900 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-ink-900 font-serif"
              />
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => addCustomInterest(interestInput)}
                className="px-4 py-2 bg-ink-900 text-white rounded text-xs font-sans font-semibold hover:bg-black transition-colors flex items-center space-x-1"
              >
                <Plus className="w-4 h-4" />
                <span>Add</span>
              </button>
            </div>

            {/* Active custom tags */}
            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5 min-h-[32px] p-2 bg-paper-100 rounded border border-paper-200 mb-2">
              {customInterests.length === 0 ? (
                <span className="text-xs text-ink-400 italic">No custom interests added yet. Click suggestions below or type your own.</span>
              ) : (
                customInterests.map((interest) => (
                  <span
                    key={interest}
                    className="inline-flex items-center space-x-1 px-2.5 py-1 bg-white border border-ink-800 rounded-full text-xs font-sans font-medium text-ink-900 shadow-2xs"
                  >
                    <span>{interest}</span>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => removeCustomInterest(interest)}
                      className="text-ink-400 hover:text-editorial-crimson ml-1"
                    >
                      <X className="w-3 h-3" />
                    </button>
                  </span>
                ))
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Quick Suggestions Chips */}
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5">
              <span className="text-[11px] font-sans text-ink-500 mr-1 flex items-center">
                <Tag className="w-3 h-3 mr-1" /> Suggestions:
              </span>
              {INTEREST_SUGGESTIONS.filter((s) => !customInterests.includes(s)).slice(0, 6).map((s) => (
                <button
                  key={s}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => addCustomInterest(s)}
                  className="text-[11px] font-sans px-2 py-0.5 bg-paper-200 hover:bg-paper-300 text-ink-700 rounded transition-colors"
                >
                  + {s}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Section 4: Reading Pace */}
          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-sans font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-ink-700 mb-2">
              Morning Reading Pace
            </label>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-3">
              {[
                {
                  id: 'quick',
                  label: 'Quick Scan',
                  time: '~5 Minutes',
                  desc: 'Focuses on rapid wire briefs & top bullet points',
                },
                {
                  id: 'standard',
                  label: 'Standard Edition',
                  time: '~15 Minutes',
                  desc: 'A balanced broadsheet with lead stories and analysis',
                },
                {
                  id: 'deep',
                  label: 'Deep Dive',
                  time: '~30 Minutes',
                  desc: 'In-depth reporting, long reads, and commentary',
                },
              ].map((pace) => {
                const isSelected = readingPace === pace.id;
                return (
                  <button
                    key={pace.id}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setReadingPace(pace.id as any)}
                    className={`p-3 rounded border text-left transition-all ${
                      isSelected
                        ? 'bg-paper-200 border-ink-900 text-ink-900 shadow-sm'
                        : 'bg-white border-paper-200 text-ink-600 hover:border-paper-300'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
                      <span className="text-xs font-sans font-bold">{pace.label}</span>
                      <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-editorial-amber">{pace.time}</span>
                    </div>
                    <p className="text-[11px] text-ink-500 font-serif leading-tight">{pace.desc}</p>
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Footer actions */}
        <div className="pt-4 border-t border-paper-300 flex items-center justify-between">
          <span className="text-xs font-serif text-ink-500 italic">
            Ranked deterministically • Preferences saved to your device
          </span>
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-3">
            {!isFirstVisit && (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={onClose}
                className="px-4 py-2 text-xs font-sans font-medium text-ink-600 hover:text-ink-900"
              >
                Cancel
              </button>
            )}
            <button
              id="btn-save-preferences-confirm"
              type="button"
              onClick={handleSave}
              className="px-6 py-2.5 bg-ink-900 hover:bg-black text-white rounded text-sm font-sans font-bold tracking-wide shadow transition-colors flex items-center space-x-2"
            >
              <Sparkles className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-300" />
              <span>{isFirstVisit ? 'Generate My Morning Edition' : 'Update & Re-Rank Newspaper'}</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: /components/SavedEditionsDrawer.tsx

'use client';

import React from 'react';
import { X, Calendar, Newspaper, Trash2, ArrowRight, BookOpen } from 'lucide-react';
import { SavedEdition } from '@/types/news';

interface SavedEditionsDrawerProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  editions: SavedEdition[];
  onSelectEdition: (edition: SavedEdition) => void;
  onDeleteEdition: (id: string) => void;
  onSaveCurrent: () => void;
  activeEditionId?: string | null;
  onResetToLive: () => void;
}

export default function SavedEditionsDrawer({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  editions,
  onSelectEdition,
  onDeleteEdition,
  onSaveCurrent,
  activeEditionId,
  onResetToLive,
}: SavedEditionsDrawerProps) {
  if (!isOpen) return null;

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 overflow-hidden bg-black/50 backdrop-blur-xs flex justify-end animate-in fade-in duration-150">
      <div 
        className="w-full max-w-md bg-paper-50 h-full shadow-2xl border-l-2 border-ink-900 flex flex-col"
        role="dialog"
        aria-modal="true"
        aria-labelledby="archive-drawer-title"
      >
        {/* Drawer Header */}
        <div className="p-5 border-b border-paper-300 flex items-center justify-between bg-paper-100">
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
            <Newspaper className="w-5 h-5 text-editorial-navy" />
            <h3 id="archive-drawer-title" className="font-headline text-lg font-bold text-ink-900">
              Morning Editions Archive
            </h3>
          </div>
          <button
            onClick={onClose}
            className="p-1 text-ink-500 hover:text-ink-900 transition-colors"
            aria-label="Close archive"
          >
            <X className="w-5 h-5" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Current status bar */}
        <div className="px-5 py-3 bg-paper-200 border-b border-paper-300 flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-sans">
          {activeEditionId ? (
            <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
              <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-amber-600 animate-pulse" />
              <span className="font-semibold text-ink-900">Viewing Archived Edition</span>
              <button
                onClick={onResetToLive}
                className="text-editorial-navy underline ml-1 hover:text-editorial-crimson"
              >
                Return to Live Wire
              </button>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
              <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-green-600" />
              <span className="font-medium text-ink-700">Currently viewing Live Edition</span>
            </div>
          )}
          <button
            onClick={onSaveCurrent}
            className="px-2.5 py-1 bg-ink-900 hover:bg-black text-white rounded text-[11px] font-medium transition-colors"
          >
            + Save Today
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Editions List */}
        <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-5 space-y-3">
          {editions.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="text-center py-12 text-ink-500 font-serif">
              <BookOpen className="w-10 h-10 mx-auto text-ink-300 mb-2" />
              <p className="font-bold text-ink-800">No saved morning editions yet</p>
              <p className="text-xs text-ink-500 mt-1 max-w-xs mx-auto">
                Save today&apos;s morning paper to revisit headlines, articles, and your reading list later.
              </p>
              <button
                onClick={onSaveCurrent}
                className="mt-4 px-4 py-2 bg-ink-900 text-white rounded text-xs font-sans font-medium"
              >
                Save Today&apos;s Edition
              </button>
            </div>
          ) : (
            editions.map((ed) => {
              const isSelected = activeEditionId === ed.id;
              return (
                <div
                  key={ed.id}
                  className={`p-3.5 rounded border transition-all ${
                    isSelected
                      ? 'bg-paper-200 border-ink-900 shadow-sm ring-1 ring-ink-900'
                      : 'bg-white border-paper-300 hover:border-ink-700'
                  }`}
                >
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-[11px] font-sans text-ink-500 mb-1">
                    <span className="flex items-center font-medium text-editorial-crimson">
                      <Calendar className="w-3 h-3 mr-1" />
                      {ed.dateStr}
                    </span>
                    <span>{ed.articlesCount} articles</span>
                  </div>

                  <h4 className="font-serif font-bold text-sm text-ink-900 line-clamp-2 leading-snug mb-1">
                    {ed.leadHeadline || 'Morning Edition Headlines'}
                  </h4>

                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between pt-2 border-t border-paper-100 text-xs font-sans mt-2">
                    <button
                      onClick={() => {
                        onSelectEdition(ed);
                        onClose();
                      }}
                      className="inline-flex items-center space-x-1 text-editorial-navy font-semibold hover:underline"
                    >
                      <span>{isSelected ? 'Viewing' : 'Read Edition'}</span>
                      <ArrowRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                    </button>

                    <button
                      onClick={() => onDeleteEdition(ed.id)}
                      className="text-ink-400 hover:text-red-700 p-1 transition-colors"
                      title="Delete saved edition"
                    >
                      <Trash2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              );
            })
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Footer */}
        <div className="p-4 border-t border-paper-300 bg-paper-100 text-center text-xs font-serif text-ink-500">
          Saved offline to browser storage
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: /components/SectionBlock.tsx

'use client';

import React from 'react';
import { ScoredArticle } from '@/lib/ranking';
import ArticleCard from './ArticleCard';

interface SectionBlockProps {
  title: string;
  sectionCode?: string;
  articles: ScoredArticle[];
  onLike: (article: ScoredArticle) => void;
  onDislike: (article: ScoredArticle) => void;
  onSkip: (article: ScoredArticle) => void;
  onBookmark: (article: ScoredArticle) => void;
  onWhyThis: (article: ScoredArticle) => void;
  likedIds: string[];
  dislikedIds: string[];
  bookmarkedIds: string[];
}

export default function SectionBlock({
  title,
  sectionCode = 'SECTION',
  articles,
  onLike,
  onDislike,
  onSkip,
  onBookmark,
  onWhyThis,
  likedIds,
  dislikedIds,
  bookmarkedIds,
}: SectionBlockProps) {
  if (articles.length === 0) return null;

  return (
    <section className="my-8 print-break-inside-avoid">
      {/* Section Header Rule */}
      <div className="flex items-center space-x-3 mb-4">
        <span className="font-headline font-black text-xs uppercase tracking-widest text-editorial-crimson bg-paper-200 px-2 py-0.5 rounded border border-paper-300">
          {sectionCode}
        </span>
        <h3 className="font-headline text-lg sm:text-xl font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-ink-900">
          {title}
        </h3>
        <div className="flex-1 h-px bg-ink-900"></div>
      </div>

      {/* Grid of Section Articles */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-5">
        {articles.map((article) => (
          <ArticleCard
            key={article.id}
            article={article}
            variant="standard"
            onLike={onLike}
            onDislike={onDislike}
            onSkip={onSkip}
            onBookmark={onBookmark}
            onWhyThis={onWhyThis}
            isLiked={likedIds.includes(article.id)}
            isDisliked={dislikedIds.includes(article.id)}
            isBookmarked={bookmarkedIds.includes(article.id)}
          />
        ))}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

interface EmptyStateProps {
  onResetFilters: () => void;
  onOpenPreferences: () => void;
}

export function EmptyState({ onResetFilters, onOpenPreferences }: EmptyStateProps) {
  return (
    <div className="max-w-md mx-auto my-16 p-8 text-center bg-white/60 border border-paper-300 rounded-lg shadow-sm">
      <FilterX className="w-12 h-12 mx-auto text-ink-400 mb-3" />
      <h3 className="font-headline text-xl font-bold text-ink-900 mb-2">
        No Stories In This Section
      </h3>
      <p className="font-serif text-xs text-ink-600 mb-5 leading-relaxed">
        All stories for this filter may have been read or skipped, or your custom interest filters might be overly specific.
      </p>
      <div className="flex items-center justify-center space-x-3">
        <button
          onClick={onResetFilters}
          className="px-4 py-2 bg-paper-200 hover:bg-paper-300 text-ink-800 rounded text-xs font-sans font-medium transition-colors"
        >
          View All Topics
        </button>
        <button
          onClick={onOpenPreferences}
          className="px-4 py-2 bg-ink-900 hover:bg-black text-white rounded text-xs font-sans font-medium transition-colors"
        >
          Curate Interests
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

interface ErrorStateProps {
  error: string;
  onRetry: () => void;
}

export function ErrorState({ error, onRetry }: ErrorStateProps) {
  return (
    <div className="max-w-md mx-auto my-16 p-8 text-center bg-red-50/70 border border-red-200 rounded-lg shadow-sm">
      <AlertTriangle className="w-12 h-12 mx-auto text-editorial-crimson mb-3" />
      <h3 className="font-headline text-xl font-bold text-ink-900 mb-2">
        Morning Wire Interruption
      </h3>
      <p className="font-serif text-xs text-ink-700 mb-5 leading-relaxed">
        {error || 'Unable to retrieve live public feeds at this moment. The wire service might be undergoing maintenance.'}
      </p>
      <button
        onClick={onRetry}
        className="px-5 py-2.5 bg-editorial-crimson hover:bg-red-900 text-white rounded text-xs font-sans font-semibold transition-colors inline-flex items-center space-x-2"
      >
        <RotateCw className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
        <span>Reconnect Wire Service</span>
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}

'use client';

import React from 'react';
import { X, Sparkles, CheckCircle2, Clock, ThumbsUp, Compass, Target } from 'lucide-react';
import { ScoredArticle } from '@/lib/ranking';

interface WhyThisModalProps {
  article: ScoredArticle | null;
  onClose: () => void;
  preferences: UserPreferences;
}

export default function WhyThisModal({ article, onClose, preferences }: WhyThisModalProps) {
  if (!article) return null;

  const breakdown = article.scoreBreakdown || {
    topicScore: 20,
    keywordScore: 16,
    recencyScore: 16,
    paceScore: 10,
    feedbackScore: 0,
    total: article.relevanceScore || 85,
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-sm animate-in fade-in duration-150">
      <div 
        className="bg-paper-50 border-2 border-ink-900 rounded-lg shadow-2xl max-w-lg w-full p-6 md:p-7"

            </span>
            <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-ink-500 block uppercase">Match Quality</span>

            <span className="font-mono font-bold text-ink-900">+{breakdown.topicScore} pts</span>
          </div>

          {/* Keywords Match */}

              <Target className="w-4 h-4 text-editorial-crimson" />

                    ? `Matched: "${article.matchedKeywords.join('", "')}"`
                    : 'No specific keyword match; broad topic relevancy'}
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>
            <span className="font-mono font-bold text-ink-900">+{breakdown.keywordScore} pts</span>
          </div>

          {/* Recency */}
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
              <Clock className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-700" />
              <div>
                <span className="font-semibold text-ink-900">Morning Freshness (Recency)</span>
                <span className="text-[11px] text-ink-500 block">
                  Published {new Date(article.publishedAt).toLocaleDateString()} at{' '}
                  {new Date(article.publishedAt).toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' })}
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>
            <span className="font-mono font-bold text-ink-900">+{breakdown.recencyScore} pts</span>
          </div>

          {/* Pace Alignment */}
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
              <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-editorial-olive" />

                <span className="font-semibold text-ink-900">Reading Pace Alignment</span>
                <span className="text-[11px] text-ink-500 block">
                  {article.readTimeMinutes} min read fits your &quot;{preferences.readingPace}&quot; pacing
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>
            <span className="font-mono font-bold text-ink-900">+{breakdown.paceScore} pts</span>
          </div>

          {/* Feedback adjustment */}
          {breakdown.feedbackScore !== 0 && (
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
                <ThumbsUp className="w-4 h-4 text-ink-700" />
                <div>
                  <span className="font-semibold text-ink-900">User Interaction Affinity</span>
                  <span className="text-[11px] text-ink-500 block">Feedback from your previous votes</span>
                </div>
              </div>
              <span className={`font-mono font-bold ${breakdown.feedbackScore > 0 ? 'text-green-700' : 'text-red-700'}`}>
                {breakdown.feedbackScore > 0 ? `+${breakdown.feedbackScore}` : breakdown.feedbackScore} pts
              </span>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Footer */}
        <div className="pt-4 border-t border-paper-300 mt-2 flex justify-end">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            className="px-4 py-2 bg-ink-900 text-white rounded text-xs font-sans font-medium hover:bg-black transition-colors"
          >
            Understood
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

# System Architecture: The Morning Chronicle

## Architectural Overview

The Morning Chronicle is designed as a zero-dependency, server-aggregated editorial broadsheet built with Next.js App Router, TypeScript, and Tailwind CSS.

```mermaid
graph TD
    subgraph External Sources [Public RSS News Wires]
        BBC[BBC News RSS<br/>World, Tech, Business, Science, Health]
        NYT[New York Times RSS<br/>World, Tech, Science, Business, Climate]
        ARS[Ars Technica & NPR RSS<br/>Technology, Culture, Wire]
    end

    subgraph Server Layer [Next.js App Router Server]
        API["/api/news<br/>Route Handler"]
        FETCHER["lib/rss.ts<br/>Parallel Multi-Feed Fetcher<br/>Timeout & Graceful Fallbacks"]
        PARSER["fast-xml-parser<br/>Normalizer, Thumbnail Extractor,<br/>HTML Sanitizer"]
    end

    subgraph Client Layer [Next.js Client Application]
        STORE["lib/storage.ts<br/>LocalStorage Persistence<br/>(Preferences, Feedback, Editions)"]
        RANKER["lib/ranking.ts<br/>Explainable Scoring Engine<br/>Topic Match + Custom Keywords + Recency + Feedback"]
        PAGE["app/page.tsx<br/>Editorial Controller & BroadSheet Layout"]
        
        subgraph UI Components
            MAST["components/Masthead.tsx<br/>Woodblock Title, Date, Weather, Controls"]
            HERO["Lead Story (Hero Variant)<br/>Large Headline, Drop Cap, Actions"]
            WIRE["The Morning Wire<br/>Compact 2-min briefs sidebar"]
            SECTIONS["Categorical Section Blocks<br/>Tech, World, Business, Science"]
            MODAL_ONB["components/OnboardingModal.tsx<br/>Topic & Interest Curator"]
            MODAL_WHY["components/WhyThisModal.tsx<br/>Transparent Explainability Popover"]
            DRAWER_ARCH["components/SavedEditionsDrawer.tsx<br/>Archive & Restore Past Editions"]
            MODAL_PRINT["components/PrintPreviewBanner.tsx<br/>Print Dialog & Save as PDF Engine"]
        end
    end

    External Sources --> FETCHER
    FETCHER --> PARSER
    PARSER --> API
    API -->|Aggregated Articles| PAGE
    STORE -->|Preferences & Feedback| RANKER
    PAGE --> RANKER
    RANKER -->|Ranked & Scored Articles| HERO
    RANKER --> WIRE
    RANKER --> SECTIONS
    PAGE --> MODAL_ONB
    PAGE --> MODAL_WHY
    PAGE --> DRAWER_ARCH
    PAGE --> MODAL_PRINT
```

---

## Data Flow & Core Subsystems

### 1. Server-Side Wire Aggregation (`lib/rss.ts`, `/api/news`)
- Requests are dispatched in parallel to multiple public XML feeds using `Promise.allSettled`.
- Each feed has an independent `AbortController` timeout (6000ms), ensuring high resiliency: even if one network source hangs, the remaining sources compile seamlessly.
- XML parsing extracts article titles, descriptions, publication timestamps, authors, source metadata, and thumbnail images across `<media:content>`, `<media:thumbnail>`, and `<enclosure>`.
- Articles are deduplicated across URLs and titles and cached server-side.

### 2. Explainable Ranking Engine (`lib/ranking.ts`)
Each article is scored on a 0–99 scale via an explainable multi-factor formula:
$$\text{Score} = \text{TopicMatch} + \text{CustomInterests} + \text{Recency} + \text{Pacing} + \text{Feedback}$$

- **Topic Alignment (0–30 pts)**: Base +20 pts for user's subscribed categories + user dynamic topic affinity (-12.5 to +12.5).
- **Custom Interests Match (0–35 pts)**: Searches title and abstract for user's custom keywords and multi-token interests (+16 pts per exact phrase match, +8 pts per token match).
- **Recency Decay (0–20 pts)**:
  - $< 3\text{ hours}$: +20 pts
  - $3-6\text{ hours}$: +16 pts
  - $6-12\text{ hours}$: +12 pts
  - $12-24\text{ hours}$: +8 pts
- **Reading Pace (0–10 pts)**: Matches article length with user's reading style (Quick Scan vs Standard vs Deep Dive).
- **Feedback Adjustments**: Upvoted stories (+20 pts), downvoted stories (-45 pts), dismissed stories filtered out.

### 3. Client-Side Persistence (`lib/storage.ts`)
- Zero external database required for demo or hackathon judging.
- Fully stored in browser `localStorage`:
  - `morning_chronicle_preferences`: User name, custom title, topics, custom interest tags, reading pace.
  - `morning_chronicle_feedback`: Liked IDs, disliked IDs, skipped IDs, saved clipping IDs, dynamic topic affinities.
  - `morning_chronicle_saved_editions`: Array of historical edition snapshots with full article payloads.

### 4. Print & Broadsheet Rendering Engine (`app/globals.css`)
- Uses CSS `@media print` rules:
  - Suppresses all interactive controls, buttons, toolbars, and modals (`.no-print`).
  - Sets up authentic newspaper multi-column layouts with classic editorial typography (`font-serif`, `newspaper-rule-double`).
  - Enforces clean page breaks (`break-inside: avoid`).


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /lib/ranking.ts
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import { Article, UserPreferences, UserFeedback } from '@/types/news';

export interface ScoredArticle extends Article {
  relevanceScore: number;
  rankingReason: string;
  matchedKeywords: string[];
  scoreBreakdown: {
    topicScore: number;
    keywordScore: number;
    recencyScore: number;
    paceScore: number;
    feedbackScore: number;
    total: number;
  };
}

export function rankArticles(
  articles: Article[],
  preferences: UserPreferences,
  feedback: UserFeedback,
  includeSkipped: boolean = false
): ScoredArticle[] {
  const scored = articles
    .filter((art) => {
      if (!includeSkipped && feedback.skippedArticleIds.includes(art.id)) {
        return false;
      }
      return true;
    })
    .map((article) => {
      let topicScore = 0;
      let keywordScore = 0;
      let recencyScore = 0;
      let paceScore = 0;
      let feedbackScore = 0;
      const matchedKeywords: string[] = [];
      const reasons: string[] = [];

      const textToSearch = `${article.title} ${article.description}`.toLowerCase();

      // 1. Topic Score (Base 0 - 30)
      const isSelectedTopic = preferences.selectedTopics.includes(article.category);
      if (isSelectedTopic) {
        topicScore += 20;
        reasons.push(`Selected topic (${article.category})`);
      } else {
        topicScore += 5; // general discovery
      }

      // Add dynamic topic affinity from feedback
      const affinity = feedback.topicAffinities[article.category] || 0;
      const topicAffinityScore = affinity * 2.5; // -12.5 to +12.5
      topicScore += topicAffinityScore;
      if (affinity > 0) {
        reasons.push(`High engagement with ${article.category}`);
      }

      // 2. Custom Interests Match (0 - 35)
      for (const interest of preferences.customInterests) {
        const cleanInterest = interest.trim().toLowerCase();
        if (!cleanInterest) continue;

        // Exact phrase match
        if (textToSearch.includes(cleanInterest)) {
          keywordScore += 16;
          matchedKeywords.push(interest);
        } else {
          // Token matches
          const tokens = cleanInterest.split(/\s+/).filter((t) => t.length > 3);
          const hasTokenMatch = tokens.some((token) => textToSearch.includes(token));
          if (hasTokenMatch) {
            keywordScore += 8;
            matchedKeywords.push(interest);
          }
        }
      }

      // Keyword affinities from feedback
      for (const [kw, aff] of Object.entries(feedback.keywordAffinities)) {
        if (textToSearch.includes(kw.toLowerCase())) {
          keywordScore += aff * 3;
        }
      }
      keywordScore = Math.min(Math.max(keywordScore, 0), 35);
      if (matchedKeywords.length > 0) {
        reasons.push(`Matches interest: "${matchedKeywords.slice(0, 2).join('", "')}"`);
      }

      // 3. Recency Score (0 - 20)
      const pubDate = new Date(article.publishedAt);
      const hoursAgo = Math.max(0, (Date.now() - pubDate.getTime()) / (1000 * 60 * 60));
      if (hoursAgo <= 3) {
        recencyScore = 20;
        reasons.push('Just in (under 3h ago)');
      } else if (hoursAgo <= 6) {
        recencyScore = 16;
        reasons.push('Fresh (under 6h ago)');
      } else if (hoursAgo <= 12) {
        recencyScore = 12;
      } else if (hoursAgo <= 24) {
        recencyScore = 8;
      } else {
        recencyScore = 4;
      }

      // 4. Reading Pace Alignment (0 - 10)
      if (preferences.readingPace === 'quick') {
        paceScore = article.readTimeMinutes <= 3 ? 10 : article.readTimeMinutes <= 5 ? 6 : 2;
      } else if (preferences.readingPace === 'deep') {
        paceScore = article.readTimeMinutes >= 5 ? 10 : 5;
      } else {
        // standard
        paceScore = article.readTimeMinutes >= 3 && article.readTimeMinutes <= 6 ? 10 : 6;
      }

      // 5. Direct Feedback (Likes / Dislikes)
      if (feedback.likedArticleIds.includes(article.id)) {
        feedbackScore += 20;
        reasons.push('You liked this');
      }
      if (feedback.dislikedArticleIds.includes(article.id)) {
        feedbackScore -= 45;
        reasons.push('Previously downvoted');
      }

      const totalRaw = topicScore + keywordScore + recencyScore + paceScore + feedbackScore;
      // Clamp 5 to 99 for a polished percentage display
      const total = Math.min(Math.max(Math.round(totalRaw), 5), 99);

      let rankingReason = '';
      if (reasons.length > 0) {
        rankingReason = reasons.slice(0, 2).join(' • ');
      } else {
        rankingReason = 'Curated general interest from morning wire';
      }

      return {
        ...article,
        relevanceScore: total,
        rankingReason,
        matchedKeywords: Array.from(new Set(matchedKeywords)),
        scoreBreakdown: {
          topicScore: Math.round(topicScore),
          keywordScore: Math.round(keywordScore),
          recencyScore: Math.round(recencyScore),
          paceScore: Math.round(paceScore),
          feedbackScore: Math.round(feedbackScore),
          total,
        },
      };
    });

  // Sort by relevance score descending
  return scored.sort((a, b) => (b.relevanceScore || 0) - (a.relevanceScore || 0));
}

import { XMLParser } from 'fast-xml-parser';
import { Article, NewsTopic } from '@/types/news';

interface FeedSource {
  name: string;
  url: string;
  category: NewsTopic;
  domain: string;
}

const FEED_SOURCES: FeedSource[] = [
  // Technology
  { name: 'BBC News', url: 'http://feeds.bbci.co.uk/news/technology/rss.xml', category: 'technology', domain: 'bbc.com' },
  { name: 'Ars Technica', url: 'https://feeds.arstechnica.com/arstechnica/index', category: 'technology', domain: 'arstechnica.com' },
  { name: 'NYT Technology', url: 'https://rss.nytimes.com/services/xml/rss/nyt/Technology.xml', category: 'technology', domain: 'nytimes.com' },
  
  // World Affairs
  { name: 'BBC World', url: 'http://feeds.bbci.co.uk/news/world/rss.xml', category: 'world', domain: 'bbc.com' },
  { name: 'NYT World', url: 'https://rss.nytimes.com/services/xml/rss/nyt/World.xml', category: 'world', domain: 'nytimes.com' },
  { name: 'NPR News', url: 'https://feeds.npr.org/1004/rss.xml', category: 'world', domain: 'npr.org' },

  // Business & Markets
  { name: 'BBC Business', url: 'http://feeds.bbci.co.uk/news/business/rss.xml', category: 'business', domain: 'bbc.com' },
  { name: 'NYT Business', url: 'https://rss.nytimes.com/services/xml/rss/nyt/Business.xml', category: 'business', domain: 'nytimes.com' },

  // Science & Discovery
  { name: 'BBC Science', url: 'http://feeds.bbci.co.uk/news/science_and_environment/rss.xml', category: 'science', domain: 'bbc.com' },
  { name: 'NYT Science', url: 'https://rss.nytimes.com/services/xml/rss/nyt/Science.xml', category: 'science', domain: 'nytimes.com' },

  // Arts & Culture
  { name: 'NYT Arts', url: 'https://rss.nytimes.com/services/xml/rss/nyt/Arts.xml', category: 'culture', domain: 'nytimes.com' },
  { name: 'BBC Arts', url: 'http://feeds.bbci.co.uk/news/entertainment_and_arts/rss.xml', category: 'culture', domain: 'bbc.com' },

  // Health
  { name: 'NYT Health', url: 'https://rss.nytimes.com/services/xml/rss/nyt/Health.xml', category: 'health', domain: 'nytimes.com' },
  { name: 'BBC Health', url: 'http://feeds.bbci.co.uk/news/health/rss.xml', category: 'health', domain: 'bbc.com' },

  // Climate / Environment
  { name: 'NYT Climate', url: 'https://rss.nytimes.com/services/xml/rss/nyt/Climate.xml', category: 'environment', domain: 'nytimes.com' },

  // Opinion / Ideas
  { name: 'NYT Opinion', url: 'https://rss.nytimes.com/services/xml/rss/nyt/Opinion.xml', category: 'opinion', domain: 'nytimes.com' },
];

const parser = new XMLParser({
  ignoreAttributes: false,
  attributeNamePrefix: '@_',
  textNodeName: '#text',
  trimValues: true,
});

function stripHtml(html: string): string {
  if (!html) return '';
  return html
    .replace(/<!\[CDATA\[([\s\S]*?)\]\]>/gi, '$1')
    .replace(/<[^>]*>/g, '')
    .replace(/&amp;/g, '&')
    .replace(/&lt;/g, '<')
    .replace(/&gt;/g, '>')
    .replace(/&quot;/g, '"')
    .replace(/&#39;/g, "'")
    .replace(/&nbsp;/g, ' ')
    .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
    .trim();
}

function extractImage(item: any): string | undefined {
  // 1. Check media:content (could be array or single object)
  const mediaContent = item['media:content'];
  if (Array.isArray(mediaContent)) {
    const img = mediaContent.find((m) => m['@_medium'] === 'image' || m['@_url']);
    if (img && img['@_url']) return img['@_url'];
  } else if (mediaContent && mediaContent['@_url']) {
    return mediaContent['@_url'];
  }

  // 2. Check media:thumbnail
  const mediaThumb = item['media:thumbnail'];
  if (Array.isArray(mediaThumb)) {
    if (mediaThumb[0] && mediaThumb[0]['@_url']) return mediaThumb[0]['@_url'];
  } else if (mediaThumb && mediaThumb['@_url']) {
    return mediaThumb['@_url'];
  }

  // 3. Check enclosure
  const enclosure = item.enclosure;
  if (enclosure && enclosure['@_url'] && enclosure['@_type']?.startsWith('image/')) {
    return enclosure['@_url'];
  }

  // 4. Check HTML in description or content:encoded
  const htmlContent = item['content:encoded'] || item.description || '';
  if (typeof htmlContent === 'string') {
    const match = htmlContent.match(/<img[^>]+src=["']([^"']+)["']/i);
    if (match && match[1]) {
      return match[1];
    }
  }

  return undefined;
}

function generateSimpleId(str: string): string {
  let hash = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < str.length; i++) {
    hash = (hash << 5) - hash + str.charCodeAt(i);
    hash |= 0;
  }
  return 'art_' + Math.abs(hash).toString(36);
}

function estimateReadTime(text: string): number {
  const words = text.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean).length;
  // Estimate assuming full article is about 4x the summary
  const estimatedFullWords = Math.max(words * 4, 300);
  const minutes = Math.ceil(estimatedFullWords / 200); // 200 words per minute
  return Math.min(Math.max(minutes, 2), 10);
}

export async function fetchFeedArticles(source: FeedSource): Promise<Article[]> {
  const controller = new AbortController();
  const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 6000);

  try {
    const res = await fetch(source.url, {
      signal: controller.signal,
      headers: {
        'User-Agent': 'Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) MorningPaper/1.0',
        'Accept': 'application/rss+xml, application/xml, text/xml, */*',
      },
      next: { revalidate: 300 }, // Cache server-side for 5 minutes
    });

    clearTimeout(timeoutId);

    if (!res.ok) {
      console.warn(`[RSS] Failed to fetch ${source.name}: ${res.status}`);
      return [];
    }

    const xmlText = await res.text();
    const parsed = parser.parse(xmlText);

    let rawItems: any[] = [];
    if (parsed.rss && parsed.rss.channel && parsed.rss.channel.item) {
      rawItems = Array.isArray(parsed.rss.channel.item) ? parsed.rss.channel.item : [parsed.rss.channel.item];
    } else if (parsed.feed && parsed.feed.entry) {
      rawItems = Array.isArray(parsed.feed.entry) ? parsed.feed.entry : [parsed.feed.entry];
    }

    const articles: Article[] = [];

    for (const item of rawItems) {
      const title = stripHtml(typeof item.title === 'string' ? item.title : item.title?.['#text'] || '');
      const rawDesc = item.description || item.summary || item['content:encoded'] || '';
      const description = stripHtml(typeof rawDesc === 'string' ? rawDesc : rawDesc?.['#text'] || '');

      let link = '';
      if (typeof item.link === 'string') {
        link = item.link;
      } else if (item.link && item.link['@_href']) {
        link = item.link['@_href'];
      } else if (Array.isArray(item.link)) {
        const alt = item.link.find((l: any) => l['@_rel'] === 'alternate' || !l['@_rel']);
        link = alt ? alt['@_href'] : item.link[0]?.['@_href'] || '';
      }

      if (!title || !link) continue;

      const publishedAt = item.pubDate || item.published || item.updated || new Date().toISOString();
      const imageUrl = extractImage(item);
      const author = item['dc:creator'] || item.author?.name || undefined;
      const readTime = estimateReadTime(description);

      articles.push({
        id: generateSimpleId(link + title),
        title,
        description: description || 'No summary provided. Tap to read the full report.',
        url: link,
        source: {
          name: source.name,
          domain: source.domain,
        },
        category: source.category,
        publishedAt: new Date(publishedAt).toISOString(),
        imageUrl,
        author: author ? stripHtml(author) : undefined,
        readTimeMinutes: readTime,
      });
    }

    return articles.slice(0, 15); // Take top 15 from each feed
  } catch (error: any) {
    clearTimeout(timeoutId);
    console.warn(`[RSS Error] ${source.name}: ${error?.message || error}`);
    return [];
  }
}

export async function fetchAllNews(selectedTopics?: NewsTopic[]): Promise<Article[]> {
  const sourcesToFetch = selectedTopics && selectedTopics.length > 0
    ? FEED_SOURCES.filter((s) => selectedTopics.includes(s.category))
    : FEED_SOURCES;

  // Run in parallel with Promise.allSettled
  const results = await Promise.allSettled(sourcesToFetch.map((src) => fetchFeedArticles(src)));

  const allArticles: Article[] = [];
  const seenUrls = new Set<string>();

  for (const res of results) {
    if (res.status === 'fulfilled') {
      for (const article of res.value) {
        // Deduplicate
        const cleanUrl = article.url.split('?')[0].toLowerCase();
        if (!seenUrls.has(cleanUrl) && !seenUrls.has(article.title.toLowerCase())) {
          seenUrls.add(cleanUrl);
          seenUrls.add(article.title.toLowerCase());
          allArticles.push(article);
        }
      }
    }
  }

  // Sort initially by publication date descending
  return allArticles.sort((a, b) => new Date(b.publishedAt).getTime() - new Date(a.publishedAt).getTime());
}

FILE: /lib/storage.ts

import { UserPreferences, UserFeedback, SavedEdition, DEFAULT_PREFERENCES, Article } from '@/types/news';

const PREFS_KEY = 'morning_chronicle_preferences';
const FEEDBACK_KEY = 'morning_chronicle_feedback';
const EDITIONS_KEY = 'morning_chronicle_saved_editions';

export const INITIAL_FEEDBACK: UserFeedback = {
  likedArticleIds: [],
  dislikedArticleIds: [],
  skippedArticleIds: [],
  savedArticleIds: [],
  topicAffinities: {},
  keywordAffinities: {},
};

export function getStoredPreferences(): UserPreferences {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return DEFAULT_PREFERENCES;
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(PREFS_KEY);
    if (!raw) return DEFAULT_PREFERENCES;
    return { ...DEFAULT_PREFERENCES, ...JSON.parse(raw) };
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('Failed to load preferences from storage:', e);
    return DEFAULT_PREFERENCES;
  }
}

export function saveStoredPreferences(prefs: UserPreferences): void {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return;
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(PREFS_KEY, JSON.stringify(prefs));
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('Failed to save preferences to storage:', e);
  }
}

export function getStoredFeedback(): UserFeedback {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return INITIAL_FEEDBACK;
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(FEEDBACK_KEY);
    if (!raw) return INITIAL_FEEDBACK;
    return { ...INITIAL_FEEDBACK, ...JSON.parse(raw) };
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('Failed to load feedback from storage:', e);
    return INITIAL_FEEDBACK;
  }
}

export function saveStoredFeedback(feedback: UserFeedback): void {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return;
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(FEEDBACK_KEY, JSON.stringify(feedback));
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('Failed to save feedback to storage:', e);
  }
}

export function getStoredEditions(): SavedEdition[] {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return [];
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(EDITIONS_KEY);
    if (!raw) return [];
    return JSON.parse(raw);
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('Failed to load saved editions:', e);
    return [];
  }
}

export function saveCurrentEdition(edition: SavedEdition): void {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return;
  try {
    const existing = getStoredEditions();
    // Keep max 20 saved editions
    const filtered = existing.filter((e) => e.id !== edition.id);
    const updated = [edition, ...filtered].slice(0, 20);
    localStorage.setItem(EDITIONS_KEY, JSON.stringify(updated));
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('Failed to save edition to storage:', e);
  }
}

export function deleteStoredEdition(editionId: string): void {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return;
  try {
    const existing = getStoredEditions();
    const updated = existing.filter((e) => e.id !== editionId);
    localStorage.setItem(EDITIONS_KEY, JSON.stringify(updated));
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('Failed to delete edition from storage:', e);
  }
}

export type NewsTopic = 
  | 'world'
  | 'technology'
  | 'business'
  | 'science'
  | 'culture'
  | 'health'
  | 'environment'
  | 'opinion';

export interface Article {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  description: string;
  url: string;
  source: {
    name: string;
    domain?: string;
  };
  category: NewsTopic;
  publishedAt: string;
  imageUrl?: string;
  author?: string;
  readTimeMinutes: number;
  relevanceScore?: number;
  rankingReason?: string;
  matchedKeywords?: string[];
}

export interface UserPreferences {
  readerName: string;
  newspaperTitle: string;
  selectedTopics: NewsTopic[];
  customInterests: string[];
  readingPace: 'quick' | 'standard' | 'deep'; // quick: 5min, standard: 15min, deep: 30min
  onboarded: boolean;
  theme?: 'vintage' | 'modern' | 'sepia' | 'dark';
}

export interface UserFeedback {
  likedArticleIds: string[];
  dislikedArticleIds: string[];
  skippedArticleIds: string[];
  savedArticleIds: string[];
  topicAffinities: Record<string, number>; // -5 to +5
  keywordAffinities: Record<string, number>; // -5 to +5
}

export interface SavedEdition {
  id: string;
  dateStr: string;
  savedAt: string;
  title: string;
  leadHeadline: string;
  articlesCount: number;
  articles: Article[];
  preferencesSnapshot: UserPreferences;
}

export interface TopicMeta {
  id: NewsTopic;
  label: string;
  description: string;
  iconName: string;
}

export const AVAILABLE_TOPICS: TopicMeta[] = [
  { id: 'technology', label: 'Technology & AI', description: 'Computing, artificial intelligence, startups & software', iconName: 'Cpu' },

  { id: 'opinion', label: 'Commentary & Ideas', description: 'In-depth perspectives, essays & editorial analysis', iconName: 'PenTool' },
];

};

# NaSCon'26 Challenge: Personalized Digital Morning Newspaper
# Progress & Handover Document

## 1. Zero-Budget Compliance Audit (PKR 0 Total Budget)
- **Total Financial Expenditure**: PKR 0 / $0.00 USD.
- **Credit Card / Payment Details**: None required or used at any point.
- **News Data Ingestion**: 100% Free Public RSS Feeds (BBC News, The New York Times, Ars Technica, NPR News). No paid news APIs or subscriptions.
- **AI / Personalization Engine**: 100% Free Transparent Rule-Based Ranking Engine (`lib/ranking.ts`). No paid OpenAI/Gemini/Anthropic tokens or API keys required.
- **Audio Dispatch Engine**: 100% Free Native Browser Web Speech API (`SpeechSynthesis`). Zero external audio API costs or quotas.
- **Database / Backend Storage**: 100% Free Browser `localStorage` (`lib/storage.ts`). No paid PostgreSQL, Supabase, Redis, or cloud storage instances.
- **PDF Generation**: 100% Free Browser Native Print Engine (`@media print` in `globals.css`). No paid PDF generation services.
- **Hosting & Deployment**: 100% Free Vercel Hobby Tier (or Render/Netlify Free Tiers). No credit card required, free `*.vercel.app` subdomain.

## 2. Platform Free-Tier Limits & Mitigations
| Service | Free Tier Limits | Mitigation / Status |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Public RSS Feeds** | Rate limiting if queried excessively per second | Server-side 5-minute caching (`next: { revalidate: 300 }`) and 6s timeout abort controller to prevent rate-limit bans |
| **Web Speech API** | Browser-dependent voice synthesizers | Native client-side speech synthesis; runs fully local with 0 network or subscription overhead |
| **Vercel Hobby** | 100GB bandwidth/mo, 10s serverless function timeout, 100 deployment/day | Lightweight payload (<104KB), parallel RSS fetch finishes in ~600ms, well within all free limits |
| **Browser Storage** | ~5MB localStorage quota per domain | Compact JSON storage; saved editions capped at 20 snapshots; clippings store IDs and metadata |
| **Browser Print** | Dependent on client browser PDF driver | Standardized CSS print broadsheet rules, explicit dialog instructions for "Save as PDF" |

## 3. Implemented Features & Verification State
- **Status**: Complete MVP, Verified in Real Browser, Production Build Running.
1. **Editorial Broadsheet Front Page** [VERIFIED]:
   - Classic newspaper typography (Playfair Display, Cinzel, Newsreader), authentic double rules, and editorial drop caps.
   - Distinct sections: Lead Headline Hero story, 2-column front-page reports, The Morning Wire fast-dispatch sidebar, and 5 categorical section blocks.
   - Real-world source attribution (BBC, NYT, Ars Technica, NPR), publication dates, reading time estimates, and direct external links.
2. **Personalized Onboarding & Preferences** [VERIFIED]:
   - Customizable reader name & newspaper title (e.g., *THE VANCE DISPATCH*).
   - 8 core editorial topics.
   - Dynamic custom interests tags manager with 1-click suggestions (AI, Quantum Computing, SpaceX, Clean Energy, etc.).
   - Reading pace selector (Quick Scan 5 min, Standard 15 min, Deep Dive 30 min).
3. **Live Public Feed Aggregation (Zero Hallucination)** [VERIFIED]:
   - Server-side multi-feed parallel fetcher in `lib/rss.ts`.
   - Verified live retrieval of 215 real articles across BBC, NYT, Ars Technica, and NPR.
4. **Explainable Ranking Engine** [VERIFIED]:
   - Deterministic 0–99% personal relevance score calculated from:
     - Topic match (+20 pts)
     - Custom keyword phrase/token matches (+16 to +35 pts)
     - Morning freshness / recency decay (+8 to +20 pts)
     - Reading pace fit (+10 pts)
     - User feedback affinity (+/- pts)
   - "Why this?" modal breaking down the exact mathematical contribution for each story.
5. **Interactive Reader Feedback** [VERIFIED]:
   - Thumbs up (boosts topic & keyword weights).
   - Thumbs down (demotes topic & keyword weights).
   - Skip / Dismiss (✕) (removes article from today's paper).
   - Saved Clippings / Bookmarks (🔖) with quick filter toggle.
   - All state persisted locally in browser `localStorage`.
6. **Native Web Speech Audio Briefing** [VERIFIED]:
   - 1-click "Audio Brief" button in the masthead synthesizing a spoken morning dispatch of top ranked headlines using the browser's native Web Speech API at PKR 0 cost.
7. **Morning Editions Archive** [VERIFIED]:
   - "Save Today" button snapshots today's edition.
   - Slide-over archive drawer to browse and read historical morning editions.
8. **Clean Broadsheet PDF / Print** [VERIFIED]:
   - Dedicated print modal instructing users to select "Save as PDF".
   - `@media print` CSS engine formatting stories into broadsheet columns and hiding web UI buttons.

## 4. Commands Run and Verification Results
- `npm install`: Exited with code 0 (108 packages).
- `npm run build`: Exited with code 0 (Next.js 14 production build compiled successfully).
- `npm run start`: Running on `http://localhost:3000`.
- API verification (`/api/news`): Verified live retrieval of 215 real articles.
- Browser Subagent End-to-End Test:
  - Onboarding completed for "Eleanor Vance" / "THE VANCE DISPATCH".
  - Custom topic "Artificial Intelligence" added.
  - Front-page lead story displayed with 94% relevance match.
  - Explainability modal verified with scoring breakdown.
  - Thumbs up and skip/dismiss (✕) verified.
  - "Save Today" verified (archive incremented to 1).
  - Archive drawer opened, confirming saved edition with 134 articles.
  - Print / Save as PDF modal verified.

## 5. Git Status & Commit History
- Branch: `main`
- Remote: `origin` (`https://github.com/aliabdullah-se/Ai-Hackathon-Project.git`)
- Milestones committed:
  - `34c7541`: Basic Structural Feature implementation (Core Next.js components, RSS aggregator, ranking engine, print engine)
  - `626dab8`: docs: add architecture diagrams, comprehensive README, and environment setup instructions
  - `a4e0ace`: deploy: add zero-budget Vercel configuration and handover progress record
  - `[Current]`: feat: add native Web Speech audio briefing dispatch and update progress record

## 6. Deployment Guide (PKR 0 Cost)
### Deploying to Vercel (Recommended):
1. Push your latest code to GitHub:
   ```bash
   git push origin main
   ```
2. Go to [https://vercel.com/new](https://vercel.com/new) and log in with your GitHub account (No credit card required).
3. Import the `Ai-Hackathon-Project` repository.
4. If prompted for **Root Directory**, select `Ai-Hackathon-project` (or let `vercel.json` handle it).
5. Click **Deploy**. Vercel will build and assign a free production URL (e.g. `https://morning-chronicle.vercel.app`).

## 7. Next Highest-Priority Task
- Push commits to GitHub `origin main` and deploy to Vercel's free tier for final submission before the 8:00 PM deadline.

FILE: /README.md
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The Morning Chronicle — Personalized Digital Broadsheet

> **NaSCon '26 Official AI Challenge Submission: Personalized Digital Morning Newspaper**  
> An editorial, AI-ranked morning broadsheet delivering real, verified news tailored to your unique intellectual interests.

---

## 📰 Overview & Key Features

The Morning Chronicle reinvents the classic broadsheet experience for the digital era:

1. **Distinctive Editorial Aesthetic**:
   - Classic newspaper masthead typography (Playfair Display, Cinzel, Newsreader).
   - Authentic broadsheet column layouts, editorial drop caps, woodblock double-rules, and weather/issue metadata.
   - Distinct sections: Front Page Lead Story, Front Page Reports, The Morning Wire sidebar, and categorical sections (Technology, World Affairs, Markets, Science, and Culture).

2. **Real-Time Live Wire (Zero Hallucination)**:
   - Server-side multi-feed aggregation from premier global editorial outlets:
     - **BBC News** (World, Technology, Business, Science, Health, Entertainment)
     - **The New York Times** (World, Technology, Science, Business, Climate, Opinion)
     - **Ars Technica** & **NPR News**
   - Resilient XML parsing with `fast-xml-parser`, image thumbnail normalization, and graceful timeouts.
   - **Zero API key required** out of the box.

3. **Transparent & Explainable Ranking Engine**:
   - Calculates deterministic personal relevance scores (0–99%) for every story.
   - **Scoring breakdown**:
     - Topic alignment (+20 pts)
     - Custom keyword phrase & token matches (+16 to +35 pts)
     - Morning freshness / recency decay (+8 to +20 pts)
     - Reading pace alignment (+10 pts)
     - Interactive feedback weighting (+/- pts)
   - Every article has an inspectable **"Why this?"** modal explaining the exact scoring components.

4. **Interactive Reader Feedback & Local Learning**:
   - **Thumbs Up / Down**: Boosts or demotes categorical and keyword affinities in real-time.
   - **Skip / Dismiss (✕)**: Instantly dismisses irrelevant stories from today's paper.
   - **Clippings / Bookmarks (🔖)**: Saves articles to personal morning clippings.
   - **100% Client-Side Local Persistence**: Preferences, votes, dismissed stories, and saved editions persist in `localStorage`.

5. **Morning Editions Archive**:
   - Save today's morning paper as a standalone historical edition snapshot.
   - Browse and read past editions anytime from the slide-over Archive drawer.

6. **Archival Print & "Save as PDF" Broadsheet**:
   - Custom `@media print` CSS engine designed for physical printing and digital PDF archival.
   - Dedicated Print dialog instructing users how to choose **"Save as PDF"** and enable **"Background graphics"** for a clean multi-column physical layout.

---

## 🚀 Quick Start Guide

### Prerequisites
- **Node.js**: v18.0.0 or later (Tested on Node v24.15)
- **npm** or **yarn** / **pnpm**

### Installation

```bash
# Clone repository
git clone https://github.com/aliabdullah-se/Ai-Hackathon-Project.git
cd Ai-Hackathon-Project

# Install dependencies
npm install
```

### Running Locally

```bash
# Development server:
npm run dev

# Or Production build:
npm run build
npm run start
```

Open [http://localhost:3000](http://localhost:3000) in your web browser.

---

## 🛠️ Tech Stack

- **Framework**: Next.js 14 (App Router)
- **Language**: TypeScript 5
- **Styling**: Tailwind CSS with custom editorial broadsheet theme
- **Icons**: Lucide React
- **RSS Parsing**: Fast-XML-Parser (resilient, zero API key requirement)
- **State & Storage**: React 18 hooks with SSR-safe `localStorage` persistence

---

## 📖 How to Demo

1. **Onboarding / Preferences**:
   - On first visit, the onboarding modal appears automatically.
   - Enter your name and custom newspaper title (e.g. *THE VANCE DISPATCH*).
   - Select topics (e.g., Technology & AI, Science & Discovery).
   - Type custom interests (e.g. *Artificial Intelligence*, *Quantum Computing*, *Space Exploration*).
   - Click **Generate My Morning Edition**.

2. **Inspect Explainability**:
   - Click the **Relevance Score Badge** (e.g., *94% Match*) on any story to view the transparent mathematical scoring breakdown.

3. **Interact & Feedback**:
   - Click **Thumbs Up** on an interesting article.
   - Click the **✕ (Dismiss)** button on an unwanted article to see the layout adjust dynamically.
   - Click **Saved Clippings** in the top bar to view bookmarked articles.

4. **Archive Today's Edition**:
   - Click **Save Today** in the masthead.
   - Open **Archive** to see your saved edition snapshot.

5. **Print or Save as PDF**:
   - Click **Print / Save PDF** in the masthead.
   - Follow the instructions to select Destination: *Save as PDF*.

# Daymark — Your world, clearly delivered.
# NaSCon'26 Challenge: Personalized Digital Morning Newspaper
# Progress & Handover Document

## 1. Zero-Budget Compliance Audit (PKR 0 Total Budget)
- **Total Financial Expenditure**: PKR 0 / $0.00 USD.
- **Credit Card / Payment Details**: None required or used at any point.
- **News Data Ingestion**: 100% Free Public RSS Feeds (BBC News, The New York Times, Ars Technica, NPR News). No paid news APIs or subscriptions.
- **AI / Personalization Engine**: 100% Free Transparent Rule-Based Ranking Engine (`lib/ranking.ts`). No paid OpenAI/Gemini/Anthropic tokens or API keys required.
- **Audio Dispatch Engine**: 100% Free Native Browser Web Speech API (`SpeechSynthesis`). Zero external audio API costs or quotas.
- **Database / Backend Storage**: 100% Free Browser `localStorage` (`lib/storage.ts`). No paid PostgreSQL, Supabase, Redis, or cloud storage instances.
- **PDF Generation**: 100% Free Browser Native Print Engine (`@media print` in `globals.css`). No paid PDF generation services.
- **Hosting & Deployment**: 100% Free Vercel Hobby Tier (or Render/Netlify Free Tiers). No credit card required, free `*.vercel.app` subdomain.

## 2. Platform Free-Tier Limits & Mitigations
| Service | Free Tier Limits | Mitigation / Status |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Public RSS Feeds** | Rate limiting if queried excessively per second | Server-side 5-minute caching (`next: { revalidate: 300 }`) and 6s timeout abort controller to prevent rate-limit bans |
| **Web Speech API** | Browser-dependent voice synthesizers | Native client-side speech synthesis; runs fully local with 0 network or subscription overhead |
| **Vercel Hobby** | 100GB bandwidth/mo, 10s serverless function timeout, 100 deployment/day | Lightweight payload (<104KB), parallel RSS fetch finishes in ~600ms, well within all free limits |
| **Browser Storage** | ~5MB localStorage quota per domain | Compact JSON storage; saved editions capped at 20 snapshots; clippings store IDs and metadata |
| **Browser Print** | Dependent on client browser PDF driver | Standardized CSS print broadsheet rules, explicit dialog instructions for "Save as PDF" |

## 3. Implemented Features & Verification State
- **Product Name**: Daymark
- **Tagline**: "Your world, clearly delivered."
- **Status**: Complete MVP, Verified in Real Browser, Production Build Running.

1. **Award-Worthy Editorial Aesthetic** [VERIFIED]:
   - Warm ivory canvas background (`#F7F5EF`), charcoal typography (`#20211F`), and restrained forest-green accents (`#496653`).
   - High-contrast serif headlines (Playfair Display & Newsreader) paired with crisp sans-serif body text (Inter).
   - Tide-inspired Cover Story layout: large photography on the left, oversized serif italic headline on the right, reporting byline, editorial drop cap, and "Read the full feature" link.
   - Refined section dividers, aligned columns, and generous whitespace.
2. **Personalized Onboarding & Reader Identity** [VERIFIED]:
   - Customizable reader name & newspaper title (*DAYMARK*).
   - 8 core editorial topics with clean icons and indicators.
   - Dynamic custom interests tags manager with 1-click suggestions (AI, Quantum Computing, SpaceX, Clean Energy, etc.).
   - Reading pace selector (Quick Scan 5 min, Standard 15 min, Deep Dive 30 min).
3. **Live Public Feed Aggregation (Zero Hallucination)** [VERIFIED]:
   - Server-side multi-feed parallel fetcher in `lib/rss.ts`.
   - Verified live retrieval of 215 real articles across BBC, NYT, Ars Technica, and NPR.
4. **Explainable Ranking Engine** [VERIFIED]:
   - Deterministic 0–99% personal relevance score calculated from:
     - Topic match (+20 pts)
     - Custom keyword phrase/token matches (+16 to +35 pts)
     - Morning freshness / recency decay (+8 to +20 pts)
     - Reading pace fit (+10 pts)
     - User feedback affinity (+/- pts)
   - "Why this?" modal breaking down the exact mathematical contribution for each story.
5. **Interactive Reader Feedback** [VERIFIED]:
   - Thumbs up (boosts topic & keyword weights).
   - Thumbs down (demotes topic & keyword weights).
   - Skip / Dismiss (✕) (removes article from today's paper).
   - Saved Clippings / Bookmarks (🔖) with quick filter toggle.
   - All state persisted locally in browser `localStorage`.
6. **Native Web Speech Audio Briefing** [VERIFIED]:
   - 1-click "Audio Brief" button in the masthead synthesizing a spoken morning dispatch of top ranked headlines using the browser's native Web Speech API at PKR 0 cost.
7. **Reflective Editorial Card (Reflecta Inspiration)** [VERIFIED]:
   - Elegant Daily Reflection box: *"Knowledge becomes wisdom when connected with curiosity."*
8. **Morning Editions Archive** [VERIFIED]:
   - "Save Today" button snapshots today's edition.
   - Slide-over archive drawer to browse and read historical morning editions.
9. **Clean Broadsheet PDF / Print** [VERIFIED]:
   - Dedicated print modal instructing users to select "Save as PDF".
   - `@media print` CSS engine formatting stories into broadsheet columns and hiding web UI buttons.

FILE: /Ai-Hackathon-project/README.md
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Daymark — Your world, clearly delivered.

> **NaSCon '26 Official AI Challenge Submission: Personalized Digital Morning Newspaper**  
> An award-worthy, editorial morning broadsheet delivering real, verified reporting tailored to your intellectual focus areas.

---

## 📰 Editorial Design & Vision

**Daymark** reimagines the morning newspaper as a quiet, thoughtful, and beautifully typeset daily broadsheet inspired by modern editorial publications like *Tide* and *The Atlantic*:

1. **Award-Worthy Editorial Aesthetic**:
   - **Curated Palette**: Warm ivory canvas (`#F7F5EF`), charcoal typography (`#20211F`), and restrained forest-green accents (`#496653`).
   - **Editorial Typography**: Elegant serif display typography (Playfair Display & Newsreader) paired with crisp sans-serif body text (Inter).
   - **Asymmetric Broadsheet Layout**: High-impact Cover Story with generous photography and oversized serif headlines, balanced by two-column front-page reports, *The Morning Wire* fast dispatches, and daily editorial reflections.
   - **Thin Editorial Dividers & Generous Whitespace**: Aligned columns, subtle borders, no boxy shadows or gratuitous animations.

2. **Real-Time Live Wire (Zero Hallucination Policy)**:
   - Aggregates verified reports in parallel from world-class publications:
     - **BBC News** (World, Technology, Business, Science, Health, Culture)
     - **The New York Times** (World, Technology, Science, Business, Climate, Opinion)
     - **Ars Technica** & **NPR News**
   - Resilient XML parsing via `fast-xml-parser` with thumbnail extraction and 6-second timeout fallbacks.
   - **Zero API keys required** — 100% free and reliable.

3. **Transparent & Explainable Ranking Engine**:
   - Calculates deterministic personal relevance scores (0–99%) for every story:
     - Topic alignment (+20 pts)
     - Custom keyword phrase & token matches (+16 to +35 pts)
     - Freshness & recency decay (+8 to +20 pts)
     - Reading pace fit (+10 pts)
     - User feedback affinity adjustments (+/- pts)
   - Every story features a transparent **"Why this?"** modal explaining the exact scoring components.

4. **Interactive Reader Feedback & Local Learning**:
   - **Thumbs Up / Down**: Learns topic and keyword affinities in real-time.
   - **Skip / Dismiss (✕)**: Instantly dismisses irrelevant stories from today's edition.
   - **Clippings / Bookmarks (🔖)**: Saves articles to your personal morning clippings with a dedicated filter toggle.
   - **100% Client-Side Local Persistence**: Preferences, votes, dismissed stories, and saved editions persist in browser `localStorage`.

5. **Audio Dispatch (Web Speech API)**:
   - 1-click **Audio Brief** toggle synthesizing a spoken morning dispatch of top headlines using the browser's native Web Speech API at **PKR 0 cost**.

6. **Morning Editions Archive**:
   - Save today's morning paper as a standalone historical edition snapshot.
   - Slide-over archive drawer to browse and read past editions anytime.

7. **Archival Print & "Save as PDF" Broadsheet**:
   - Custom `@media print` CSS engine designed for physical printing and digital PDF archival.
   - Dedicated Print dialog instructing users how to choose **"Save as PDF"** and enable **"Background graphics"**.

---

## 🚀 Quick Start Guide

### Prerequisites
- **Node.js**: v18.0.0 or later
- **npm** or **yarn** / **pnpm**

### Installation

```bash
# Clone repository
git clone https://github.com/aliabdullah-se/Ai-Hackathon-Project.git
cd Ai-Hackathon-Project/Ai-Hackathon-project

# Install dependencies
npm install
```

### Running Locally

```bash
# Development server:
npm run dev

# Or Production build:
npm run build
npm run start
```

Open [http://localhost:3000](http://localhost:3000) in your web browser.

---

## 🛠️ Tech Stack & Zero-Budget Audit (PKR 0)

- **Framework**: Next.js 14 (App Router)
- **Language**: TypeScript 5
- **Styling**: Tailwind CSS with custom Daymark editorial theme
- **Icons**: Lucide React
- **RSS Parsing**: Fast-XML-Parser (resilient, zero API key requirement)
- **Audio Dispatch**: Native Browser Web Speech API (`SpeechSynthesis`)
- **State & Storage**: React 18 hooks with SSR-safe `localStorage` persistence
- **Total Budget**: PKR 0 (Zero paid APIs, zero credit card requirements)

---

## 📖 How to Demo Daymark

1. **Onboarding / Preferences**:
   - Enter your name and custom newspaper title (*e.g., "THE VANCE DISPATCH"*).
   - Select topics and add custom interest lenses (*e.g., Artificial Intelligence, Clean Energy*).
   - Click **Generate My Morning Edition**.

2. **Inspect Cover Story & Explainability**:
   - View the TIDE-inspired two-column Cover Story.
   - Click the **Match Badge** (*e.g., 94% Match*) to inspect the transparent scoring logic.

3. **Audio Dispatch**:
   - Click **Audio Brief** in the top bar to listen to your morning audio briefing.

4. **Archive & Print PDF**:
   - Click **Save Issue** in the masthead to snapshot today's edition.
   - Click **Print / PDF** to export an archival broadsheet PDF.

# Daymark — Your world, clearly delivered.
# NaSCon'26 Challenge: Personalized Digital Morning Newspaper
# Progress & Handover Document

## 1. Zero-Budget Compliance Audit (PKR 0 Total Budget)
- **Total Financial Expenditure**: PKR 0 / $0.00 USD.
- **Credit Card / Payment Details**: None required or used at any point.
- **News Data Ingestion**: 100% Free Public RSS Feeds (BBC News, The New York Times, Ars Technica, NPR News). No paid news APIs or subscriptions.
- **AI / Personalization Engine**: 100% Free Transparent Rule-Based Ranking Engine (`lib/ranking.ts`). No paid OpenAI/Gemini/Anthropic tokens or API keys required.
- **Audio Dispatch Engine**: 100% Free Native Browser Web Speech API (`SpeechSynthesis`). Zero external audio API costs or quotas.
- **Database / Backend Storage**: 100% Free Browser `localStorage` (`lib/storage.ts`). No paid PostgreSQL, Supabase, Redis, or cloud storage instances.
- **PDF Generation**: 100% Free Browser Native Print Engine (`@media print` in `globals.css`). No paid PDF generation services.
- **Hosting & Deployment**: 100% Free Vercel Hobby Tier (or Render/Netlify Free Tiers). No credit card required, free `*.vercel.app` subdomain.

## 2. Platform Free-Tier Limits & Mitigations
| Service | Free Tier Limits | Mitigation / Status |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Public RSS Feeds** | Rate limiting if queried excessively per second | Server-side 5-minute caching (`next: { revalidate: 300 }`) and 6s timeout abort controller to prevent rate-limit bans |
| **Web Speech API** | Browser-dependent voice synthesizers | Native client-side speech synthesis; runs fully local with 0 network or subscription overhead |
| **Vercel Hobby** | 100GB bandwidth/mo, 10s serverless function timeout, 100 deployment/day | Lightweight payload (<104KB), parallel RSS fetch finishes in ~600ms, well within all free limits |
| **Browser Storage** | ~5MB localStorage quota per domain | Compact JSON storage; saved editions capped at 20 snapshots; clippings store IDs and metadata |
| **Browser Print** | Dependent on client browser PDF driver | Standardized CSS print broadsheet rules, explicit dialog instructions for "Save as PDF" |

## 3. Implemented Features & Verification State
- **Product Name**: Daymark
- **Tagline**: "Your world, clearly delivered."
- **Status**: Complete MVP, Verified in Real Browser, Production Build Running.

1. **Award-Worthy Editorial Aesthetic** [VERIFIED]:
   - Warm ivory canvas background (`#F7F5EF`), charcoal typography (`#20211F`), and restrained forest-green accents (`#496653`).
   - High-contrast serif headlines (Playfair Display & Newsreader) paired with crisp sans-serif body text (Inter).
   - Tide-inspired Cover Story layout: large photography on the left, oversized serif italic headline on the right, reporting byline, editorial drop cap, and "Read the full feature" link.
   - Refined section dividers, aligned columns, and generous whitespace.
2. **Personalized Onboarding & Reader Identity** [VERIFIED]:
   - Customizable reader name & newspaper title (*DAYMARK*).
   - 8 core editorial topics with clean icons and indicators.

   - Reading pace selector (Quick Scan 5 min, Standard 15 min, Deep Dive 30 min).
3. **Live Public Feed Aggregation (Zero Hallucination)** [VERIFIED]:
   - Server-side multi-feed parallel fetcher in `lib/rss.ts`.
   - Verified live retrieval of 215 real articles across BBC, NYT, Ars Technica, and NPR.
4. **Explainable Ranking Engine** [VERIFIED]:
   - Deterministic 0–99% personal relevance score calculated from:
     - Topic match (+20 pts)
     - Custom keyword phrase/token matches (+16 to +35 pts)
     - Morning freshness / recency decay (+8 to +20 pts)
     - Reading pace fit (+10 pts)
     - User feedback affinity (+/- pts)
   - "Why this?" modal breaking down the exact mathematical contribution for each story.
5. **Interactive Reader Feedback** [VERIFIED]:
   - Thumbs up (boosts topic & keyword weights).
   - Thumbs down (demotes topic & keyword weights).
   - Skip / Dismiss (✕) (removes article from today's paper).
   - Saved Clippings / Bookmarks (🔖) with quick filter toggle.
   - All state persisted locally in browser `localStorage`.
6. **Native Web Speech Audio Briefing** [VERIFIED]:

# Daymark — Your world, clearly delivered.

> **NaSCon '26 Official AI Challenge: Personalized Digital Morning Newspaper**  
> An award-winning editorial morning broadsheet delivering real, verified reporting tailored to your intellectual focus areas.

---

## ⚡ Quick Start: Running the Project

The application is built with **Next.js 14**, **TypeScript**, and **Tailwind CSS**.

### Option A: From Repository Root (`D:\Git-Hub Repo`)
```bash
# Run the development server
npm run dev

# Or run the production build
npm run build
npm run start
```

### Option B: From Application Subfolder (`Ai-Hackathon-project`)
```bash
cd Ai-Hackathon-project

# Run the development server
npm run dev

# Or run the production build
npm run build
npm run start
```

Once running, open **[http://localhost:3000](http://localhost:3000)** in your browser.

---

## 🌐 Current Server Status
The production server is **currently active and running** locally at:
👉 **[http://localhost:3000](http://localhost:3000)**

---

## 📰 About Daymark
Daymark reimagines the morning newspaper as a quiet, thoughtful, and beautifully typeset daily broadsheet inspired by modern editorial publications like *Tide* and *The Atlantic*:
- **Warm Ivory Palette**: Ivory `#F7F5EF`, Charcoal `#20211F`, and Forest Green `#496653`.
- **Live Parallel RSS Feeds**: Real-time verified articles from BBC News, The New York Times, Ars Technica, and NPR News (100% zero hallucination, zero API key cost).
- **Explainable Ranking Engine**: Transparent 0–99% personal relevance scoring with a breakdown modal ("Why this?").
- **1-Click Audio Dispatch**: Browser-native Web Speech API morning briefing.
- **Morning Editions Archive & Broadsheet Print**: Save past editions to `localStorage` and print or export to PDF with `@media print`.

For full documentation and architecture details, see [Ai-Hackathon-project/README.md](file:///d:/Git-Hub%20Repo/Ai-Hackathon-project/README.md).
